anayorabhidheyā- bhidhānayoḥ parasparaṃ vācyavācakalakṣaṇaḥ sambandhaḥ
vācyo bhagavān kālacakraḥ pañcapaṭala- svabhāvatayā'vasthitaḥ vācakaṃ kālacakramabhidhānaṃ pañcapaṭalātmakam

akarmabhūmijātitvāditi
iha devādīnāmapi manuṣyajanmalābhād buddhatvaphalapradaṃ bhaviṣyatīti niyamo bhagavataḥ
puṇyajñānasambhārābhyāṃ ṣaḍdhātvātmakena mahāpuruṣapuṅgavene 'ti
na cānyathā kukarmaṇi manuṣyajanmanīha buddhatvaphalapradaṃ bhaviṣyatīti niyamastathāgatasya
athāsmin mantrayāne buddhabhagavatoktam, tadyathā- caṇḍālaveṇukārādyāḥ pañcānantaryakāriṇaḥ
janmanīhaiva buddhāḥ syurmantracaryānucāriṇaḥ || iti bhagavato vākyaṃ satyam
iha janmani yat prākkṛtaṃ pañcānantaryādikaṃ raudraṃ karma
tadasmin mantrayāne mahāmudrākṣarasukhasamādhinā dhvaṃsayitvā'parāgantukākuśalāpraveśāya vajradharmodayagṛhe caturmāravighnādipraveśadvāreṣu maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣādibodhipākṣika - dharmakapāṭaṃ datvā tatra vajrasattvaṃ sādhayitvā mahāmudrayā sārddhaṃ pañcānantaryakāriṇo'pīha janmani buddhatvaphalalābhino bhavantīti tathāgataniyamaḥ,
na punarmantrayāne praviṣṭāḥ santaḥ pañcānantaryādikaṃ raudraṃ karma kurvanto'pīha janmani buddhatvaphalalābhino bhavantīti tathāgatasammatam
iha yānatraye'pi bhagavato vākyam - ādau kalyāṇaṃ madhye kalyāṇaṃ paryavasāne kalyāṇam iti bhagavato vacanāt

evamuktakrameṇa niyamarahitānāṃ buddhatvaphalapradaṃ tantrarājam
buddhatvaṃ sarvajñatā sarvā- kārajñatā-mārgajñatā-mārgākārajñatā- daśabala-vaiśāradyādiguṇavibhūtayaḥ, tāṃ (tāḥ) dadātīti buddha- tvaphalapradam
mahāsukhāvāsa iti
mahāsukhāvāso dharmadhāturākāśalakṣaṇo'saṃkīrṇo dharmo- dayo lokopamāmatikrāntaḥ samantabhadro mahāsukhāvāsaḥ
paramādibuddhavajradhātumahāmaṇḍale niranvaye jñānajñeyaikalolībhūte acchedye'bhedye sarvākāradhātulakṣaṇe, ādarśapratisenātulye
tasmin vajradhātumahāmaṇḍale vajrasaṃhāsanasthena
vajra siṃhāsana ( - ) candrasūryāgnimaṇḍala- macchedyamabhedyam
ekāro vā ākāśadhāturvajrasiṃhāsa [nam, tasmin sthito vajrasiṃhā- |
ka. ka. 0sana; paṭale ] tantraniyamoddeśaḥ sanasthaḥ ; kāyavāścittajñānātmako va~kāra:, tena vajrasiṃhāsanasthena
buddhabodhisattva- krodharāja devanāgadevatādevīgaṇaparivṛteneti
buddhā akṣobhyādayaḥ, bodhisattvāḥ samanta- bhadrādayaḥ, krodharājā ullī (ṣṇī) 'ṣādayaḥ, devā īśvarādayaḥ, nāgā anantādayaḥ, devyo vajradhātvīśvaryādyāḥ, dharmadhātvādayaḥ, atinīlādayaḥ, carcikādayaḥ, gauryādayaḥ, śvānā- spā (yā) dayaḥ, eṣāṃ samūho gaṇaḥ tena parivṛto devatādevīgaṇaparivṛtaḥ tena devatādevīgaṇa - parivṛteneti
traidhātukavanditacaraṇāravindeneti
traidhātukaṃ kāmarūpārūpam, tena vanditaṃ caraṇāravindaṃ yasyāsau traidhātukavanditacaraṇāravindaḥ tena traidhātukavanditacaraṇāravinde- neti
tredhātukacakravattineti
traidhātuke dharmacakraṃ yugapad yasya pravartate, asau traidhātuka- cakravartī, tena traidhātukacakravattineti
paramādibuddheneti
paramādibuddhaḥ ekakṣaṇapañcā- kāraviśatyākāramāyājālābhisambodhilakṣaṇo'kṣarasukhaḥ paramaḥ tenādibuddhaḥ paramādibuddhaḥ, tena paramādibuddheneti
niranvayeneti
anvayaḥ prajñopāyātmako grāhyagrāhakalakṣaṇo dharmaḥ, so'nvayo nirasto yenā'sau niranvayaḥ tena niranvayeneti
kālacakrabhagavateti
kālaḥ paramākṣaro mahāsukha (la) rakṣaṇa:, tenotsāditaṃ nirāvaraṇaṃ skandhadhātvādikaṃ cakraṃ yasya śarīram, asau kālacakraḥ
anyacca, pratyekaikaikākṣareṇa- kākāro kāraṇe śānte lakārācca layo'tra vai
cakārāñcalacittasya ṛkārāt kramabandhanāt
||
rāt kramaḥ kāyādīnāṃ bindūnāṃ cyavanam, tasya bandhaḥ sahajasukheneti kālacakraḥ
bhagavateti
mārakleśabhañjanād bhagaḥ, sarvajñeśvaryādiguṇasamūhaḥ, sa bhago'syāstīti bhagavān, tena kālacakrabhagavateti
sucandrādhyeṣiteneti
śobhanaścāsau candraśceti sucandraḥ, sarvatathāgataśrotṛbhūtaḥ, vajrendu vimalaprabhaḥ, sarvasattvabhāṣāntareṇa tathāgatoktadharmāṇāṃ saṃgrāhaka tvāt sarvatathāgatakarṇabhūtaḥ
guhyādhipatiḥ
guhyaṃ śrāvakapratyeka- yānayoruttaraṃ vajrayānam
tasmin saṅgītikārakatvenādhipatirguhyādhipatiḥ, tathā bāhye lokasaṃvṛtyo (tyā) guhyaśabdena yakṣāsteṣāmadhipatiḥ guhyakādhipatiḥ, aḍakavatīnivāsī
bāhye aḍakaśabdena naṣṭaprāṇo mṛtakasamūhaḥ, so'syāmastīti aḍakavatī śmaśānabhūmiḥ, tasyāṃ mahāyakṣāḥ sattvānāṃ viheṭhakā anekavighnakarttāraḥ, teṣāṃ vadhakaḥ
aḍakavatīnivāsī- ti
aṭavyāṃ mṛgādhipatiriva aḍakavatīnivāsī mahāyakṣādhipatiriti
adhyātmani aḍaka iti ṣaṭśatādhikaikaviṃśatisahasraśvāsapraśvāsānāṃ ṣaṭtriṃśacchatonānāṃ nirodhaḥ, so'syāmastīti dharmameghābhūmiḥ, tasyāṃ nivāsī aḍakavatīnivāsī
mārakleśajñeyasamāpa- tyāvaraṇayakṣāṇāṃ vadhako yakṣādhipatiḥ
tathāgatadeśitadhamasiṃhanādena sarvasattvānāṃ pratyekarutairdharmapravarttakaḥ, tena nirmitakāyena vajrapāṇinā
sucandrarājādhyeṣiteneti ullī kha
uṣṇī carcikā vajrenda guhyaśabdena ucyate yakṣyāṇāṃ bho
sucandrādhyeṣitena
paramādibuddhamiti
dvādaśasahasramiti caturaśītisahasrādhikatrilakṣākṣarasamūhaṃ dvātriṃśadakṣarānuṣṭapād dvādaśasāhasrikamiti
niranvayamiti
paṭale ] tantra deśanoddeśaḥ ato nāmasaṅgītikādhyeṣaṇavacanāt sarvatathāgatairmantrayānaṃ deśitamiti
iha yatsamāje dīpaṅkaraśākyamunimadhye na kenacit tathāgatena mantrayānaṃ deśitam' (iti), tat tena kālena tena samayeneti
āryaviṣayadharmadeśanākālena, āryaviṣayaparṣado na deśitam, caturvarṇābhimānābhavya sattvāśayavaśāditi
na punaranyakālenānyaviṣaye lokadhātuparṣadi na deśitamiti
atastathāgatapravacanāt
sarvatathāgatā yānatrayadeśakāḥ, anyathā sarvajñatābhāvo vajrayānādeśakatvāditi
ataḥ sarve tathāgatā yānatrayadeśakāścaturaśīti- sahasra 'dharmaskandhadeśakāḥ sattvāśayavaśāditi, ato vajradharajñānakāyasākṣibhūtayā nāmasaṅgītyāliṅgitamiti
sarvatantra rājeṣu vajrapadasākṣibhūtamiti
iha vajrayāne sattvāśayavaśena sarvatantra- rājeṣu vajrapadaṃ guptam, caturthaṃ tatpunastatheti tathāgatavacanāt
caturthaṃ tṛtīyaṃ na bhavati, caturthamiti vacanāt tatpunastatheti vacanāt
prajñājñānaṃ tadeva caturthamato bhagavato
vacanāt|
vajrapadaṃ guptamiti sarvatantrarājeṣu vajrapadaṃ prakaṭaṃ na bhavati, gurupāramparya- krameṇāvagantavyam;
tantraṃ tantrāntareṇa bodhavyamiti tathāgatavacanāt|
iha mantranaye dvidhā vajrapadam — ekaṃ laukikasaṃvṛtyā dvitīyaṃ paramārthataḥ|
tayorlokasaṃvṛtyā vajrapadaṃ māraṇādau samayasiddhidāyakam, paramārthasatyena vajrapadaṃ caturthaṃ tatpunastathā mahāmudrāsiddhi - phaladāyakamiti|
ata evāsminnādibuddhe vajrapadaṃ prakaṭamuddeśanirdeśapratinirdeśairbhagavatā prakāśitam|
asyaiva sādhanāya mahāmudrābhāvanā dhūmādinimittamārgaḥ prakāśitaḥ|
śūnye ekāgramanaḥ kṛtvā dinamekaṃ parīkṣayediti|
ato bhagavato vacanāt paramādibuddhe vajrapadaṃ mahāmudrābhāvanāmārge dhūmādikaḥ prakaṭaḥ, na gurupāramparyakrameṇāgataḥ, nādhiṣṭhāni (ṣṭhitaṃ ) gurvājñayeti|
asya ca mārgasya pratyayo'sti, arhāna śaṃ dinamekaṃ parīkṣayediti bhagavato vacanāt|
iha na cānyanmantrādisādhanaṃ dhūmādinimittaṃ vihāya dinaikena yoginā parīkṣaṇīyam|
nimittamapi tridhā - ādinimittam, madhyanimittam, antanimittañceti|
ādinimittaṃ dhūmādimārgaḥ, ṣaḍaṅgayogena viśva (mba) niṣpattirakṣaṇa- ( rakṣa rakṣaṇa) 'lābhaḥ|
madhyanimittaṃ paramākṣarakṣaṇairaṣṭādaśaśatairādibhūmilābhaḥ, pañcā- bhijñā'dṛṣṭvā (ṣṭā) 'rthasandarśanaṃ laukikasiddhiprāptiriti|
animittaṃ ( antanimittaṃ ) ' buddhatvaṃ vajradharatvamekaviṃśatisahasraiḥ ṣaṭśatādhikaiḥ paramākṣarakṣaṇairdvādaśadaśabhūmilābhāt mahāmudrā- siddhiriti etad vajrapadādikaṃ [11] nimittapūrvakaṃ prakaṭaṃ tantrarāje paramādibuddhe bhagavatā deśitam|
ataḥ sarvatantrarājeṣu vajrapadasākṣibhūtaṃ tantraṃ tantrāntarānveṣaka - nāmiti|
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- udghāṭitabuddharatnakaraṇḍakamiti buddharatnaṃ paramākṣarasukhaṃ karaṇḍakaṃ vajramaṇi - ' padmamiti buddharatnakaraṇḍakam tamevodghāṭitaṃ yena tantrarājena tadudghāṭitabuddharatnakaraṇḍa- kamiti|
laukikalokottarasatyāśritamiti|
laukikasatyaṃ maṇḍalacakravikalpabhāvanā laukikasiddhisādhanamutpattikrameṇeti|
lokottarasatyaṃ dhūmādinimittena nirvikalpacittena utpannakrameṇa mahāmudrāsiddhisādhanamiti utpannakramaḥ sahajo nirvikalpaḥ sarvākāro mukhabhujavarṇasaṃsthānakalpanārahita iti;
anayoḥ satyayorāśritaṃ laukikalokottarasatyā- śritamiti mārgadvayadarśanāditi|
catasṛbhirabhisambodhibhiścaturvajraḥ viśuddhamiti|
ekakṣaṇābhisambodhiḥ, pañcākārābhisambodhiḥ, viṃśatyākārābhisambodhiḥ, māyājālābhisambodhiḥ, e (ā) bhiḥ pariśuddhaṃ garbhajotpattikrameṇa dhūmādibimbotpannakrameṇa evaṃ catasṛbhirabhisambodhibhiḥ pariśuddhamiti|
catuḥ kāya - ṣaṭkula- dvādaśasatya ṣoḍaśatattva - ṣoḍaśaśūnyatā- ṣoḍaśakaruṇā-laukika- lokottarābhiṣekakarmajñānamahāmudrāsiddhimāgaṃprakāśakamiti|
catuḥ kāyāḥ|
śuddha-dharma-sambhoga - nirmāṇā iti garbhajasya turyasusu (ṣu) ptisvapnajāgradava- sthālakṣaṇāḥ|
te ca buddhānāṃ nirāvaraṇā iti|
ṣaṭ kulānīti|
akṣarasukhaṃ jñānadhātuḥ, vijñānamākāśadhātuḥ, saṃskāro vāyudhātuḥ, vedanā tejodhātuḥ, saṃjñā toyadhātuḥ, rūpaṃ pṛthvīdhāturiti garbhajānāṃ sāvaraṇāni, buddhānām nirāvaraṇānīti|
dvādaśa satyānīti|
avidyā-saṃskāra - vijñāna- nāmarūpa - ṣaḍāyatana sparśa-vedanā-tṛṣṇā- upādāna - bhava-jāti- jarāmaraṇānīti dvādaśasatyāni garbhajānāṃ sāvaraṇāni, buddhānām nirāvaraṇānīti|
dvādaśasaṃkrāntibhedena prāṇavāyupravāhāt sāvaraṇāni garbhajānām, bu[11b]ddhānāṃ nirāvaraṇāni, dvādaśāṅganirodhāditi|
ṣoḍaśa tattvānīti|
nirmāṇakāyo nirmāṇavākū nirmāṇacittaṃ nirmāṇajñānam, sambhogaḥ sambhogavāk sambhogacittaṃ sambhogajñānam, dharmakāyo dharmavāk dharmacittaṃ dharmajñānam, sahajakāyaḥ sahajavāk sahajacittaṃ sahajajñānamiti ānanda-parama-virama- tantra deśanoddeśaḥ sahajabhedena ṣoḍaśa tattvāni'|
garbhajānāṃ sāvaraṇāni ṣoḍaśārddhārddhabindumocanatvāditi, buddhānāṃ nirāvaraṇāni ṣoḍaśaddharddhabindudha ratvāditi|
ṣoḍaśa śūnyateti|
kṛṣṇapakṣaḥ sūryaḥ prajñā|
ṣoḍaśa karuṇeti|
śuklapakṣaścandramā upāyaḥ|
śūnyatāyāstrayo bhedāḥ – śūnyatā mahāśūnyatā paramārthaśūnyatā ceti|
tatra śūnyatā pañcaskandhaśūnyatā, kṛṣṇapratipadādyāḥ pañca tithayaḥ|
mahāśūnyatā pañcadhātuśūnyatā, ṣaṣṭhayādyāḥ pañca tithayaḥ|
paramārthaśūnyatā pañcendriyaśūnyatā, ekādaśyādyāḥ pañca tithayaḥ|
tena pañcadaśa tithayaḥ pañcadaśaśūnyatā, amāparyantam|
amāntaśuklapratipat- praveśādyau ( praveśayo) madhye ṣoḍaśī śūnyatā sarvākārā|
evaṃ karuṇā tridhā - sattvāvalambinī dharmāvalambinī anavalambinī ceti|
tatra sattvāvalambinī śuklapratipadādyāḥ pañca tithayaḥ|
dharmāvalambinī ṣaṣṭhyādyāḥ pañca tithayaḥ|
anāvalambinī ekādaśyādyāḥ pañca tithayaḥ, pūrṇimāparyantam|
pūrṇimāntakṛṣṇapratipat- praveśādyau (praveśayo)rmadhye ṣoḍaśī karuṇā|
anayoraketvaṃ ṣoḍaśaśūnyatāṣoḍaśakaruṇātmakā- bhidheyaṃ tasya vācakamiti|
laukikalokottarābhiṣekā iti|
laukikāstāvat udakaṃ mukuṭaḥ paṭṭe vajraghaṇṭā mahāvrataṃ nāma anujñā kalaśo guhyaṃ prajñā jñānamiti|
garbhajānāṃ lokasaṃvṛtyā daśābhiṣekāḥ -kāyavākcittajñānadhātuskandhāyatanakarmendriyādipariśuddhyeti lokottara ekādaśatama 4- śvaturthaṃ (:) tat punastatheti niyamāt mahāmudrā - paramākṣarajñānalakṣaṇo guruvaktraṃ kāya- vāgādinirāvaraṇatvena śodhaka iti|
karmajñānamahā [ 12 ] mudrāsiddhiriti|
karmamudrā stanakeśavatī|
jñānamudrā svacittaparikalpitā|
mahāmudrā vikalparahitā pratisenāsvarūpiṇīti|
āsāṃ trividhā siddhiḥ – kāmāvacarā karmamudrāsiddhi:, rūpāvacarā rūpabhavabhāvalakṣaṇā jñānamudrāsiddhiḥ ', bhāvābhāvarahitā sarvākāravaropetā mahāmudrāsiddhiriti eṣāṃ catuḥ kāyādīnāṃ laukika- lokottaramārgaprakāśakaṃ (ḥ) para mādibuddha ( : ) mi (i) ti|
evamuktakrameṇa lokadhātvadhyātmābhiṣeka sādhanajñānapañcapaṭalātmakam, pañcakalpā- tmakaṃ vā narādisakalasānāṃ samyaksambuddhatvalābhāya sandeśitam|
samiti samyak- prakāreṇa, (na) vajrapadaguptaprakāreṇeti|
ataḥ paramādibuddhāllaghutantrasaṅagītikaraṇāya mañjuśriyastathāgatavyākaraṇaṃ mamaiva ca ṭīkākaraṇāya, yamāntakādīnāṃ tantradeśanāya ca|
e vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- anāgate'dhvani brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ vaineyārthaṃ sambhalaviṣayādi ṣasma (ṣaṇṇa) vatigrāmakoṭi- nivāsināṃ sarvasattvānāṃ mahāyānamārgalābhāyeti|
ato dvādaśasāhasrikāt paramādi- buddhāt laghutantrasaṅgītikaraṇāya mañjuśrīstathāgatena vyākṛtaḥ, anāgate'dhvani sārddhatri- koṭonāṃ brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ kalāpagrāmanivāsināṃ vaineyārtham|
ahamapi lokeśvaro laghu- tantre ṭīkākaraṇāya vyākṛto'nyapi ( nye'pi ) trayoviṃśati krodharājabodhisattvāḥ ṣaṇṇava- tigrāmakoṭinivāsināṃ sarvasattvānāṃ nirmāṇakāyena laghutantradeśakāḥ vyākṛtā mahāyāna- mārgalā bhāyeti|
mahāyānamārga iti mañjuśriyo vajrakulena kalaśaguhyaprajñājñānābhiṣekaḥ, tasya lābhāya mahāvajrayāna ' māgaṃlābhāyeti|
ataḥ kalaśaguhyaprajñājñānābhiṣekataḥ sarva- varṇānāmekakalko bhavati|
sa kalko'syāstīti kalkī, tasya gotraṃ kalkigotram|
vajrakulābhiṣekataḥ sakalamantriṇāmiti nītārthaḥ|
evaṃ mañjuśrīrya (ya) śa: kalkī vyākṛ- to'sya yaśaso gotre'haṃ lokeśvaro dvitīyaḥ kalkī vyākṛta iti|
evaṃ yathānukra[ [2b] meṇa yamāntakādayo daśakrodharājāḥ kṣitigarbhādayastrayodaśabodhisattvāḥ, teṣāṃ trayodaśabo- dhisattvānāṃ yamāntakādikrodharājānāmantarāntareṇa vyākṛtāḥ ; nirmāṇakāyena ṣaṇṇavati- koṭigrāmanivāsināṃ rājānaḥ sakalasattvānāṃ tathāgatadharmapravartakāḥ mlecchādikudharmavi- dhvaṃsakā dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇāḥ pañcābhijñādyaiśvaryaguṇaparipūrṇā iti|
ataḥ paramādi- buddhāllaghutantrasaṅgītikaraṇāya mañjuśrī vyākaraṇa mama ṭīkākaraṇāya ca yamāntakādīnāṃ dharmadeśanāyeti|
aśīlinubhana iti mūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyām abhidheyābhidhāna sambandhaprayojanaprayojanasaṃvīkṣya
bhagavatastantradeśanoddeśo dvitīyaḥ || 2 ||
prayojamabhisevīpa bhī (3) deśakādhyeṣaka mūlatantralaghutantrasambandhoddeśaḥ , ** idānīṃ punardeśakā dhyeṣakasambandho'trocyate |
kadācit pūrvāparatantraṭīkā'śru- tasya vacanaṃ bhaviṣyati |
iha nanu vajrayāne aḍakavatīnivāsī mahāyakṣādhipatirmahābo- dhisattvo vajrapāṇistathāgatasyādhyeṣakaḥ saṅgītikāraśca kathaṃ sambhalaviṣayakalāpagrāmā- dhipateḥ sūryaprabhasya vijayādevīgarbhasambhūtaḥ sucandro rājādhyeṣakaḥ ; kacit tantrāntare na śrūyate, na vyākṛta iti * ; parasparaṃ deśakādhyeṣakasambandho viruddhaḥ ?
tasmāducyate |
iha hi yat kenacidvaktavyaṃ mantranaye garbhotpannastathāgatasyādhyeṣako na bhavati, tanna;
paṭale ] deśakādhyeṣakamūlatantralaghutantrasambandhoddeśaḥ kasmād ?
bhagavato'pi garbhotpannatvāt |
iha mantranaye yadi garbhotpannastathāgatasyādhye- ṣako na bhavati saṅgītikāraśca tadā śākyakule śuddhodananarendrasya mahāmāyādevīkukṣisa- mbhūtaḥ śākyamuniḥ sarvajño deśako na syāt, garbhotpannatvāditi |
atha kasyacidvacanaṃ prāk tena mārabhaṅgaḥ kṛtaḥ; bodhirutpāditā tatsarvajño dharmadeśako'bhūta, paścāt parinirvṛtasya punargarbhāvakramaṇābhāva iti vacanāt paramavirodhaḥ, svayamasiddhasya parasādhakatvāt |
ihādī vṛddhatvābhāvena ko'pi mārabhaṅgaṃ [13] karoti devāsuramanuṣyāṇāṃ madhye |
ādyabhisambuddho- 'pi na karoti, sarvāvaraṇābhāvāt |
yugapacca na karoti, yasmin kṣaṇe mārastasmin kṣaṇe buddhatvaṃ na syāt, sāvaraṇacittāt |
yasmin kṣaṇe buddhatvaṃ tasmin kṣaṇe māro nāsti, nirāvaraṇacittāditi |
kiñcānyat, iha karmabhūmyāṃ bhagavato vinā nānyasya nārīgarbha- jānasya dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇānyaśītyanuvyañjanāni dvātriṃśadūrṇāparimaṇḍalaṃ ṣaḍabhijño bhavati, tasmāt māro nāma sattvānāṃ saṃsāracittaṃ vāsanāmalaḥ, buddhatvaṃ nāma saṃsāravāsanā- rahitaṃ cittam |

sva vimalaprabhāyāṃ
lokadhātu- evaṃ vedadharmaṃ pramāṇīkṛtya mlecchadharmaparigrahaṃ kariṣyanti
tena kāraṇenānāgate'dhvani ' mlecchadharmāpraveśāya yuṣmadbhyo mayā niyamo dattaḥ, tasmād bhavadbhirmamājñā kartavyā iti
evamuktenānena yaśo rājño vacanena sadaṇḍājñāsahitena śirasi vidyutpatitairiva brahma ṛṣibhiḥ sūryaratha ukta:- he sūryaratha, tvaṃ yaśo narendraṃ vijñāpaya - nāsmākaṃ vajra- kulābhiṣekadharmapravṛttirvedoktajātidharmaṃ vihāyeti
tasmād vayaṃ tavājñayā varamāryaviṣayaṃ gamiṣyāmaḥ, śītāhimavatordakṣiṇaṃ laṅkādīpāntaram iti
teṣāṃ brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ vacanena sūryaratho yaśo narendra vijñāpayati - he mahārājādhirāja, parameśvara, dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣa- lakṣaṇāśītyanuvyañjanāṅgaparipūrṇa, śrīśākyakulatilaka, paramakāruṇika, karuṇāṃ kuruṣva kuśala meṃ pravṛttānām
athāsmābhiravaśyaṃ tavājñā kartavyā
tadā vayaṃ vajraku- lābhiṣekapravṛtti na kariṣyāmaḥ
tavājñayā varaṃ śotānadīdakṣiṇe himavato laṅkādvopasya madhye āryaviṣayaṃ gamiṣyāmaḥ iti
atha sūryarathavacanāt yaśorājā āha - śīghraṃ sambhalaviṣayānnirgacchantu bhavanto yena śītānadyuttare ṣaṇṇavatikoṭigrāmanivāsinaḥ sarve sattvāḥ prāṇātipātādyakuśa- lakarmapathān parityajya kālacakrabhagavato'dhiṣṭhānena samyakjñānamārgalābhino bhaviṣyanti iti
tena yaśorājājñayā sarve amī brahma ṛṣayaḥ kalāpagrāmānnirgatāḥ ; daśame dine vanāntaraṃ praviṣṭāḥ
teṣāṃ vanamadhye praviṣṭānāṃ pañcābhijñābalena yaśorājñā jñātam, yathaiṣāṃ brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ āryaviṣaye gamanena ṣaṇṇavatikoṭigrāmanivāsināṃ sarvasattvānāṃ vaiṣamyacittaṃ bhaviṣyati, iha yena kāraṇena vajrayānoktaḥ samyagjñānamārgo na bhavati, tena kāraṇena amī ṛṣayaḥ śroyaśorājño bhayena svasthānaṃ parityajya svakuṭumbādi gṛhītvā āryaviṣayaṃ gatāḥ
sarva mokṣārthina iti kṣatriyādayo janāścintayanto durbhagā bhaviṣyanti, gambhīroṃ- dāradharmābhājane ' abhavyacittāt
evaṃ ' 'sarvajanānāṃ svacittābhiprāyaṃ jñātvā yaśonarendraḥ sarvaviṣṇubrahmarudrakulamohanaṃ nāma samādhi samāpannaḥ
tena samādhinā devatādhiṣṭhānabalena sarve te ṛṣayastasminneva vane mohitāḥ santastasmin vananivāsibhi sa ( rā ) barādibhiḥ sarva ṛṣayo bandhayitvā punareva mahāmaṇḍala gṛhamānītāḥ, yaśonarendrasya pādamūle prakṣiptāḥ santaḥ prabuddhā yaśonarendraṃ paśyanti
tadeva maṇḍalagṛhaṃ malayodyāne taṃ dṛṣṭvā vismayamāpannā idaṃ vacanamāhuḥ - aho mahāṃścaryamidam, vayaṃ mahāvanādaprabuddhāḥ santaḥ kenānītā mahāmaṇḍala- gṛhe ? ityevametadvacanaṃ śrutvā brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ yaśorājño mantriṇā nirmitakāyena sāgarama- tinoktam - he sūryarathādayo brahma - ṛṣayo mā vismayaṃ kuruta
ayaṃ yaśorājā dvīpāntara paṭale ] sunasā deśakādhyeṣakamūlatantralaghutantrasambandhoddeśaḥ prādeśiko na bhavati
ko'pi mahābodhisattvo yuṣmākamanugrahārthī buddhādhiṣṭhānenābhūt, tasmādasya pādayoḥ śaraṇaṃ gatvā āddhibuddhatantrarāje laukikalokottarasiddhisādhanamārgābhi- ṣekādhyeṣaṇaṃ kuruta iti
atha tena sāgaramativacanena buddhādhiṣṭhānena sūryarathādayo brahma- ṛṣayaḥ prabuddhā idaṃ vacanamāhuḥ - sādhu sādhu sāgaramate, yena te vacanenāsmākaṃ cittaprabo- dho'bhūt, tasmādidānīṃ ratnatrayaśaraṇaṃ gatvā kālacakratantrarāje laukikalokottarasiddhi - sādhanamārgābhiṣekādhyeṣaṇāṃ kuryyāḥ', sakalasattvānāṃ samyaksambuddhatvalābhāye haiva janmani” iti
evamuktvā taiḥ ṛṣibhiḥ brahmakule sūryaratho rājā'mantritaḥ - he sūryaratha, tvaṃ vedādiśāstrakapustaka sarvalaukika lokottaraśāstraprameyagrāhakahṛdayaḥ, tenāsmākamadhyeṣaṇāvaca- nena yaśorājño'dhyeṣaṇāṃ kuru
vayamapi maṇḍalapūrvaṅgamaṃ śaraṇaṃ gatvā sarve'dhyeṣaṇāṃ kariṣyāmaḥ
atha tena brahma ṛṣivacanena sūryaratho ratnasuvarṇamayaiḥ puṣpaimaṃṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā yaśonare- ndrasya pādamūle ratnapuṣpāñjaliṃ prakṣipya jānuyugmena bhūgatena hastayugmena śirasi gatena brahma- ṛṣibhiḥ sārddhaṃ yaśaḥ pādau praṇamya dakṣiṇaṃ jānumaṇḍalaṃ pṛthivyāṃ saṃsthāpya lalāṭe karapuṭaṃ datvā yaśonarendramadhyeṣayati- deśayatu bhagavāṃstantrarājamādibuddham, yasmin buddhatvāya pañcānantaryakāriṇo'pi vyākṛtā ihaiva janmani vajradharabhagavatā paramākṣarasukhena saṃgṛhītā mahāmudrālābhino vyākṛtāḥ
iyaṃ ṣaḍdvādaśasāhasrikāṃ ādibuddhaṃ sucandrarājñastathāgatena idaṃ yada deśitam
tadevālpagranthenādibuddhamalpatantrarājaṃ saṅgīti kṛtvā deśayatu brahma ṛṣīṇāṃ śāsye (ste) iti
atha sūryarathādhyeṣaṇaṃ śrutvā brahma ṛṣīṇāmadhimukticittavaśāt tathāgatādhiṣṭhānabalena laghusaṅgītikāra katvena sragdharāvṛtteḥ sarvajñadeśakādisaṃgrāha- kaistantrarājaṃ deśayati
teṣāṃ ca suśabdavādināṃ suśabdagrahavināśāya arthaśaraṇatāmāśritya kacidavṛtte'pa- śabdaḥ; kacid vṛtte yatibhaṅgaḥ kacidavibhaktikaṃ padam
kacidvarṇasvaralopaḥ kacid vṛtte dīrgho hrasvaḥ, hrasvo'pi dīrghaḥ kacit pañcamyarthe saptamī, caturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī; kutracit parasmai- padini dhātāvātmanepadam, ātmanepadini parasmaipadam; kacidekavacane bahuvacanam, bahuvacane ekavacanam, pulliṅge napuṃsakam, napuṃsake pulliṅgam; kacit tālavyaśakāre dantyamūrddhanyau; kacit mūrddhānye dantyatālavyau; kacid dantye tālavyamūrddhanyau
evamanye'pyanusartavyāstantra- deśakopadeśakeneti
tathā mūlatantre bhagavānāha - sucandra sarvabuddhānāṃ deyaṃ nityeṣṭavastukam

atha yena yena prakāreṇa kulavidyāsuśabdābhimānakṣayo bhavati, tena tena prakāreṇārthaśaraṇatāmāśritya buddhānāṃ bodhi- sattvānāṃ dharmadeśanā deśabhāṣāntareṇa śabdaśāstrabhāṣāntareṇa mokṣārtham
iti mūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ dvādaśasāhasikāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ deśakālādhyeṣakamūlata ntralaghutantrasambandhoddeśaḥ tṛtīyaḥ || 3 ||
mo nāsti (4) deśakādhyeṣakasādhanoddeśaḥ idānīṃ mañjuśrī nirmita śonarendrasūrya rathadeśakādhyeṣakādisambandhādinā laghutantra- rājaṃ vitanomīti
iha mahāśrīmati kālacakamaṇḍalagṛhe pūrvadvā rāvasāne mahāmaṇi- avasanne bho ratnamaṇḍape mahāratnasiṃhāsanastho devāsuranāganirmita kāyaiḥ ṣaṇṇavatimahārājakulaprasūte- mahāratnamukuṭabaddheḥ koṭikoṭigrāmādhipatibhirnamaskṛtacaraṇāravindaḥ sarvatathāgataprajñāmūttiḥ sūryarathādhyeṣito yaśonarendraḥ sūryarathamidamavocat - sādhu sādhu sūryaratha yena tvaṃ brahma- ṛṣikulādīnāṃ sarvasattvānāṃ samyaksambuddhatvamārgalābhāya paramādibuddhatantrarājasadbhāvaṃ śrotuṃ mattaḥ samudyataḥ
tena sādhu te idaṃ yat paramādibuddhatantrarājasadbhāvaṃ laukikalokottara- siddhisādhakaṃ kālacakravajrayogaṃ tvayā pṛṣṭaṃ tatsarvamahaṃ deśayāmi; saṅgītikārakatvena tvamapyekāgramanāḥ śṛṇu prāgvyākṛtatathāgatādhiṣṭhānabalena laghutantrarājasadbhāvaṃ prajñopā- yātmakaṃ yogaṃ śra ( kha ) dharāvṛttasaṅgītyā mahātantrarājāduddhāryamāṇam

pṛcchedrājā sucandraḥ karakamalapuṭa sthāpayitvottamāṅge yogaṃ śrīkālacakre kaliyugasamaye muktihetornarāṇām || 1 ||
māśritya ' ityataḥ gha. pustakaṃ khaṇḍitam

aka eka bayāna paṭale ] deśakādhyeṣakasādhanoddeśaḥ

atra deśakādayaḥ deśakaḥ,sthānam, mahāparṣat, adhyeṣakaḥ, dharmadeśanā, prayojanaprayojanaprayojanamiti '
eṣu deśakasaṃgrahastāvat sarvajñaṃ jñānakāyaṃ dinakaravapuṣaṃ padmapatrāyatākṣaṃ buddhamityebhistrayo- viśatyakṣarairdeśakasaṃgrahaḥ kṛto bhagavatā

evaṃ mayā - ādi - vijahāra - sthāna nirdeśābhāvādidaṃ tantrarājaṃ buddhabhagavatā deśitaṃ na bhavatīha kasyacit saṃjñāvyañjanaśaraṇāśritasya vacanaṃ bhaviṣyati

tanna, kasmāt ?
dharmadeśakasyārthaśaraṇatāmāśritatvāt, saṅgītikārāṇāmapi nānādeśabhāṣāntareṇa saṅgītikaraṇāditi
iha yadi evaṃ mayā śrutam ityādinā saṃskṛtavacanena kaṇṭhatālvādiprayatnato janitena prādeśikena tathāgatasya dharmadeśanā, tadā caturaśītisahasradharma skandhānanekakālairapi tathāgato deśayituṃ na śaknoti ; saṅgītikārakaśca likhituṃ prādeśikaikasaṃskṛtavacanāditi
ihānantanirmāṇakā yābhāvāda- nantānantalokadhātuṣu anantānantasattvarutairyugapacca dharmadeśako na syāt, caturaśītisahasra- dharmaskandhadeśanābhāvāt sarvajño na bhavati ?
na caivam, ihāpramāṇo buddhaḥ, apramāṇo dharmaḥ, apramāṇaḥ saṅghaḥ ; atastathāgatavacanāt naikaḥ sarvajño deśakaḥ

naikaḥ śrāvakasaṅgho'dhyeṣakaḥ tathāgata- syeti

ataḥ sarvatantra deśanā na evaṃ mayā śrutam ityādinā ekadevabhāṣayā kaṇṭhatālvādipratya (ya) nato janitayā tathāgatasyeti

anena prādeśika saṃskṛtavacanena buddho'pi prādeśiko bhavati, sarvasattvarutasvabhāvinyā sarvajñabhāṣayā vinā
ihāryaviṣaye śabdavādināṃ torthikānāṃ paṇḍitānāmabhimānaṃ dṛṣṭvā bālamatīnāṃ bauddhānāmabhiprāyaḥ, yathā brahmahariharādayaḥ saṃskṛtavaktāro brāhmaṇavaiṣṇavaśaivādīnāmiṣṭa- devatā, tathā'smadīyā iṣṭadevatā buddhabodhisattvāḥ saṃskṛtavaktāro bhavantīti
iha na ca te anena prādeśikasaṃskṛtavacanena sarvasattvarutairdharmadaśakāḥ saṅgītikārakā bhavanti, buddhabodhisattvāḥ sarvajñabhāṣayā vinā
ato devajātipratibaddhā prādeśikā buddhabodhi- sattvānāṃ na syāditi, nānāsattvarutadharmadeśakatvāt
iha mantranaye ekasaṃjñā na bhāvaḥ, ekasyāpi bhāvasyānekāḥ saṃjñāḥ saṃjñābahutvāt
na caikā saṃjñā pradhānā sthāt, sarvasaṃjñānātmakā' bhāvapratipādakatvāt
yathā strī - nārī - yuvatītyādīnāṃ naikā strīsaṃjñā pradhānā syāt, sarvāsāṃ stanakerāvatībhāvapratipādakatvāt; tathā ekāra- rahasya-padma-dharmodaya khadhātu- mahāsukhāvāsasiṃghā (hā ) sana- bhaga-guhya-saṃjñānāṃ madhye naikā ekārasaṃjñā pradhānā, sarvāsāṃ sarvākāraśūnyatāpratipādakatvāt
tathā vaṃkāra- mahāsurata (sukha) - mahārāga sahaja-paramākṣara bindu-tattva-jñāna-viśuddhacitta-saṃjñānāṃ madhye na ekā vaṃkārasaṃjñā pradhānā, sarvāsāṃ mahāmudrā - sahajānandākṣarasukhapratipādakatvāditi
evamekāra-vaṃkārayoḥ sarvākāravaropetā śūnyatā, sarvadharmanirālambakaruṇā'bhinnabodhicitta - bhāvapratipādakatvāt
evaṃkāro vajrasattvo bodhicittaṃ kālacakraḥ [ ] ādibuddha: prajñopāyātmako yogaḥ jñeyajñānātmakaḥ advayaḥ anādinidhanaḥ śāntaḥ samājaḥ saṃvara eva- mādyanekasaṃjñābhiḥ prajñopāyātmako'dvayo yogo niranvayo yoginā'vagantavya iti
iha yat samājādau tantrarāje ekāro vaṃkārastantrādau bhagavatā niruktirūpeṇa nirdiṣṭaḥ, tad devānāṃ devarutena pācanāya saṅgītikāreṇāpi tantrādau likhitam; ato'syākṣaradvayasya samājādau paṭale ] deśakādhyeṣakasādhanoddeśaḥ tantrarāje luptirneṣyate
na cedakṣaradvayaṃ bhagavatā tālvādinā deśitam, na cāśrutvā saṅgītikāreṇa likhitamiti
ato'syākṣaradvayasya nītārtha ucyate- pūrvo'kāraḥ khadhāturguhyakamalam, tato visargaḥ sūryo rajaḥ, tato'kāro rāhuvijñāna sukhādhiṣṭhitam, tato'nusvāraḥ candraḥ śukram
anayorakārayoḥ khadhātu-rāhuvijñānayormadhye visarga: sūrya: rajaḥ itvamā 'padyate
tato visarge sūryarajasi utvamāpanne sati paramārthasatye guṇābhāvaḥ, guṇābhāvād yaṇādeśaḥ syāditi
para-akāreṇa rāhuvijñānena sahavatvam, anusvāraḥ candraśukeṇa saṃyogaḥ, ato vaṃkāraḥ
tathā pūrvo'kāraḥ khadhāturguhyakamalaṃ saptamyanto akāraḥ para-ikāreṇa saha guṇī bhavati, ata ekāraḥ
asminnekāre khadhātau ādhāre vaṃkāra ādheya visargākārānusvārasūrya rāhucandraraja ālayavijñānaśukrātmako madhye bāhye dehe pare cāvagantavya iti
iha mūlatantre bhagavānāha - ekāre madhyavaṃkāraḥ sarvabuddhasukhālayaḥ
khadhātau vajrasattvo'yaṃ kāyavākacittayogataḥ ||
kāyo bindvandu śukraṃ ca vāgvisargo rajo raviḥ
cittākārastvamī proktā ekhadhātau vyavasthitaḥ ||
kāyavākcittayogena kāyavākcittamaṇḍale
kāyavākcittarāgeṇa saṃsthitastribhavātmani
ityanena hetunā devānāṃ paripācanāya samyaksambuddhatvalābhāya ca samājādike tantrarāje “evaṃ mayā [ śrutam * ityādinā vijahārasthānanirdeśaḥ tathāgatenokta iti
evam ityanayā saṃjñayā yo bhāvaḥ śrīsamājādāvuktaḥ, sa eva bhāvo lakṣābhi- dhānādau tantrarāje rahasya ityādinā tathāgatenoktaḥ, sa ca gurūpadeśāt tantratantrāntare sandhyābhāṣāntare gāvagantavya iti
atra mūlatantre bhagavānāha - sandhyābhāṣaṃ tathā naiva rutaṃ caiva tathā na ca
neyārthaṃ na ca nītārthaṃ ' tantraṃ ṣaṭkoṭilakṣaṇam
ato bhagavato vacanānnaikā saṃjñā pradhānā syāditi
iha mantranaye* trividhaḥ pratyayo bhagavatoktaḥ - prathamaṃ tāvat tantrapratyayaḥ, tato gurupratyayaḥ, tata ātmapratyayaḥ
ebhistribhiḥ pratyayaiḥ pariśuddhaḥ samyaksambuddhamārgo bhavati ; anyathā tribhiḥ pratyayairvinā yo mārgo guruṇā kathyate, śiṣyasya samyaksambuddhatvaphaladāyako na bhavati, śiṣyasya śraddhājaḍatvāt
laukikaṃ phalaṃ bhavati saṃvṛtisatyeneti
iha tantrāntare bhagavatā pratijñā kṛtā - vikalparahitaṃ cittaṃ kṛtvā dinamekaṃ parīkṣayet nimittam, yadi tantroktavidhinā gurūpadeśena śiṣyasya pratyayo na bhavati, tadā bhagavato mṛṣā vacaḥ
atha gurustantroktavidhinā śiṣyasya mārgadāyako na bhavati, tadā na bhagavataḥ pratijñā mṛṣā bhavati, gurormārgāparijñānāt, viparītāviśuddha mārgaṃbhāvanāpratipādakatvāditi
iha na cānyanmaṇḍalacakrasādhanānimittaṃ dinamekaṃ parīkṣayet, dhūmādinimittaṃ vihāya bhagavato vacanamiti
atra trikulātmake tantrarāje kāyavākcitta kulātmako'bhidheyaḥ trimukhaḥ, catuḥkulā- tmake kāyavākcittajñānātmako'bhidheyaścaturmukhaḥ; jñānaikena' kulena saha catuḥ kulātmakaṃ tantraṃ bhavati, abhidheyaśca
tathā pañcakulātmakaṃ svabhāvaikena sārddhaṃ ṣaṭkulātmakaṃ bhavati
atra catuḥkulātmake tantre catuḥ kulātmako'bhidheyaḥ sūrya-candra- rāhu-agni [ rajaḥ- śukra - citta - jñānaikayoga iti
iha nāmasaṅgītyāṃ prakaṭaḥ saṅgīto vajrayogastathāgateneti pratyavekṣaṇājñānastave trayastriśattamādiślokatrayeṇa ; tadyathā - vajrasūryamahāloko vajra endu vimalaprabhaḥ
virāgādimahārāgo viśvavarṇojjavalaprabhaḥ ||
*sambuddhavajraparyaṅko buddhasaṅgītidharmadhṛk
buddhapadmobhavaḥ śrīmān sarvajñajñānakośadhṛk ||
viśvamāyādharo rājā buddhavidyādharo mahān
vajratīkṣṇo mahākhaḍgo viśuddhaḥ paramākṣaraḥ ||
iti
( nā sa ato bhagavato vacanāt catuḥ kulātmako'bhidheyo vajrasatva viśuddhadharmamantra- saṃsthāpanātmakaḥ kālacako bhagavāniti
evaṃ pañcakulātmakaḥ skandhadhātubhedena, tathā
māyājāle bhagavānāha - “pañcānanaḥ pañcaśikhaḥ pañcacīrakaśekharaḥ | mahāvratadharo mauñjī brahmacārī vratottamaḥ |
( nā sa evaṃ skandhadhātubhedena ṣaṭkulātmako'bhidheyaḥ; śatakulātmako'pi vaktrabhedena vatokta iti viśuddhadharmadhātvantimaślokapādena sārddhaṃ ādarśajñānastave prathamaślokena; tadyathā- vajrabhairavabhīkaraḥ | krodharāṭ ṣaṇamukho bhīmaḥ ṣaṇṇetraḥ ṣaḍbhujo balī | daṃṣṭrākarālakaṅkālo halāhalaśatānanaḥ || iti | ato bhagavato vacanāt tantratantrāntareṣvabhidheyastrimukhaścaturmukhaḥ pañcamukhaḥ ṣaṇmukhaḥ śatamukho'vagantavya iti | iha samājādike ekāreṇa yat sthānaṃ bhagavatoktaṃ tadeva sthānaṃ rahasyetyādiśabdena lakṣābhidhānādike bhagavatoktam | yo vaṃkāreṇābhidheya uktaḥ sa eva mahāsukhaśabdena vajra- sattva ukta iti | evamuktakrameṇa catuḥkulātmake paramādibuddhe ṣaṭkulātmake catuḥkulātma- ko'bhidheyaḥ ṣaṭkulātmakaśceti | sarvajñetyādibhiḥ trayoviṃśatyakṣarairbhagavatoddiṣṭaḥ śūnyatā- karuṇābhinno' bodhicittavajjo mahāsukha iti | iha siṃhāsanaśabdenākāśadhātuḥ sarvā - kāraḥ , tasmin siṃhāsane sthitaḥ siṃhāsanasthaḥ buddha iti vajrasattvaḥ | yoga iti mahārthaḥ paramākṣara iti | asau yogo nāmasaṅgītyāṃ vajradhātumaṇḍalastave caturdaśa- ślokaiḥ saṅgīta:- tadyathā bhagavān buddhaḥ sambuddho'kārasambhavaḥ | akāraḥ sarvavarṇāgrayo mahārthaḥ paramākṣaraḥ || mahāprāṇo hyanutpādo vāgudāhāravarjitaḥ | sarvābhilāpahetvagrayaḥ sarvavāksuprabhāsvaraḥ || mahāvidyottamo nātho mahāmantrottamo guruḥ | mahāyānanayā rūḍho mahāyānanayottamaḥ” || iti paryantaṃ sarvatantrāntareṣu asāvabhidheyo saṅgītaḥ, madhyottamasattvāśayavaśeneti | tathā śūnyaṃ jñānaṃ cetyādibhiścaturdaśākṣaraiḥ ṣaṭkulātmako'bhidheyaḥ | sa eva saṅgīto nāma- saṅgītyāmapi kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānastave dvitīyaślokenoktaḥ; tadyathā- sarvamantrārthajanako - mahābinduranakṣaraḥ | pañcākṣaro mahāśūnyo binduśūnyaḥ ṣaḍakṣaraḥ || iti | vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ lokadhātu-
ato'nantasaṃjñābhiḥ dharmadeśakatvād evaṃ mayā śrutam ityādinā deśitaṃ tantrarājaṃ tathāgatadeśitaṃ bhavati, rahasyetyādinā ' sarvajñādinā ca deśitaṃ tathāgatadeśitaṃ na bhavatīti bauddharna vaktavyam, svasiddhānte parasiddhānte doṣagrahaṇāt ṣaṣṭhī mūlāpattirbhavati | tasmāt tantrāntare pūrvāparasambandhaṃ jñātvā guṇadoṣāvagantavyāḥ (vyau ) | anyathā'dṛṣṭadoṣagrahaṇād- vīcigamanaṃ bhavati duṣṭānāryāṇāṃ tathāgatahṛdayabāhyabhūtānāṃ viṣayendriyopabhogāsaktānāṃ mithyāpralāpināmiti | evamuktakrameṇa paramāgamayuktyā pūrvāparāviruddhaṃ sarvajñetyādinā paramādibuddhaṃ tantrarājaṃ bhagavatā sandeśitamiti | nanu yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sa eva buddho bhagavān, dvābhyāṃ saṃjñābhyāṃ ekabhāvapratipādakatvā- diti bhāvaḥ ; idaṃ kimarthaṃ punaruktavacanaṃ bhagavato buddhamitīha kasycidabhiprāyo bhavi - ṣyati | tasmāducyate - iha satya [ metad vacanaṃ yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sa buddho bhagavān iti | ki tarhi, anye'pi hariharādayaḥ sarvajñatvena balajanaiḥ parikalpitāḥ | teṣāṃ sarva- jñatānirākaraṇāya sarvajño buddho bhagavān iti vacanam | iha nānyaḥ sarvajñastre dhātuke hariharāṇāṃ madhye yaḥ sarvadharmāṇāṃ sarvasattvarutakairdeśaka iti | iha kasmāt teṣāṃ madhye sarvajñaḥ sarvasattvarutakaiḥ sarvadharmadeśako na bhavatītyucyate | iha ṣaḍgatisaṃsāre devajātī hariharādīnāṃ sambhūtatvāt; buddhabhagavataḥ saṃsārapārakoṭivyavasthitvāditi | iha nāma- saṅgītyāṃ tathāgatenoktaṃ suviśuddhadharmadhātustave trayodaśamena ślokena ; tadyathā- saṃsārapārakoṭisthaḥ kṛtakṛtyaḥ sthale sthitaḥ | kaivalyajñānaniṣṭhataḥ prajñāśastravidāraṇaḥ || iti | ataḥ sarvajño buddho bhagavān buddhamiti punarvacanaṃ na bhavatīti | nanu yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sa eva jñānakāya:, kimarthaṃ jñānakāya iti punarvacanaṃ bhagavataḥ ? tadevocyate | iha yaḥ sarvajñaḥ sa eva jñānakāya iti tat satyam | kiṃ tarhi, anye'pi buddhāḥ śrāvakapratyekāḥ santi teṣāṃ samyaksambuddhatvanirākaraṇāya sarvajño jñānakāyaḥ samyaksambuddho bhagavān jñānakāya iti punaruktavacanaṃ bhagavataḥ | iha śrāvaka pratyekabu- dudhānāṃ madhye na kaścid jñānakāyaḥ samyaksambuddho'bhūditi | iha kasmānna śrāvakapratyeka- buddhānāṃ madhye kaścit samyaksambuddho'bhūdityucyate | iha śrāvakabuddhānāṃ sopadhini- rvāṇe sthitatvāditi, samyaksambuddhasya sarvopadhivinirmuktatvāt | tathā ca nāmasaṅgītyāṃ bhagavatoktaṃ pratyavekṣaṇājñānastave ekādaśamaślokena; tadyathā- - sarvopādhivinirmukto vyomavartmani susthitaḥ | mahācintāmaṇidharaḥ sarvaratnottamo vibhuḥ || iti | paṭale ] deśakādhyeṣakasādhanoddeśaḥ ataḥ sarvajño jñānakāyaḥ samyaksambuddho bhagavān jñānakāya iti punaruktavacanaṃ bhagavata iti | - nanu yo jñānakāya: [ sa eva dinakaravapurajñānāndhakāradhvaṃsakatvāt, kimarthaṃ punaruktavacanaṃ dinakaravapuriti bhagavata iti kasyacid vacanaṃ bhaviṣyati ; tasmāducyate- iha satyamidaṃ vacanaṃ yo jñānakāyaḥ sa eva dinakaravapuḥ, ajñānāndhakāradhvaṃsakatvāditi | kiṃ tarhi, anye'pi vijñānavādino bauddhāḥ santi, teṣāṃ vijñānadharmatānirākaraṇāya jñāna- kāyo dinakaravapuḥ vijñānadharmatātītaḥ samyaksambuddha iti punaruktavacanaṃ dinakaravapu- riti | iha vijñānavādināṃ madhye kaścinna jñānakāyo dinakaravapuḥ samyaksambuddho'bhūt iha kasmād vijñānavādināṃ madhye kaścinna ca jñāna kāyo dinakaravapuḥ samyaksambuddho'bhū- dityucyate ? iha vijñānavādināṃ vijñānadharme sthitatvāt' samyaksambuddhasya vijñāna - dharmatātītatvāditi | tathā ca nāmasaṅgītyāṃ tathāgatenoktaṃ pratyavekṣaṇājñānastave trayovi- titamena ślokena tadyathā - vijñānadharmatātīto jñānamadvayarūpadhṛk | nirvikalpo nirābhogastryadhvasambuddhakāyadhṛk || iti | ato jñānakāyo dinakaravapuḥ samyaksambuddha iti punaruktavacanaṃ bhagavataḥ | nanu yaḥ tantre'bhidheyaḥ sa jñāna kāyo arūpī, yaśca padmapatrāyatākṣaḥ sa rūpī; kimarthaṃ padmapatrāyatākṣamiti bhagavato vacanamiha kasyacidabhiprāyo bhaviṣyati, tasmāducyate - iha hi yad vaktavyaṃ bālajanaiḥ padmapatrāyatākṣaḥ tantre'bhidheyo rūpī, tanna, iha padmaśabdena nītā- rthenākāśadhāturucyate, tasmin padme khadhātau padmapatrāṇīva śatakuladīrghasvabhāvatvenāva - sthitāḥ sattvā iti padmapatrāṇi teṣāmantaṃ yāvat; śatākṣīṇi yasya bhagavata āyatāni sa padmapatrāyatākṣa iti | iha nāmasaṅgītyāmuktaṃ bhagavatā ādarśajñānastave ṣaṣṭha ślokena; tadyathā--- vajrajvālākarālākṣo vajrajvālāśiroruhaḥ | vajrāveśo mahāveśaḥ śatākṣo vajralocanaḥ || iti | ato bhagavato vacanāt padmapatrā yatākṣaḥ samyaksambuddho bhagavān rūpa- rahita iti | taṃ buddhaṃ siṃhāsanastham, siṃhāsanaṃ khadhātuḥ; aparaṃ candrasūryāgnimaṇḍalaṃ siṃhāsanam, tasmin sthitaḥ siṃhāsanastha iti | iha mūlatantre bhagavānāha -
e-rahasye khadhātau vā bhage dharmodaye'mbuje |

kasmāt ?
prādeśikaikadevajātau sambhūtatvāditi, prādeśikaikasaṃskṛtavacanāta |
na teṣāṃ sā sarvajñabhāṣā nānādhimuktikānāṃ sattvānāṃ svasvabhāṣāntareṇa laukikalokottara- dharmaṃ pratipādayantī (tī) ti |
iha martyaloke pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyate saṃskṛtabhāṣayā tairdeśitā gītā siddhāntapurāṇadharmāḥ sarve prādeśikāḥ caturvedāśca |
kutaḥ brahmakṣatriyavedādhyayanataḥ |
iha brāhmaṇakṣatriyābhyāṃ vedādhyayanaṃ kartavyam, na hi viṭśūdrādibhiriti |
tathā gotāsiddhāntapurāṇadharmā brāhma- ṇakṣatriyaviṭaśūdraiścaturvarṇaiḥ śrotavyā brāhmaṇamukhataḥ pravajyāgrahaṇañca na kaivartādibhiriti |
ato dharmapratiṣedhavacanāntairdeśitā dharmāḥ prādeśikāḥ sarvasattvopakāriṇo na bhavantīti |
iha martye yena kāraṇena prādeśika saṃskṛtabhāṣayā nānādhimuktikānāṃ sattvānāṃ nānārutairloki- kalo[a]kottaradharmadeśanāṃ kartumaśaktāḥ tena viṭaśūdrādīnāṃ pratiṣedhaṃ kurvanti |
iha martye viṭśūdrādibhinikṛṣṭayonijātairvedādhyayanaṃ na kartavyam, pravrajyādaṇḍagrahaṇañceti |
pāṇinī “ekaḥ śabdaḥ suprayuktaḥ svarge kāmadhuk” (mahā bhā) iti mithyāhaṅkārābhi- mahābhāṣya bhūtānāmabhiprāyaḥ, sarvajñabhāṣā'bhāvāt |
iha martye loke yadi sarve manuṣyā vedagītā- siddhāntatarkaśāstravido bhavanti śūdrādayaḥ, tadā brāhmaṇānāṃ ko gauravaṃ kariṣyati; vidyādharmajñānasādhāraṇaparijñānāt, sarveṣāṃ gṛhavāsināṃ saṃsārabhogāsaktānāṃ viśeṣaguṇābhā- vāt |
iti jñātvā duṣṭa - ṛṣibhirdravyalubdhaiḥ saṃskṛtabhāṣayā gītāsiddhāntapurāṇādayo dharmāḥ pustake likhitāḥ vedāśca mukhapāṭhenādhyayanīyā iti niyamaḥ kṛto bālajanānāṃ mahāmohajanaka iti asarvajñadeśanābhiprāyaḥ |
iha pūrvakāle vedagītāsiddhāntapurāṇa- dharmā na pustake likhitāḥ santi yatīnāṃ mukhe tiṣṭhanti tataḥ pañcakaṣāyakālavaśāt ; mma paṭale ] deśakādhyeṣakasādhanoddeśaḥ pustake likhitāḥ |, prajñāhīnatvād yatibhiriti |
iha prādeśikī hariharādīnāṃ dharma- deśanā bauddharnānumodanīyā sarvasattvakṛpayā rahitā, saṃsāraduḥkhadāyikī (nī) mithyāhaṅkāra- kāriṇī jātivādābhimāninīti |
iha dhātuke ye sarvajñena sarvajñabhāṣayā deśitā dharmā nānāsattvabhāṣāntareṇa saṅgī- tikārakaiḥ pustake likhitāḥ, vedādilaukikārtha' pratipādakāḥ, yānatrayārthapratipādakāḥ, lokasaṃvṛtisatyena paramārthasatyena deśitāḥ, sarvasattvānāṃ śravaṇāyādhyayanāya ca tadadhimu- ktikāḥ sattvāstān sarvajñadeśitān caturaśītisahasradharmaskandhān laukikalokottarārthaprati- pādakān śṛṇvanti paṭhanti vācayanti dhārayanti parebhyaśca vistareṇa saṃprakāśayanti prati- ṣṭhāpayanti, pūjayanti nānāpuṣpairnānāgandhairnānādhūpairnānācūrṇairnānāvastrairnānāghaṇṭābhirnānā- patākābhirnānācāmarairnānāchatrairnānāvitānairnānāmuktāhārairnānāratnairnānāpradīpairnānāratnā raṇaiḥ pūjayitvā tebhyaḥ pañcāṅgapraṇāmaṃ kurvanti |

ihāryaviṣaye'nāgate'dhvani vajrācāryā dravyaluṇṭhakā bhaviṣyanti;
dravyārthaṃ dravyābhimānināmīśvarāṇāṃ gṛhaṃ gatvā dharmavikrayaṃ kariṣyanti;
abhavyānāṃ dravyalobhena lokottarābhiṣekaṃ dāsyanti;
rājādīnāṃ prāk kiṅkarāḥ paścād guravo bhaviṣyanti;
anyeṣāṃ dravyahīnānāṃ bhavyacittānāmapi daśākuśalakarmapathaparitya- ktānāṃ lokottarābhiṣekaṃ na dāsyanti;
teṣāṃ kleśamutpādayiṣyanti;
dravyavaśād vajrapadaṃ duṣṭasattvebhyaḥ prakāśayiṣyanti
anye'pi vajrapadamajñāyamānāḥ parasparaṃ vivādaṃ kariṣyanti ;
paṇḍitābhimānena tantrārthaṃmajānanto'pi tantraṭīkāṃ kariṣyanti;
mārakāyikāḥ sattvānāṃ guravo bhūtvā vajrapadaṃ viparītaṃ deśayiṣyanti;
buddhajñānaṃ dvīndriyajaṃ sukhaṃ bālamatīnāṃ prakāśa- yiṣyanti;
tṛtīyaṃ prajñājñānaṃ caturthaṃ tadeva tatpunastathāśabdena tṛtīyaṃ jñānaṃ buddhajñānaṃ vadiṣyanti — hetuphalayorabhedena bhagavatoktamiti
evamanāgate'dhvani duṣṭācāryapravarttanaṃ dṛṣṭvā buddhabhagavatā caturthaṃ prajñājñānābhiṣekaṃ sarvatantrāntareṣu nottānīkṛtam,
yad āryaviṣaye paṇḍitābhimānena pustakaṃ dṛṣṭvā vinābhiṣekena vajrayānadeśakā bhaviṣyanti;
tataḥ sarvalaghu- tantre mūlatantreṣu caturthaṃ prajñājñānaṃ mahāmudrābhāvanā dhūmādimārgaḥ suguptaḥ, kacinmūlatantreṣu prakaṭa iti |
atra punaḥ paramādibuddhe mūlatantre laghutantre'pi caturthaṃ prajñājñānaṃ prakaṭam |
mahāmudrābhāvanā dhūmādimārgaśca prakaṭa iti vajrācāryapāramparyakrameṇa nāgataḥ, yathā mantradevatābāhyasiddhisādhanaṃ vīrakramasvādhiṣṭhānakramaṃ ca gurupāramparyakrameṇāgatamiti |

ato ye paramādibuddhaṃ na jānanti te nāmasaṅgīti na jānanti ye nāmasaṅgīti na jānanti te vajradharajñānakāyaṃ na jānanti ye vajradharajñānakāyaṃ na jānanti te mantrayānaṃ na jānanti, ye mantrayānaṃ na jānanti te saṃsāriṇaḥ sarve vajrarabhagavato mārga rahitāḥ |
evaṃ paramādi- a buddhaṃ mokṣārthibhiḥ sacchiṣyaiḥ śrotavyaṃ sadguruṇā deśayitavyamiti |

99 vimalaprabhāya 1 [ lokadhātu- vāyvantān merusīmno narakaphaṇipuraṃ yojanānāṃ dvilakṣamiti
iha lokasaṃvṛtyā nānādhimuktisattvāśayavasena mānaṃ sattvādīnāṃ deśitaṃ pratibhāsate lokadhātoḥ
paramārthato mānonmānaṃ lokadhātorna sambhavati, sattvānāṃ puṇyapāpavaśāditi
iha yasyāṃ guhāyāṃ pañcahastapramāṇāyāṃ vītarāgo bodhisattvo vāvasati, tasyāṃ guhāyāṃ tasya puṇyajñānabhājanasya prabhāvena ṛddhibalena sasainyacakravarttī āgataḥ praviśati na ca sā kenacid vistāritā, na ca tasyāṃ guhāyāṃ praviṣṭasya cakravattisasainyasya saṃkīrṇatā bhavati, evaṃ lokadhātumānamapi paramārthato veditavyam
atra yallokadhātumānaṃ, tadyathā bāhye tathā dehe [kā ta iti vacanāt, lokadhātumānaṃ lokadhātumaṇḍalārthaṃ kāyadhātumānaṃ kāyamaṇḍalārthaṃ kāya- maṇḍalamityuktam iti vacanāt
yathā bāhya merulaukikamānena lakṣayojanocchrayaḥ, tathā śarīrakaṅkālaṃ hastamekamucchrayaṃ tena hastamānena kāyaścaturhastaḥ

evaṃ lokadhātumānaṃ saṃvṛtyā yathā tathā bhavatu, atra pratijñā na syād bhagavataḥ tāpācchedād” ityādyā
kiṃ ca pratijñā puṇyajñānasambhāraviṣaye

hatvā mlecchāṃśca yuddhe vicarati purataḥ sthāpayitvā svadharme kṛttretādvāparaṃ vai kaliyugamaparaṃ vartate kālayogāt || 22 ||
meroḥ pṛṣṭheṣu dikṣu bhramati bhuktile durjayo dānavānāṃ mlecchānāṃ yasmin bhūkhaṇḍe tathāgatadhamoṃ vinaṣṭasteṣāṃ viparyāsadharmo vartate, tasmin bhūmitale bhramati cakrī kaliyuge sa evādharmaṃ pravṛttiḥ, kaliyugaṃ vahutītyarthaḥ
tadevādharmātmakaṃ kaliyugam

hatvā mlecchāṃścakārād dānavādīṃśca yuddha e vicarati purataḥ sthāpayitvā svadharme tān mlecchādīn kṛttretādvāparaṃ vai kaliyugamaparaṃ vartate kālayogāt

etad yugaṃ kālayogād vartate

yasmin khaṇḍe sa cakrī praviśati balavān kṛdyugaṃ tatra yāti tretā pṛṣṭhe ca rājñaḥ kalirapi purato dvāparañca dvimadhye
viṃśatyekaṃ sahasraṃ rasaśatasahitaṃ varṣamānaṃ yugānām ekaikasya pramāṇaṃ yugaśaraguṇitaṃ mānavābde śataṃ yat || 23||
yasmin khaṇḍe sa cakkī praviśati balavān kṛyugaṃ tatra yāti; kṛyugaṃ nāma samyaksambuddhadharmaṃ yātītyarthaḥ
tretā pṛṣṭhe ca rājñastretā pṛṣṭhe rājño bhavati, dharmasyaikapādā- bhāvaḥ kṛdante tretānte dvipādābhāva:, kalirapi purato rājño dharmasya catuḥ pādābhāvaḥ varṣamānaṃ yugānāṃ caturṇāṃ mānaṃ tad bhavati
ekaikasya pramāṇaṃ yugaśaraguṇitamiti
yuga iti catvāraḥ, śara iti pañca taiḥ catuḥpañcāśadbhirguṇitaṃ yugaśaraguṇitamiti
mānavābde śataṃ yaditi
varṣaśataṃ guṇitaṃ catuḥśatādhikaṃ sahasrapañcakaṃ bhavati, pratyekayuga- mānaṃ tulyamiti
havinīta
paṭale lokadhātumāna saṃgrahauddeśaḥ idānīṃ manuṣyatanujādīnāṃ svasvaśvāsamānamucyate- sūkṣmocchvāsadhunāḍīdinayuga sahasra kaikaviṃśacchataiśca ṣaḍ bhirmānaṃ krameṇa tvaṇutanujanṛṇāṃ bhūtadevāsurāṇām
śakterbharturdinakaṃ vahati bhuktile śaktimānaṃ yugānāṃ khaṇḍe khaṇḍe ca cakrī vrajati śivapadaṃ dvādaśārkā dineke || 24 ||
sūkṣmocchvāsetyādi
sūkṣma iti manuṣyaśvāsaniḥśvāsasya ekasya ṣaṭśatādhika- ekaviṃśatsahasrabhūtasya ekāṃśaḥ sūkṣmaśvāsaḥ, tena śvāsaṣaṭkena tanujādīnāṃ kṛmikulā- dīnāmekaṃ pāṇī (ṇi) palam, ṣaṣṭibhiḥ pāṇipalaiḥ ghaṭīghaṭībhiḥ ṣaṣṭibhirdinamiti
dinaṃ nāma tanujādīnāṃ ṣaṭśatādhika ekaviṃśatsahasraśvāsaniḥśvāsasaṃkhyā, terucchvāsairgatanaṃ rāṇāṃ svadinaṃ bhūtapretānāṃ dhrubhiḥ svadinaṃ dhruriti
manuṣyaśvāsasya triśadguṇaḥ śvāsaḥ kṛṣṇa- pakṣo divā, śuklapakṣo rātriḥ pretānāṃ pitṝṇāṃ svadinamiti
nāḍībhirmanuṣyāṇāṃ ṣaṣṭayuttaratriśataśvāso devānāmekaśvāsaḥ
dinamati manuṣyāṇāṃ ṣaṭśatādhika-ekaviṃśat- sahasraśvāsāḥ, asurāṇāmekaśvāsaḥ
dhruriti manuṣyāṇāṃ varṣa:
śakterekaśvāsaḥ śakti- rityakaniṣṭhajātiḥ
yuga iti manuṣyāṇāṃ ṣaṭśatādhika ekavaśatsahasravarṣapiṇḍitaṃ bhartturekaśvāsaḥ
bharturiti naivasaṃjñāyatanopagajāteriti
sūkṣmocchvāsanāḍodina - yugasahasraika viśacchateśca ṣaḍbhirmānaṃ krameṇa tvaṇutanujanṛṇāṃ bhūtadevāsurāṇāṃ śakterbharturdinaikam
evaṃ pūrvoktakrameṇa eṣāṃ tanujādīnāṃ svakīyasvakīyaśvāsaiḥ ṣaṭ- śatādhika - ekaviṃśatsahasrudinaṃ bhavati
eṣu māneṣu śaktimānamakaniṣṭhadevamānaṃ vahati bhuvitale cakriṇo yugānāṃ śakti- mānamiti
khaṇḍe khaṇḍe ca cakro vrajati śivapadam
pratyekakhaṇḍe buddhadharmaṃ pravattayitvā vrajati mahāsukhapadaṃ mahāsukhasthānamiti
dvādaśārkā dinaike
evaṃ pratyekaikakhaṇḍe pratye- kaikacakravartī dharmapravarttakaḥ
ekadine dvādaśa cakravattino bhavanti; aṣṭādaśāṣṭādaśaśataiḥ śaktiśvāsaiḥ manuṣyāṇāṃ varṣerekaiko vrajati
evaṃ dvādaśārkā dharmaṃdivākarāḥ puṇyajñānamārga- pravarttakā iti
bāhye varṣeṇa dvādaśarāśibhedena dvādaśārkā vrajantīti
idānīṃ sūkṣmādonāṃ śvāsabhedena pratyekadine kālabheda ucyate- pakṣā māsāḥ samastā ṛtuyugasamayā agnikālo'yane dve varṣaṃ rājan samastaṃ tvanudinaghaṭikālagnapāṇīpalāni
etānyevaṃ vrajanti tvanutanujanṛṇāṃ bhūmidevāsurāṇāṃ śakterbharttṛdina ke tribhuvananilaye dehamadhye tathaiva || 25 ||
pakṣā ityādi

asya paramāgamayuktyā niyamo nāsti; kintu vedeṣūktam- śatāyurvai puruṣaḥ śatendriyaḥ (ai0 brā0 2|17|4|19) iti
atra prapañcavacanamidam - manuṣyāṇāṃ varṣaśataṃ paramāyuriti, kṛyuge narāṇāmāyurvṛddhivaśāditi

jyotirjñānavidhimahodda eśaḥ idānīṃ sambhalaviṣaye mañjuśriya utpādanaṃ mlecchadharmotpādāt jyotiṣasiddhānta- vināśaḥ laghukaraṇapravṛttiḥ tathāgatavyākaraṇamādyābdādityādinā vitanomīti-
ādyābdāt ṣaṭśatāndaiḥ prakaṭa (ṭe ) yaśanṛpaḥ sambhalākhye bhaviṣyat tasmānnāgaiḥ śatābdaḥ khalu makhaviṣaye mlecchadharmapravṛttiḥ
tasmin kāle dharaṇyāṃ sphuṭalaghukaraṇaṃ mānava rveditavyaṃ siddhāntānāṃ vināśaḥ sakalabhuvitale kāla yoge'bhaviṣyat || 26 ||
ādyābdāt ṣaṭ śatābdaiḥ prakaṭa yaśanupaḥ sambhalākhye bhaviṣyaditi

siddhāntānāṃ vināśa iti
siddhāntaṃ brahma' sauraṃ yama nakaṃ romakamiti, eṣāṃ caturṇāṃ vināśaṃ siddhāntānāṃ vināśaḥ
sakalabhuvitala iti
sakala iti yatra tīrthikasiddhāntā varttante * ; tatra sakalaṃ bhuvitalaṃ śītādakṣiṇam, tasmin bhuktile; na sambhalādiviṣayeṣu bauddha- siddhāntasya vināśa iti
kālayoge bhaviṣyaditi
kālaḥ mlecchadharmaḥ tena siddhāntānāṃ yogaḥ kālayogaḥ, tasmāt kālayogād bhaviṣyati ; kālayoga iti pañcamyarthe saptamī
ataḥ siddhāntavināśāllaghukaraṇaṃ sphuṭamiti mātṛmodakavacanam, tīthikānāṃ paramārthato na sphuṭaṃ laghukaraṇādikam
kutaḥ ? siddhāntānāṃ vināśa iti kaṭākṣavacanāt

hṛtamṛturaviṇā
adhomāsapiṇḍaṃ hṛtaṃ ṣaḍviṃśatyadhikaśatena labdhaṃ mūni rāśau dhanaṃ bhavati
pratimāsaṃ piṇḍa- dvayārthaṃ mūni rāśau deyaṃ bhavati
prathamakarahate paścād deyaṃ bhavati
bhūtamiśraṃ samasta- miti
karaṇāpekṣā ṛturaviṇā bhāgalabdhaṃ siddhāntam ( : )
yataḥ ṣaḍviṃśatya- dhikamāsaśatena ekapiṇḍaṃ piṇḍasthāne'dhikaṃ bhavati, candracaraṇavaśāditi
piṇḍamaṣṭadvibha- 1. bho. sTen Hog (ūrdhvo'dho ); ka. ūrddhāradho | 2. ṅa. vārabhāge | ktamiti
aṣṭaviṃśatibhirbhaktaṃ piṇḍaṃ bhavati; saptavārapadatvādaṣṭaviṃśatibhirbhāgaḥ syā- [52b]t, tena bhāgena labdhaṃ pratimāsaṃ vārapadaṃ bhavati
vāraghaṭikādhaḥ ṛturaviṇā bhāga- labdhāvaśeṣaṃ (:) piṇḍāvayavaṃ (:) bhavati, piṇḍādhaḥ sthāpanīyami (i) ti
idānīṃ sūryanakṣatra vakamucyate- tristhāmadhye hateśā iti
te śuddhamāsā ( : ) tristhā ( - ne) mūrdhni-madhye adhasi (adho) bhavanti
madhye ye māsāste īśairhatā ekādaśahatā iti karaṇāpekṣā na siddhāntāpekṣe ti
adharanavaguṇairbhāgalabdhaṃ dvimiśramiti
adho rāśau navaguṇai reko- nacatvāriṃśadbhirbhāgalabdhaṃ dvimiśramiti karaṇāpekṣayā kṣepakaḥ
ūnaṃ madhye madhyarāśau hīnaṃ ṛṇamityarthaḥ
ghaṭobhirhṛtamapi tu dhanamiti
madhyarāśerghaṭībhiḥ ṣaṣṭibhirhataṃ labdhaṃ nakṣatrapiṇḍaṃ mūni rāśau dhanaṃ bhavati
ṣaṣṭibhāgāvaśeṣā nakṣatraghaṭikā bhavanti
tadeva dhanaṃ mūrdhni rāśi (śau) dvihatvā, yuktaṃ cakṣaṃ prabhakto bhavati nṛpa raveḥ śeṣa ṛkṣādibhogaḥ vāre vāraṃ pradeyaṃ dviguṇanṛpaghaṭī piṇḍake dve tvadhaśca
dve ca rudranāḍya : prakaṭayati dhanaṃ sūryabhoge pradeyam etanmāsaṃ dhruvaṃ syāt kathitamapi punarmāsi māsi pradeyam ||30||
yuktaṃ ca prabhaktaṃ saptā viṃśatibhiḥ prabhakto bhavati
nṛpāmantraṇam
raveḥ śeṣa ṛkṣādibhogaḥ
atra mūrdhni nakṣatrapiṇḍamadho nakṣatraghaṭikā piṇḍamiti
punaśśuddhādapya- śuddhamāsadha vakaṃ bālānāṃ pratibodhanārthaṃ racitam
vāre vāraṃ pradeyam, vārasthāne vāramekaṃ deyam
dviguṇanṛpaghaṭī
nṛpa iti ṣoḍaśa; dviguṇanṛpaghaṭī dvātriṃśad ghaṭī ghaṭikāsthāne dehā ; piṇḍake dve tvadhaśceti ghaṭikādhaḥ piṇḍasthāne dve deye bhavataḥ
dve carkṣe nakṣatra- sthāne
rudranāḍyo nakṣatraghaṭikāsthāne
rudra iti ekādaśa nāḍyaḥ deyā iti
dhanaṃ prakaṭayati sūryabhoge pradeyaṃ nakṣatrādikam
tasmāt māsaghra vakaṃ syāt kathitamapi punarmāsi māsi pradeyam
pratyekamāsi pradeyamiti māsaghravakaṃ ka raṇāpekṣāvaśena bhagavatā anāgatameva vyākṛtamiti
idānīṃ māsa va tithyā digaṇitamucyate- deyā heyāśca deyāḥ punarapi tithayo vāranāḍīpadeṣu piṇḍe bhāge'bdhicandraḥ samaviṣamagate deyaheyau padārthoṃ śūnye śūnyaṃ viśuddhaṃ tridaśaśaśipade pañca netrārkayordik tithyākhyaikādaśe'gnau daśajaladhipade viṃśatiścaikahīnā || 31 ||
deyā heyāśca deyāḥ punarapi tithayo vāranāḍīpadeṣviti
iha pratimāsadhruvake śuklapratipadādyekatithimārabhya yāvat triṃśattamāmāvāsī tāvat tithayo deyā heyāḥ 1. ṅa. adho | 2. kha. navaguṇainavaguṇai | 3 ṅa 0 dhruvāt
paṭale jyotirjñāna vidhimahoddeśaḥ punardeyā iti vārasthāne deyāḥ pratidinavārārtham; ghaṭikāsthāne heyāścandrakalāyā ekaghaṭī- hānita:, punardeyāḥ piṇḍasthāne candrapadānāṃ dhanaṛṇabhogapariśuddhita iti
piṇḍe bhāge'- bdhicandraḥ samaviṣamagate deyaheyau padārthāviti
piṇḍasthāne piṇḍādabdhicandreṇa ' caturdaśabhāgena samagatena padārtho dhanaṃ bhavati; viṣamagatena ṛṇaṃ padārtho bhavati
atra sama- bhāge dvābhyāṃ gataścaturbhirvāgata iti na gato'pi sama iti
viṣamabhāge ekena gataḥ tribhi - vagata iti
tadupari nāsti bhāgaḥ
saptapañcāśadūrdhvaṃ triśattithisahitāḥ saptā - viṃśatipiṇḍakā na bhavanti
ataścaturdaśabhāgaiścatvāro vibhaktā bhavantīti tena samaviṣama- bhāgena padārthā deyā heyā vā veditavyāḥ
padārtho nāma candracā (vā) rapadāni caturdaśa- sthāne'dha ūrdhve saptasaptapadavyāvṛttyā anulomavilomābhyāṃ pṛthivyādiguṇabhedabhinnāni
te ca padārthā deyā heyā bhavanti
śūnye śūnyaṃ viśuddhamiti
caturdaśabhāgāvaśeṣaṃ yadi śūnyaṃ tadā padārtho'pi śūnyaḥ, na deyo na heya iti nyāya: karaṇā
tridaśaśaśipade pañceti
trayodaśame pade avaśeṣe dṛṣṭe ekapade vā pañcaghaṭikā dhanaṃ vā ṛṇaṃ vā bhavati
evaṃ netrārkayodi (53b)- giti dvipade adhike'vaśeṣe dvādaśe vā daśaghaṭikā deyā bhavanti, candrakalāvṛddhihānivaśā- diti
tithyākhya iti pañcadaśa; ekādaśe agnāviti tṛtīye bhavanti
daśapadajaladhīti caturthe pade bhāgāvaśeṣe dṛṣṭa viṃśatiścaikahīnā bhavanti
dvātriṃśat ( ti ) pañcarandhre ṛtuvasuni jinā: saptame pañcaviṃśat (tiḥ ) tasminnarkaprabhedaiḥ prakaṭitaravikā deyayā bhavanti
bhūtābhūteṣu vedāḥ śikhikaraśaśinaḥ pūrvabhāge'pare ca piṇḍe bhāge'bdhicandraḥ samaviṣamagate deyaheyāśca vārāḥ ||32||
dvāviṃśat (ti) pañcame randhra iti navame bhavanti
ṛturiti ṣaṭ (ḍ) vasunītyaṣṭame pade jināścaturviṃśatirbhavanti
saptame pade bhāgāvaśeṣe dṛṣṭe pañcaviṃśat ghaṭikā same deyā viṣame heyā bhavanti
tasminnarkaprabhedaiḥ prakaṭitaravikā deyayā bhavanti
tasmin tithibhoge'rkaprabhedena sūryapadāni bhavanti; sūryasya ṛṇaṃ tasmin ṛṇam, sūryasya dhanaṃ tasminneva dhanamiti sūryapramedaḥ
atra candrapadāni bhūtābhūteṣu vedāḥ śikhikaraśaśinaḥ pūrvabhāge'pare ceti
bhūtā iti pañca, punabhūteti tataḥ adhaḥ pañca, iṣuriti tato'dhaḥ pañca vedā iti tato'dhaścatvāri, śikhīti tato'dhaḥ trayaḥ; kara iti tato'dho dve, śaśina iti tato'dha ekapadam
evaṃ sapta- sthāneṣu padāni
tato'parasaptasthāneṣu vilomena ekādinā caturdaśasthāne pañca padāni yāvat
evaṃ candrapadānāṃ nyāso bhavati caturdaśasthāneṣviti
1. ṅa. piṇḍādardhavaktrendreṇa | 2. ṅa. pustake nāsti | 3. bho. Cig La Sogs Pa ( ekādinā ); ka. ekodinā; ṅa. ekadināt | piṇḍe bhāge'bdhicandraiḥ samaviṣamagate deyaheyāśca vārā iti
atrāpi caturdaśa- bhāgāvaśeṣe dṛṣṭe samagate vārāḥ pañcādayo deyāḥ, viṣamagate heyā [54] iti
caturdaśa- bhāgāvaśeṣe eke dṛṣṭe pañca ghaṭikā grāhyā; dvitīye dvitīyasthānasthaiḥ saha daśa; tṛtīye tṛtīyasthānasthaiḥ saha pañcā (ca) daśa; caturthe caturthasthānasthaiścaturbhiḥ saha ekonaviṃśatiḥ; pañcame pañcamasthānasthaistribhiḥ saha dvāviṃśat; ṣaṣṭhe ṣaṣṭhasthānasthābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ saha caturviṃśatiḥ saptame saptamasthānasthenaikena saha pañcaviṃśatiḥ
tataścandracaraṇavṛddhirnivarttate
aṣṭame dṛṣṭe sati aṣṭapadāni pūrvaṃ saptapadaiḥ sahitāni
tyaktvā parāṇi ṣaṭpadānyabhuktānyeka- piṇḍaṃ kṛtvā caturviṃśatighaṭikā deyā heyā vā bhavanti
navame pañcapadānāṃ dvāviṃśad ghaṭikā; daśame catuṣpadānāmekonaviṃśatiḥ ekādaśe tripadānāṃ pañca'; dvādaśe dvipadānāṃ daśa; trayodaśe ekapadasya pañca; caturdaśame (śe) śūnyamiti siddhāntaniścayaḥ
saccāreṇāvaśeṣaṃ hatamṛturaviṇā bhāgalabdhaṃ ca tadvat tatraivārkaprabhedai ravipadaghaṭikā deyayā bhavanti
vedaistithyāhataṃ yat sphuṭamapi tu dhanaṃ tat tribhāgena miśram ṛtvṛkṣaṃ satripādaṃ tvṛṇamapi tu raveḥ śodhayed bhuktimadhye || 33 ||
saccāreṇāvaśeṣaṃ hatamṛturaviṇā bhāgalabdhaṃ ca tadvaditi |
atra saccāra iti piṇḍādha(:)sthitānāṃ piṇḍāvayavānām, ṛturaviṇā bhāgalabdhāvaśeṣāṇāmiti |
saccāra- piṇḍe caturbhāgāvaśeṣaṃ cārapadaṃ yat tadupari sañcārapadaṃ piṇḍāvayavānāṃ śatapadaṃ bhavati |
tatra grahaḥ praviṣṭaḥ |
tasmāt tad grāhyaṃ deyayārtham |
tena padenāvaśeṣaṃ ( ) piṇḍāvayavaṃ (T) hataṃ (T), tataḥ ṣaṭviṃśatyadhikaśatena bhāgalabdhaṃ ca tadvad deyam |
sarvaṃ yathā pūrvacaraṇa- ghaṭikāpiṇḍaṃ tadvaditi |
tatraiva cārkaprabhedaiḥ pūrvavad ravikā deyā heyā vā bhavantīti siddhāntaḥ |
idānīṃ pratidinasūryabhoga ucyate- vedaistithyāhataṃ yat sphuṭamapi tu dhanaṃ tat bhāgena miśramiti |
vedaiścaturbhistithi (bhi)cāhataṃ guṇitaṃ vedesthiyāhataṃ sphuṭaṃ dhanaṃ bhavati |
sūryasya ghaṭikāsthāne tasyā guṇitaṃ tithyāstribhāgena miśraṃ dhanaṃ ghaṭikāsthāne deyamiti |
ṛtvṛkṣaṃ satripādaṃ tvṛṇamapi tu raveḥ śodhayed bhaktimadhye iti |
iha ravikāpadaviśuddhayarthaṃ sūryasya janmarāśiḥ śodhanīyaḥ, yena sūryasya pratyahaṃ vāraghaṭīpāṇī (ṇi) palādibhogo jñāyate, anyathā karaṇe śuddhirnāsti pratyahaṃ sūryasyeti |
ato'śvinyādinakṣatrabhogāt ṣaṭnakṣatraṃ satripādamiti pañcacatvāriṃśad ghaṭikā iti meṣādirāśitrayaṃ śodhitaṃ bhavati |
karkaṭe janmarāśirbhavati, sūryasya caraṇaśuddhita iti |
ata ṛtvṛkṣaṃ satripādaṃ tvṛṇamapi tu raveḥ śodhayed pañcadaśa |
ka. caturdaśa; bho. Cha Sas Si (caturbhāga ) |
ṅa. guruḥ |
paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ bhuktimadhye |
bhuktirnakṣatrādibhogaḥ, tasmin bhuktimadhye nakṣatrasthāne nakṣatraṃ śodhayed, ghaṭikāsthāne ghaṭikāṃ śodhayediti |
śiṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ yathāre rasayugaśaśino mandakārye padāni hatvā bhogena nāḍī : śaraguṇaśaśibhirbhāgalabdhaṃ sabhogam |
deyaṃ heyaṃ ca sūrye tvayanagativaśānnānyathā śuddhirasti sūrye kālo raso'hiśca daśa haraharā rudradignāgaṣaṭ ca || 34 ||
sūryabhogaṃ pṛthakkṛtvā śiṣṭaṃ kāryaṃ' yathāre' iti |
atra pariśeṣaṃ kāryaṃ yathā maṅga- lasya tathā sūryasyāpi jñātavyam, kintu maṅgalasya cārapadāni bhinnāni |
sūryasya rasayuga- śaśino mandakārye padāni |
rasa iti ṣaṭ yuga iti catvāri, tato'dhaḥ śaśina iti, tato'dha eka iti |
rāśitraye pūrvārddha aparārddhe vilomena caturtharāśāvekaḥ, pañcamarāśau IC catvāri, ṣaṣṭharāśau ṣaḍetad rāśikarma mandakāryamiti |
asmin mandakārye etāni ṣaṭ- padāni ṣaṭsu karkaṭādiṣu rāśiṣu bhavanti |
aparaṃ maṅgalaṃ vaktavyamiti |
hatvā bhogena nāḍīḥ śaraguṇaśaśibhirbhāga- labdhaṃ sabhogam, deyaṃ heyaṃ ca sūrye tvayanagativaśāt nānyathā śuddhirastīti idaṃ vacanaṃ niyamārtham, yathā maṅgale cārabhogena hatvā nāḍīstataḥ śaraguṇaśaśibhiriti ekarāśighaṭikāpiṇḍaṃ pañcatriṃśadadhikaśataṃ tena bhāgena labdham ; ghaṭikāpiṇḍaṃ ghaṭikā- sthāne dhanaṃ bhavati, ayanagativaśād ṛṇaṃ ca bhavati |
makarādau dhanaṃ karkaṭādī ṛṇaṃ bhavatīti ayanagativaśāt pratyahaṃ śuddhirnānyathā karaṇoktavidhineti |
atra karaṇoktavidhirucyate- sūrye kālo rasa iti karkaṭe ṛṇaṃ prathamapakṣe kāla iti tisro ghaṭikāḥ |
rasa iti ṣaṭ dvitīye karkaṭapakṣe |
evaṃ siṃhe ahiścetyaṣṭa daśa (ca) iti |
tathā kanyāyāṃ hara ekādaśa, punarhara ekādaśa, tatastulāyāṃ rudra ekādaśa, digiti daśa, ityevaṃ vṛścike nāga ityaṣṭa ṣaṭ ca ||34||
vahniḥ khañcāyanānte bhavati dhananṛṇāṃ (mṛṇaṃ ) cottare dakṣiṇe ca ṣaṇmāsaṃ pakṣabhedaiścarati dinakaro māsasaṃkrāntibhedāt |
datvā sūrye tithīṃścāpyadhararasahatā vārabhogena miśrā ūnībhūtāḥ śaśāṅko bhavati narapate miśrasūryenduyogaḥ || 35 ||

miśrasūryenduyoga iti sūryendubhogo miśro yogo bhavati |
akṣṇā tithyā hataṃ yad gataśaśikaraṇaṃ saptabhāgāvaśeṣaṃ śuddhābdā nāgamiśrāḥ khakhajaladhihatāḥ śodhitā nāgamiśraḥ |
śailendvagniprabhaktā gaganarasahatā nāḍikādayādibhaktā dvitriṃśad vāranāḍyo dhruvakamiha yutaṃ cābdasaṃkrāntimāse || 36 ||
akṣṇā tithyā hataṃ yad gataśaśikaraṇamiti |
akṣṇā dvābhyāṃ tithiṣu hatameka- rahitaṃ saptabhāgāvaśeṣaṃ karaṇaṃ bhavati; vārabhogena bhogo bhavati |
atra pañcāṅgāni vāraḥ, tithiḥ *, nakṣatram, yogaḥ, karaṇamiti |
atra vārāḥ - āditya - soma - maṅgala-budha-bṛhaspati- śukra - śanayaḥ sapta; tithayaḥ - pratipatādyāḥ pañcadaśa iti; nakṣatrāṇi - aśvinī bharaṇī kṛttikā rohiṇī mṛgaśirā ārdrā punarvasu puṣya aśleṣā madhā pūrvaphālgunī uttaraphālgunī hastā citrā svāti (tī) viśākhā anurādhā jyeṣṭhā mūlā (laṃ) pūrvāṣāḍhā uttarāṣāḍhā śrava- ṇā (ṇa) ra dhaniṣṭhā śatabhūṣā ( bhiṣā ' ) pūrvabhādrapadā uttarabhādrapadā revatīti saptaviṃśatiḥ |
tathā yogaḥ– viṣkambhaḥ prītiḥ āyuṣmān saubhāgyaḥ śobhanaḥ atigaṇḍaḥ sukarmā dhṛtiḥ śūla: gaṇḍa : vṛddhiḥ dhruvaḥ śaṅkuḥ ' vyāghātaḥ harṣaṇaḥ vajraḥ siddhiḥ vyatipātaḥ varīyān parighaḥ śivaḥ sādhyaḥ śubhaḥ śuklaḥ brahmā aindraḥ vaidhṛtiriti saptaviṃśati yogā iti |
8 śuklapratipadaparārddha karaṇaṃ prathamaṃ vavam, dvitīyāyāṃ pūrvāparārddha vālavaṃ kaulavam, tṛtīyāyāṃ pūrvāparārddha taitilaṃ garajam, caturthyāṃ pūrvāparārddhe vaṇijaṃ vṛṣṭiriti |
evaṃ pañca- myāṃ vavaṃ vālavam, ṣaṣṭhyāṃ kaulavaṃ taitilam, saptamyāṃ garajaṃ vaṇijam , aṣṭamyāṃ pūrvārddha viṣṭiriti |
aṣṭamyāmaparāddhe vavam, navamyāṃ pūrvāparāddhe vālavaṃ kaulavam, daśamyāṃ taitila garajam , ekādaśyāṃ pūrvāparārddha vaṇijaṃ viṣṭiriti |
puna- pūrvāparārddha kha. śravaṇām pustake nakṣatreṣu samāsāntaprayogaḥ kṛtaḥ pustake 'vava karaṇaṃ' iti kramaḥ pustakayoḥ nāsti kaulavam' iti kramaḥ garavaṇik pustakayoḥ nāsti kaulavagaraṃ pustakayoḥ nāsti ataḥ paraṃ ṅa. pustakaṃ na labdham paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ rdvādaśyāṃ pūrvāparārddha vavaṃ vālavam, trayodaśyāṃ kaulavaṃ' taitilam, caturdaśyāṃ garajaṃ ra vaṇijam paurṇamāsyāṃ pūrvārddhe viṣṭiriti aparārddhe vavam |
kṛṣṇapra tipadi pūrvāpa - rārddhe' vālavaṃ kaulavam, dvitīyāyāṃ pūrvāparārddha tailijaṃ (tilaṃ) garajam , tṛtīyāyāṃ pūrvā- parārddhe' vaṇijaṃ vṛṣṭiriti |
evaṃ caturthyāṃ (pūrvāparārddhe') vavaṃ vālavam, pañcamyāṃ (pūrvāparārddhe) kaulavaṃ taitilam, ṣaṣṭhyāṃ garajaṃ vaṇijam saptamyāṃ pūrvārddha viṣṭiriti aparārddha vavam, aṣṭamyāṃ (pūrvāparārddha ) vālavaṃ kaulavam, navamyāṃ taitilaṃ garajama, daśamyāṃ pūrvāparārddha
vaṇijaṃ viṣṭiriti
evam ekādaśyāṃ (pūrvāparārddha ) vavaṃ vālavam, dvādaśyāṃ kaulavaṃ taitilam, trayodaśyāṃ garajaṃ* vaṇijam **, caturdaśyāṃ pūrvārddha viṣṭiriti
sapta karaṇaparibhogaḥ ( gāḥ)

37 || meṣādau vāranāḍyāmiti
vārasthāne ghaṭikāsthāne vāranāḍyām
kramapari- racitāḥ kṣepaṇe dvādaśaite candrādrībhūta iti
candra iti vārasthāne eka: adri (drī ) bhūta iti saptapañcāśat ghaṭikāsthāne
atra vārasthāne varṣadhruvakeṇa sārddhaṃ yo vāro bhavati, tena vāreṇa meṣa saṃkrāntirbhavati; ghaṭikāsthāne yā ghaṭikāstābhirghaṭikābhiriti karaṇanyāyaḥ
vedāviti dvivacanaṃ chā (cha)ndovaśāditi
vedaṃ (dāḥ) catvāri
vārasthāne guṇaśara iti tripañcāśad ghaṭikāsthāne vṛṣasaṃkrāntau śaśina iti vārasthāne ekaḥ
śailaśa (ca) ndramiti saptadaśaghaṭikāsthāne
tṛtīya iti mithunasaṃkrāntau; veda iti vārasthāne catvāraḥ
agnibhūta iti tripañcāśad ghaṭikāsthāne karkaṭa saṃkrāntau
candra iti vāre ekaḥ
nayanakara iti dvāviṃśad ghaṭikāsthāne siṃhe ( siṃhasaṃkrāntau)
yuga iti vārasthāne catvāri
vedanetrāṇoti caturviṃśatiḥ ghaṭikāsthāne
ṣaṣṭhe iti kanyāsaṃkrāntau
ṣaḍ- vāre candrabāṇa iti ekapañcāśad ghaṭikāsthāne tulāsaṃkrāntau
candra iti vāra ekaḥ
giriyuga iti saptacatvāriṃśad ghaṭikāsthāne vṛścikasaṃkrāntau
śikhīti vārasthāne trayaḥ
ṣaṭ candra iti ṣoḍaśa ghaṭikāsthāne dhanuḥsaṃkrāntau
veda iti vārasthāne catvāraḥ
adrilokā iti saptatriṃśat ghaṭikāsthāne makarasaṃkrāntī
ṣaḍ vedau śūnyaśūnyaṃ nayanaśarapadaṃ mīnarāśau raveśca ṣaṇmāsaṃ hānivṛddhirbhavati dinaniśāyāśca sūryapracāraiḥ abdhiprāṇāgniliptāḥ pratidinasamaye vṛddhināśe'yanāṅgaiḥ savye rātreśca vṛddhirbhavati dinaniśāvuttare vāsarasya ||

nayanaśara iti dvāpañcāśat ghaṭikāsthāne

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ lokadhātu- iti
anenāpi naṣṭonnayanena sūryabhogaḥ sphuṭo bhavati
pratyahaṃ sūryamaṇḍaladinabhogena' vinā bhogaḥ śuddho bhavati varṣamekaṃ yāvat
punaraparavarṣe'parasaṃkrāntidhruvakaṃ [ bhavati, tena prativarṣamātraṃ sūryabhogaḥ śuddhaḥ ], caitramāsāt punaścaitramāsaṃ yāvat
tato'paravarṣamapekṣya karaṇasya tena kiñcinmāsamekaṃ dhruvakaṃ śuddham
tato'śuddho dinabhogaḥ sūryasya, pratyahaṃ triśvāsa catuḥ pāṇī (ṇi) palāntareṇa uttarāyaṇāduttarāyaṇaṃ yāvat pañca dināni bhogava - śādūnī bhavanti
pratimāse pañcaviṃśati ghaṭikā ūnī bhavanti
karaṇasaṃkrāntikālāt karaṇasaṃkrāntikālaṃ pratimāse pañcaviṃśatighaṭikādhiko varṣāvadheḥ pañcavārādhika iti
atronādhika ( - ) kathaṃ jñāyate ?
uttarāyaṇamāsādau daśadivasaṃ yāvat parīkṣā karttavyā saṃkucchāyayā
yasmād dinādārabhya saṃkucchāyā nivarttate uttarataḥ, tat saṃkrānti- dinaṃ sūryasya tena vāreṇa tayā tithyā tena yogena tena karaṇeneti
tasmin dine sūryabhogo nakṣatrasthāne viṃśatiḥ, ghaṭikāsthāne pañcadaśa iti mūladhruvakam
tasmin dhruvake pratyahaṃ sūryamaṇḍala dinairlabdhaṃ prakṣipya tato ravipadāni śodhayet pūrvoktavidhinā; yatrārkasya maṇḍala- dināni ghaṭikāpāṇīpalāni śvāsarāśicakraprabhogāntā uktā mūlatantre; tadyathā- kharvvagnyāhatamekābdaṃ dvisthaṃ dviguṇitaṃ tvadhaḥ
pañcaṣaṣṭyā hataṃ labdhaṃ mūrdhni rāśau dhanaṃ kṣipet
śeṣaṃ ṣaṣṭyā hataṃ bhūyo bhāgalabdhaṃ tato'pyadhaḥ
punaḥ ṣaṣṭyā hataṃ śeṣaṃ bhāgalabdhaṃ tato'pyadhaḥ
punaḥ ṣaḍbhirhataṃ śeṣaṃ pañcaṣaṣṭyā vibhañjitam
labdhaṃ tasyāpyadhaḥ sthāpyaṃ śvāsapiṇḍaṃ narādhipa
dināni ghaṭikā liptāḥ śvāsān kṛtvā tridhā punaḥ
adho girikha [58a] śailāptaṃ madhyarāśau dhanaṃ kṣipet
catuḥṣaṭyā tato labdhaṃ mūrdhni rāśau ṛṇaṃ haret
avaśeṣa dinānyatra ghaṭī pāṇī palāni ca
śvāsāśva maṇḍalaṃ bhānoścakrabhogād bhavatyamī
dinaikaṃ hatamṛkṣeryannāḍībhiḥ ṣaṣṭibhistataḥ
sūryamaṇḍalabhogena pañcaṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayeṇa
iti
sūryamaṇḍalabhogena labdhā nāḍyo dinaṃ prati
bhūyaḥ ṣaṣṭyāhatā liptāḥ śvāsaḥ ṣaḍabhirhato bhavet
bhogo'yaṃ sūryanakṣatranāḍikādiṣu yojayet
ayanādau pratyahaṃ deyo yāvad bhūyo'yanaṃ bhavet
uktakarmavidhānena śodhanīyaṃ raveḥ padam
anyathā karaṇamārgeṇa śuddhirnāsti sadā raveḥ
aśuddhe sūryabhoge'smin so ( śo ) dhitā' maṅgalādayaḥ
na sphuṭā vai bhavantyatra mūlanaṣṭā iva drumāḥ
candrasya maṇḍalaṃ vakṣye rāśicakraprabhogataḥ
cakranāḍyo śvernāḍī māsabhogena miśritāḥ
triṃśadbhāgena labdhāḥ syuḥ candrabhogo dinaṃ prati
nāḍikācakranāḍyantā yairbhuścandramādinaiḥ
ghaṭīpāṇīpalaśvāsairmaṇḍalaṃ tasya tad bhavet
dinairnavaśataiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ sahasra rāhumaṇḍalam
prasiddhaṃ maṅgalādīnāṃ maṇḍalaṃ svasvavārataḥ
dinamekaṃ hataṃ cakṣai ( ) maṇḍalena vibhañjitam
labdhaṃ bhavati nakṣatraṃ śeṣaṃ ṣaṣṭyā hataṃ punaḥ
nāḍyo maṇḍalabhāgena labdhā liptāstathaiva ca
śvāsā maṇḍalabhāgena grahāṇāṃ pratyahaṃ nṛpa
tadeva kṣepakaṃ kṛtvā grahacāraiḥ śodhayed graham
grahabhogo yadā'śuddhastadā candrakalā (lo) daye
jñātavyaścandrabhogenāyanarekhāsthitasya vai
ayanena śodhayet sūrya candra sūryeṇa śodhayet
sthitamekakalā bhoge indunā maṅgalādikam
tithi rāhupraveśena' parvacchede'śite'śite
tithyorubhayormadhye vimardanendusūryayoḥ
evaṃ sarvaṃ parijñāya siddhānte bhogamanyathā
anyathā laghukaraṇāderato'śuddhi vivarjayet”
iti
evamuktakrameṇa paramādibuddhatantrarājoktaṃ siddhāntalakṣaṇaṃ laghutantrarāje “siddhāntānāṃ vināśa (kā0 ta0 1.26) iti vacanena saṃgṛhītaṃ tantreṇa vispaṣṭaṃ kṛtam
tatkasya hetoḥ ?
iha tīrthikā laghutantrarājaṃ dṛṣṭvā sūryabho gaṃ viśuddhaṃ dṛṣṭvā svakīya- karaṇāntare likhiṣyānti ; asmākaṃ karaṇe'pi sūryabhogaviśuddhirastīti, ato hetornottā- nīkṛtaṃ ṭīkāpekṣayeti
ṣaṇmāsaṃ hānivṛddhī dve yathāsaṃkhyaṃ hānirvṛddhiśca bhavati
dinaniśāyāśca dinasya ca niśāyāśca, sūryapracārairuttaracārairdakṣiṇacāreḥ sūryasya sūrya pracārairiti
andhiprāṇāgniliptāḥ pratidinasamaye vṛddhināśe'yanāṅgeridaṃ mānaṃ kailāśasyottarabhāge himavantaṃ yāvat pratyahaṃ catvāraḥ śvāsāstroṇi pāṇīpalāni vṛddhirnāśaścāyanāṅge- rayanāṅgaṃ sārddhaṃ dvayaśītyadhikadinaśataṃ tairdinairayanāṅgaḥ savye rātrevṛddhirbhavati
uttare vāsarasyeti
kailāśa (sa) sya dakṣiṇe vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyam
iha māne dakṣiṇāyana- saṃkrāntidine ṣaṭtriṃśadbhirghaṭikābhirdinaṃ bhavati, caturviṃśatibhī rātrirbhavatīti'
evamutta- rāyaṇe rātridinama naṃ vilomena jñātavyamiti
idānīṃ rāhunakṣatrabhoga ucyate- māsā netrārkamiśrā nayanaviguṇitāścandraparvaikamiśrāḥ kharvvambhodhiprabhaktā munikaraguṇitāḥ khādibhāgena carkṣam
śiṣṭāḥ ṣaṣṭayāhatā ye punarapi ghaṭikāstena liptā hatāste cakraṃ labdhaṃ na vaktraṃ bhavati ca taminaḥ pucchaṛkṣārddhamiśram
māsā iti
māsāḥ karaṇamāsā iti
netrārkamiśrā iti karaṇāpekṣā na siddhāntāpekṣā
netrārka iti
dvāviṃśatyadhikaśatamiśrā iti
nayanaviguṇitāḥ pakṣarāśinimittaṃ dvābhyāṃ guṇitā iti
candraparveka miśrāḥ pūrṇimābhogārthaṃ pakṣamekaṃ miśram ; amāvāsyābhogārthaṃ pakṣadvayamiśraṃ pakṣarāśau bhavati
kharvvambhodhiprabhaktā iti rāhoḥ pakṣabhogārtham
pakṣamaṇḍalena ṣaṣṭayuttaracatuḥ- pakṣaśatena bhāgaḥ
tena khambhodhinā prabhaktā ye avaśeṣa pakṣāste munikara- guṇitā bhavanti
nakṣatrabhogārthaṃ khādibhāgena carkṣam
khādoti śūnyādinā ṣaṣṭyuttara- catuḥśatabhāgena labdhamṛkṣaṃ bhavati
punaravaśeṣā śiṣṭāḥ ṣaṣṭyāhatā ye, tenaiva bhāgena labdhā ghaṭikā bhavanti
evaṃ ṣaṣṭyā hatāstenaiva bhāgena liptā bhavanti
ṣaḍbhirhatāstenaiva bhāgena śvāsā bhavanti
cakra ṃ labdho ( - ) na vaktram
atra yat kharvvambhodhinā bhāga- labdhaṃ nakṣatrādikaṃ taccakre saptaviṃśatinakṣatrātmake ūnīkṛte cakra vaktraṃ bhavati
vaktraṃ nakṣatra bhoge'śvinyādinā bhavati ca tamino, rāhostamo'syāstīti tamī, tasya taminaḥ
pucchaṛkṣārddhamiśramiti
ṛkṣārddha sārddhatrayodaśanakṣatrāṇi tairmiśraṃ mukhabhoge puccha iti pucchabhogo bhavatīti
māsāstriśadhatāśca tristhāmadhye vimiśraṃ śailatvaṃ kaiśca miśraḥ pratidinasahitāḥ śailakhāgaiśca labdhaṃ tvapi jaladhirasairlabdhahīno'sphuṭāhaḥ
sphuṭadivasagaṇaḥ śailanāgartubhaktaḥ śeṣaścakṣa hataṃ yat punarapi kharasairguṇyaśailādināḍyaḥ ||
idānīṃ maṅgalādīnāṃ nakṣatrabhogārthamahargaṇamucyate- māsā iti karaṇamāsāḥ |
dinarāśinimittaṃ triśadahatāśca te dināni bhavanti |
te dinā vartamānamāsa pratidinena sahitā bhavanti |
tristhā adhorāśeḥ |
śailakhāgaiśceti saptottarasaptaśatairbhāgalabdhaṃ madhyarāśau vimiśram, tato madhyarāśerapi jaladhirasaiścatuḥṣaṣṭibhirbhāgalabdhena hono'sphuṭāha iti |
kvacit pratidinavārādhikena sphuṭārthamekavāro hīno bhavati ; tataḥ sphuṭo'hargaṇo bhavati iti |
idānīṃ maṅgala ucyate- śailavakaraca miśra iti karaṇāpekṣā na siddhāntāpekṣā iti, saptaṣaṣṭhyadhikaśatena miśraḥ śailaveṃkaiśca miśra iti |
sphuṭadivasagaṇaḥ śailanāgartubhakta iti |

ato maṅgalasya parivartanaṃ jñātvā śeṣaścakṣahataḥ saptaviṃśatibhirhataḥ punaḥ śailanāgartubhiḥ hataṃ nakṣatraṃ bhavati |

pāṇopalādikaṃ pūrvoktavidhineti atra sarvagrahāṇāṃ pañcavidhaṃ bhogasthānaṃ bhavati - rāśisthānaṃ nakṣatrasthānaṃ ghaṭikā- sthānaṃ pāṇīpalasthānaṃ śvāsasthānam, adho'dho bhavanti - śvāsasthāneṣu upari śvāsā na tiṣṭhanti ṣaḍbhāgena labdhāḥ pāṇīpaleṣu viśanti; pāṇīpalāni ṣaṣṭhayuddhe na tiṣṭhanti, ṣaṣṭhyābhāgena labdhā ghaṭikā rāśau viśanti |
ghaṭikāpyevaṃ nakṣatrarāśau viśanti; nakṣatrāṇi karmadvaye sāddha trayodaśona tiṣṭhanti |
ṣaḍ rāśayastiṣṭhantīti nimamaḥ |
idānīṃ maṅgalaśuddhirucyate- śodhyāḥ sārddhā navāre yadi ṛṇamadhikaṃ cakramiśre viśodhyaṃ tyājyaṃ cakrārddhamarddhādhikamapi ca bhavet tyaktaśeṣotkramaḥ syāt |

- paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ grahāṇāmastamanodayakāla ucyate- yaḥ kaścit sūryabhogaṃ praviśati niyataṃ sa grahaścāstameti sūryaṃ tyaktoditaḥ syādayanagativibhāgena mārgaśca tasya
vāme mārge sthito yo ravigamanavaśāt sa vrajatyuttareṇa savyastho dakṣiṇena sphuṭamapi ca riporyuddhamanyo'nyamatra || ||
yaḥ kaścit sūryabhogaṃ praviśati niyataṃ sa grahaścāstametīti sūryamaṇḍala raśmibhi-
radṛṣṭatvādastaṃgata ityucyate [b], na grahasya sarvathā'bhāva iti

ayanagativibhāgena mārgaśca tasyeti

vāme mārge sthito yo ravigamanavaśāt sa vrajatyuttareṇeti

paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ ūrdhvaṃ ṣaṇṇāgasaṃkhyā iti
iha saptamabhūvalayād ūrdhvaṃ ṣaḍaśītisahasrayojana- saṃkhyā tapanamapi raveścāyane' cottare 'nte dakṣiṇāyanādau svasvakhaṇḍe karkaṭādau dakṣiṇāyanaṃ svasvasaṃkrāntidine bhavati dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu yathākramamiti; tasmāt kṣīṇaśca savye bhavati narapate rudrasaṃkhyāsahasram
tasmāt dakṣiṇāyanasaṃkrāntidināt savye'yane uttarāyanaṃ (ṇaṃ) yāvat kṣīṇo bhavatyekādaśasahasraṃ pañcasaptatisahasraṃ tapanaṃ sūryasyordhvaṃ veditavyamiti ekāśītisahasraṃ sa (śa) rapañcaśatarahitaṃ madhyato gola rekhā bhavati, yojanamānam
tasyā golarekhāyā yadvad dvipātaṃ bhavati sūryasya tat
khakhaśareṣu iti pañcaśatādhikapañcasahasrāṇi yojanānāmiti, uttare rekhāyā dakṣiṇe ca svasvakhaṇḍe rāśibhogavaśeneti
idānīṃ sūryasya pratidinaṃ tiryakkrāntirdvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu savyottare ucyate- kṣārābdhi laṅghayitvā vrajati dinakaro dakṣiṇe yāvadagni kailāśa ( sa ) syottare ca vrajati himagiriṃ cottare cottarasthaḥ
bāṇāstithayāhatāśca vrajati digayane yojanānāṃ sahasraṃ sārddhākṣā hīndulabdhaṃ tyajati dinadine pañcabhūtaḥ samantāt || 59 ||
kṣārābdhiṃ laṅghayitvā vrajati dinakaro dakṣiṇe yāvadagnimiti
iha dvādaśāra- bhūmyāṃ pratye kaikakhaṇḍe rāśivaśāt kṣārābdhi laṅghayitvā dakṣiṇāyane sthitaḥ sūryo dakṣiṇāgnivalayādikaṃ yāvad vrajati samudrāntasīmna iti
tataḥ sīmno'gnivalayāt kailāśa ( sa ) syottare vrajati himagirim (:), uttare mārge uttarāyana (ṇa) stha: sūryo dakṣiṇāyanā- didinaṃ yāvat
bāṇāstithayāhatāśceti
bāṇa iti pañca, tithiriti pañcadaśa; tābhiḥ pañca daśabhirhatā bāṇāstithayāhatā iti pañcasaptatirbhavati'
taṃ ( i ) pañcasaptatisahasrasaṃkhyāṃ yojanānāṃ vrajati dinakaro digayane himādagni yāvada- gnerhimavantaṃ yāvat pañcasaptatisahasraṃ yojanānāṃ dakṣiṇāyanādidigvibhāgaḥ; taṃ dig- vibhāgaṃ ṣaḍmāsairyathākrameṇa vrajati uttarāyaṇe dakṣiṇāyane ceti
sārddhākṣā hīndulabdhaṃ tyajati dinadine iti
sārddhadvayaśītiśata bhāgalabdhaṃ pañcasaptatisahasrāt pratidinadig- vibhāgaṃ yojanānāṃ tyajati sūryaḥ
evamubhayāyanabhogena rāśicakrasya maṇḍaladināni pañcaṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayasaṃkhyā raverbhavanti
evaṃ dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu tiryakkrāntiḥ sūryasya veditavyeti
idānīṃ pratyekarāśisthitasya sūryasya dvādaśakhaṇḍabhramaṇavaśād dvādaśamāsabheda ucyate- ataḥ bhoṭānusāraṃ 'vṛddhipātaṃ ' ityeva suvacam
vimalaprabhāyāṃ rāśāvake sthito'rkaḥ sakalamṛtugaṇaṃ māsapakṣān karoti khaṇḍe khaṇḍe ca māso bhavati bhuvitale dvādaśāre krameṇa, yanmānaṃ yatra khaṇḍe bhavati dinavaśāt saptame tanniśāyāṃ trailakṣādarddhamārge dinamapi purataḥ pṛṣṭhato'rkasya rātriḥ || 60 ||
rāśāveke sthito'rkaḥ sakalamṛta gaṇaṃ māsapakṣān karotīti
ihaikarāśī yatra kutracit meṣādike sthitaḥ sūryo dvādaśakhaṇḍe ṣaḍ ṛtūn dvādaśamāsān caturviṃśati pakṣān karoti ; rāśicakrabhramaṇavaśena
iha meroḥ sarvadi gbhāge yo rāśicakra bhramaṇavaśād dakṣiṇāgni spṛśati, sa saptamakhaṇḍadikprāpto meruśṛṅga spṛśati himaparvato'ddhvaṃ vrajatīti nyāyāt sarvamṛtugaṇādikaṃ sakalaṃ pratyekaṃ rāśau sthitasyārkasya veditavyamiti
khaṇḍe khaṇḍe ca māso bhavati bhuktile dvādaśāre krameṇeti
iha dvādaśāre bhuvitale pratyekakhaṇḍe pratyekamāso bhavati ekarāśau sthitasya sūryasya yena prakāreṇa tathā kathyate- iha ekarāśinirdeśena sarvarāśayo veditavyā iti
atra laghujambūdvīpe merordakṣiṇe meṣādirāśayaḥ kila prasiddhāḥ, vasantādaya ṛtavaḥ, caitrādayo māsāḥ evaṃ pakṣāḥ sarve iti ; tasmāt tasmin meṣarāśau sthito'rko yathā dvādaśakhaṇḍe sarvamṛtugaṇādikaṃ karoti tathocyate — iha merordakṣiṇakhaṇḍe meṣastho'rko vasanta ṛtudhaiśākhamāsaṃ karoti, agni- koṇārddha' dvitīyakhaṇḍe jyeṣṭhamāsaṃ karoti; evamagnikoṇāt parārddhakhaṇḍe āṣāḍhaṃ karoti, tṛtīye evaṃ caturthe meroḥ pūrvakhaṇḍapūrvavidehe śrāvaṇamāsaṃ karoti, pañcame īśārddhe khaṇḍe bhādrapadaṃ karoti, īśakoṇe aparārddhe ṣaṣṭhe khaṇḍe āśvinaṃ karoti, meroruttare saptame khaṇḍe kārtikaṃ karoti, vāyavyakoṇārddha khaṇḍe aṣṭame mārgaśīrṣaṃ karoti, vāyavyāparārddhe koṇe khaṇḍe navame puṣyaṃ karoti, meroḥ paścime daśame khaṇḍe māghaṃ karoti, naiṛtyakoṇā- parārddha khaṇḍe phālgunaṃ karoti, naiṛtyakoṇāparārddha khaṇḍe caitraṃ karotīti
evaṃ māsadvayena khaṇḍadvaye ṛturbhavati; pratyekakhaṇḍe śuklakṛṣṇapakṣabhedena pakṣadvayaṃ bhavati
evaṃ ṣaḍ ṛtavo dvādaśa māsāḥ caturviṃśati pakṣā dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu cakrākārā bhramanto jñātavyā iti
asmin dakṣiṇakhaṇḍe yo māso dvādaśarāśivaśena bhavati saṃkrāntau so'parasaṃkrāntī dakṣiṇāvarttena naiṛtyakoṇe gacchati, naiṛtyāddha koṇastho'parakoṇārddhaṃ gacchati, apara- koṇārddhastho meroraparagodānyāṃ gacchati, aparagodānyāṃ sthito vāyavyārddhakoṇaṃ gacchati, vāyavyādarddhakoṇastho'parakoṇāddhaṃ gacchati, apara- koṇārddha stha uttarakuru vrajati, uttarakurustha īśānakoṇāddhaṃ gacchati, tatrastho'parakoṇārddhaṃ gacchati, apara- jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ koṇārddha sthaḥ pūrvavidehaṃ gacchati, pūrvavidehastho'gnikoṇārddhaṃ khaṇḍaṃ vrajati, pūrvāddha stho'para- koṇārddhaṃ vrajati, aparakoṇārddhastho dakṣiṇajambūdvīpaṃ vajrati iti dvādaśamāsānāṃ pratisaṃkrāntivaśena dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu saṃcaraṇaṃ jñātavyamiti
yanmānaṃ yatra khaṇḍe bhavati dinavaśāt saptame tanniśāyāṃ bhavati
iha uttarāyaṇa- saṃkrāntī dakṣiṇakhaṇḍe divāmānaṃ kṣīṇaṃ rātrimānaṃ vṛddham, tadevottarakurau saptame bhūmikhaṇḍe ādityodayavaśena dakṣiṇe rātrivṛddhivaśena tanmānaṃ tvatra na bhavati; jambūdvīpe dakṣiṇodayavaśena saptame rātrihānivaśena tato dakṣiṇe bhavati
evaṃ sarvatra pratyekakhaṇḍe veditavyamiti
trailakṣādarddhamārge * dinamapi purataḥ pṛṣṭhato'rkasya rātririti
iha vṛttamānaṃ ṣaṭ- lakṣam; sūryasya gamanādarddha mārge trilakṣaṃ dinamiti
udayāstamanaṃ yāvadastamanādudayaṃ yāvat rātriḥ trilakṣañca
evamahorātraṃ veditavyamiti
| idānīṃ dvādaśakhaṇḍasthānānmerusthitirucyate- sarveṣāñcottarastho bhavati narapate śailarājo janānāṃ pūrva sūryodayaḥ syād girisamamayanaṃ paścime cāstametat
kālāḥ sandhyāścatasra: praharadina niśāḥ sarvadā saṃkramanto merodikṣu bhramanti prakaṭaravimahau bheditā lagnabhedeḥ || 61 ||
sarveṣāñcottarastho bhavati narapate śailarājo nanānāmiti
iha dvādaśakhaṇḍe sthitānāṃ janānāṃ śailarājo meruruttare bhavati
pūrve sūryodayaḥ syāditi sarveṣāṃ meroḥ sarvakhaṇḍasthitānāñca yatra bhānurudayati sā pūrvadig bhavati; dakṣiṇe jambūdvīpasthānāṃ pūrvavidehaṃ pūrvadika; aparago dānyāṃ jambūdvīpaṃ pūrvadiguttarasthānāmaparagodānī pūrvadika; pūrvavidehasthānāmuttarakuruḥ pūrvadika, sūryabhramaṇodayavaśāditi
evaṃ koṇasthiteṣu aṣṭakhaṇḍeṣu jñātavyamiti
pañcapañcadaṇḍāntareṇa pratyekakhaṇḍe sūryodaya iti
girisamamayanamiti pratyekakhaṇḍe girisamaṃ merorabhimukhaṃ sūryagamanamuttare dakṣiṇe ca paścime cāstametat
evaṃ dvādaśakhaṇḍeṣu yathāpūrve udayastathā paścime'stametadveditavyamiti
kālāstrayaḥ - prabhāto madhyāhno vikāla iti
sandhyāścatasraḥ - arddharātra - sandhyā pūrvasandhyā madhyāhnasandhyā vikālasandhyā
praharā aṣṭau (ṣṭa ) - divāyāṃ catvāraḥ, rātrau catvāraḥ
dinaniśaṃ (śā) sarvadā saṃkramantaḥ
evaṃ kālādayo'horātraṃ sarvasmin kāle saṃkramantaḥ khaṇḍāt khaṇḍaṃ merodikṣu bhramanti
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- prakaṭaravimahau dvādaśāramahyāmiti'
bheditā lagnabhederiti
lagnā dvādaśa , teṣāṃ bhedairbheditāḥ kālādayo bhramanti
catuścaturlagnaiḥ kālo bhramati; pratyekapratyeka sandhyā trilagnairbhramati; pratyeka praharaḥ sādardhalagnena bhramati
evaṃ pratyekalagnaṃ pañcadaṇḍairbhramati; ardhapraharaḥ praharārddhena bhramati; praharāddharddhana ra muhūrtta bhramati; ṣaṣṭipāṇīpalairghaṭikā bhramati; pratyekapāṇīpalaṃ ṣaṭśvāsairbhramati
evamahorātraṃ śvāsabhedena lagnabhedena kālo bhramati gacchati dehināmiti
idānīṃ meroḥ sarvadikṣu sūrya bhramaṇamānamucyate- nāḍyabde ṣaṭsapādaṃ ca ( na ?) kramati dinakaro yojanānāṃ sahasraṃ ṣaṣṭhīnāmagnilakṣaṃ bhramati dinaniśaṃ pañcaśailaṃ sahasrama, golārddha khāgni nāḍīrvahati dinaniśaṃ hānivṛddhayośca pañca gole savyāva ( pa ) savyaṃ pratidinamapi tata śvāsayugmaṃ triliptam ||
nāḍyabdhe (bde ) ṣaṭ sapādaṃ kramati dinakaro yojanānāṃ sahasramiti
nāḍyabdhe- (bde) ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśataśvāsasaṃkhyākāle ṣaṭ sapādaṃ pañcāśadviśatādhikaṣaṭsahasra- miti kramati dinakaro yojanānāṃ rāśistho'yamākāśe'to rāśerayaṃ bhramaṇavego na sūryasya rāsyā (śyā)dhāre sthitasyeti atra rāśicakraṃ savyāvartena bhramati, graharāśiṣvava ( pa ) savyena
caranti|
yathā rāśicakraṃ bhramati tathā rāhurapi saṃcarati paścimābhimukho meroḥ pradakṣiṇāṃ kurvan|
grahāḥ pūrvamukhā merora (roḥ) pradakṣiṇāṃ kurvanto rāśicakra saṃkramaṇaṃ kurvanti|
evaṃ paramārthayuktyā rāhuḥ puṇyavān sūryādayo' duṣṭāḥ pāpagrahā ucyante iti|
ṣaṣṭhīnāmagnilakṣamiti ṣaṣṭhīnāṃ nāḍīnāmadhve (bde) kāle ṣaṭśatādhikairṛviśat- sahasraśvāsasaṃkhyākāle|
agnilakṣaṃ trilakṣam, pañcaśailaṃ sahasramiti pañcasaptatisahasra- saṃkhyā yojanānāṃ bhramati; dinaniśamahorātramiti|
golārddha khāgni nāḍīvhati dinaniśamitīha viśu (ṣu) vasaṃkrāntidine pūrvoktagola rekhāyāṃ khāgniḥ triṃśannāḍī raho vahati, triśannāḍī rātriṃ vahati, sūryaḥ samamahorātramityarthaḥ|
hānivṛddhayāśrayaṃ ( hānivṛdhayośca ) pañca gole savyāva ( pa ) savyamiti|
tasmād golārddhāt savyāva ( pa ) - savyamāsatrayeṇa savye rātrervṛddhiḥ pañcanāḍyaḥ, ava ( pa ) savye māsatrayeṇa dinavṛddhi: pañcanāḍyaḥ|
atra rātrivṛddhayā divāhānidivāvṛddhyā rātrehaniḥ jñātavyeti|
pratidinamapi paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddaśaḥ vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- tat śvāsayugmaṃ triliptamiti|
atra savyāva ( pa ) savye trimāsānāṃ pratidinamahorātre vṛddhirhānirvā śvāsadvayaṃ trīṇi pāṇīpalāni|
idaṃ mānaṃ kailāśa ( sa ) khaṇḍe, nāryaviṣayādike|
āryaviṣaye ca pratyahorātraliptā dvayaṃ ( pāṇīpalāni ) vṛddhirvā hānirvā veditavyā; māsatrayeṇa madhyaviśru ( ṣu)vāt triṃśada- horātratulyamānān nāḍītrayaṃ hānirvā vṛddhirvā ayanavaśād veditavyeti|
(70b) idānīṃ kailāśa ( sa ) khaṇḍabhedena dvādaśalagnānāmudayakāla ucyate- liptā syānmeṣalagne gagananavakaraṃ khartunetraṃ vṛṣe syāt yugme'pyākāśakhabde śararasaghaṭikāḥ syuḥ kulīre ca siṃhe|
kanyāyāṃ sārddhaṣaṭkaṃ bhavati khalu tulādyutkrameṇaiva sarvaṃ ṣaḍa lagnarastametaduditamapi tathā'rkaṃnakṣatrabhedaiḥ||
liptā syānmeṣalagne iti|
iha meṣalagne prathamodayādaparalagnodayaṃ yāvat kālo bhavati, gagananavakaraṃ liptāpāṇīpalapiṇḍaṃ navatyadhikaśatadvayam, ataḥ ṣaṣṭibhāgena ghaṭikācatuṣṭayaṃ pañcāśat pāṇīpalāni meṣalagnodayakāla iti kailāśa ( sa ) viṣaye niyamaḥ|
khartunetraṃ vṛṣe syāditi|
evaṃ vṛṣalagnodayakālaḥ ṣaṣṭya ttaraśatadvayaṃ pāṇīpala- piṇḍaṃ catasro nāḍyo viśatipāṇīpalānyudayakālaḥ|
yugme'pyākāśakhabde yugme'pi mithune udayakālaḥ śatadvayaṃ liptāpiṇḍaṃ nāḍyastisraḥ pāṇīpalāni viṃśatiriti|
sa (śa) rarasaghaṭikāḥ syuriti śara (:) pañcaghaṭikā kulīre karka lagne udayakālaḥ|
tataḥ siṃhe rasa iti ṣaṭpaṭikālagnodaye kāla iti kanyāyāṃ sārddhaṣaṭkaṃ kanyālagnodayakālaḥ ṣaṭ ghaṭikā triṃśat pāṇīpalāni sādarddhaṣaṭkaṃ bhavatīti|
tulādyutkrameṇaiva sarvam|
ataḥ ṣaḍlagna kālāvadheraparaṣaḍlagne tulādiṣūtkrameṇaiva- manena vidhinā sarvaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ bhavatīti niyamaḥ|
atra lagnodayakāle tulāyāṃ ṣaṭ ghāṭikā triṃśat pāṇīpalāni vṛścike ṣaḍ, dhanuṣi pañca, makare tisro nāḍyaḥ, pāṇīpalāni viṃśatiriti, kumbhe catasro nāḍyaḥ, viśati pāṇīpalāni; mīne ghaṭikāścatasraḥ, pāṇīpalāni pañcāśaditi kailāsaviṣaye niyamaḥ|
ṣaḍlagnairastametaduditamiti, tathā hi-yasmāt ṣaḍlagnairuditamapi tasmāt astametad bhavatīti|
arkasya nakṣatrabhederiti sārddhatrayodaśanakṣatrānte astametadudayastathā sārddhatrayodaśanakṣatrānte ahorātrabhedena ṣaḍlagnaiḥ sārddhatrayodaśanakṣatrairdivākālo bhavati; ṣaḍlagnaiḥ sārddhatrayodaśanakṣatra rātrikālo bhavati; lagnodayakālavaśena divārātri ( hīnādhiko (T) bhavati, sūryasañcāravaśāditi|
idānīṃ dehe madhyamā varṣaśatadinaśvāsādikamucyate viṃśatyeke sahasra ityādi - viṃśatyeke sahasre rasaśatasahite nirgatā dantabhāgaiḥ dehe śvāsādhikā ye pratidinasamaye bhūtaśailatu saṃkhyā:|
ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ sahasrega ṇitamapi śatābdasya mānaṃ prasiddha śūnyagniprabhaktā prabhavati ghaṭikā ṣaṣṭibhāgād dinaṃ ca||
64 ||
viśatyeke sahastre ayutadvaye sahasrādhike rasaśatasahite ṣaṭśatayukte iti|
nirgatā dantabhāgairato rāśe' dvātriṃśadbhāgairnigatā|
dehe śarīre śvāsādhikā ye pratidina- samaye dvādaśalagnaṃ saṃkrāntikāle bhūtaśalarta saṃkhyā pañcasaptatyadhikaṣaṭrāta saṃkhyā ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ sahasre guṇitamapi śatābdasya mānaṃ prasiddham; te śvāsā varṣaśatadinaiḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ sahasra guṇitā varṣaśataśvāsamānaṃ te bhavanti|
atra pratyahaṃ trināḍīśvāsāḥ ṣoḍaśaśatādhikadvayayutasaṃkhyā varṣaśatādinairguṇitā ṣaṭsaptati' lakṣādhikasaptasaptatikoṭyo bhavanti|
tebhyo dvātriṃśadbhāgena labdhāstricatvāriṃśallakṣādhikakoṭidvayasaṃkhyā madhyamāyā avadhūtyāḥ śvāsā bhavanti; avaśiṣṭāstrayastriṃśallakṣādhikapañcasaptatikoṭyo lalanā- rasanayoḥ pañcamaṇḍalavāhakāḥ samabhāgato'rddhā lalanāvāhakāḥ, arddhā rasanāvāhakā vāmasavyato varṣaśatāvadheriti|
śūnyartvagniprabhaktā prabhavati ghaṭikā|
tataḥ śvāsarāśeḥ ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatena prabhaktā labdhā ghaṭikā rāśirbhavati ṣaṣṭibhāgād dinaṃ ca|
tato ghaṭikārāśeḥ ṣaṣṭibhāgena labdhaṃ dinapiṇḍaṃ bhavati madhyamāyāḥ pañcaviṃśatyadhikaikādaśaśatasaṃkhyā ṣaṭtriṃśatsahasra - bhyo'vaśiṣṭaṃ dina piṇḍaṃ pañcasaptatyadhikāṣṭacatvāriṃśacchatottaramayutatrayaṃ lalanārasanayorvarṣa- śatāvadherjñātavyamiti mucyate- idānīmaṣṭagrahāṇāṃ varṣaśatamānaṃ sārddhacakraprabhogata ekapiṇḍatvena ra kālacakrarahita- cande pakṣe ravau cāyanamapi nava māsāśca bhaume ca rātriḥ ṣaḍa varṣāḥ syurguroḥ syādayanamapi bhṛgoścāyanaṃ candrasuno ( : )|
tithyākhyābdāni śau ro'tamini nava tathā ketuhīnā ca rājan tat sarvaṃ caikapiṇḍaṃ vasuguṇitayugābdāni sārddhatrimāsāḥ||

candre pakṣa ityādi|
candre pakṣaścandrasya pakṣa ekastriśaddaṇḍātmakaṃ rātraṃ bhavati, arddharāśicakropabhogataḥ|

paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ no bhuktā yādarddhanāḍī, rāhuṇā na bhuktā, ṛtudinasamaye catuḥṣaṣṭhidine, sā kalāhīnacandrā, candrasya naṣṭakaletyarthaḥ
sā sūryasyādhikā syāt, sūryasya dvināḍī bhūtvā adhikā pratidinayugaguṇitā 'vedaistithyāhatam' (kā ta ityādinā; yanmāsamadhyadinaṃ taṃ tadevādhikaṃ bhavati
sūryamāse saṃkrāntimāse dinamekamadhikaṃ tadeva madhyamāṅgaṃ veditavyamiti
rāhormāsasya bhogānnayanaviguṇitāditi rāhoḥ' ṛtubhogāt pañcadaśaghaṭikātaḥ pakṣabhogena bhaktāt, pādona caturbhirbhakāllabdhāḥ ketośca nāḍyaḥ catasraḥ pratidinasamaye; punarmāsabhogāt rāhoḥ

sūryabhogāt krameṇa sārddhadvāviṃśallikā bhavantīti karaṇavivakṣā svarūpataḥ

asau sūryabhogaḥ pratyahaṃ catasro ghaṭikā viṃśatipāṇīpalāni siddhānte ṣaṭpāṇīpalānyadhikānīti, tasmin sūryabhoge śodhayet, śodhitāvaśeṣaṃ caraṇaṃ bhavati
