vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ lokadhātu- idānīṃ kālacakratrivarṣadināni pañcaviṃśat tattvaguṇitāni ayanadinahīnāni navasahitāni grahāṇāṃ maṇḍalānyucyante- varṣāhaḥ pañcaguṇyaṃ punariṣunihataṃ cāyanāhaḥ prahīṇaṃ raktandhremi bhavanti svagatiguṇavaśānmaṇḍalāni grahāṇām
bhaume śailāhiṣaṭa kaṃ bhavati dinagaṇaṃ śailarandhrādināgaṃ saumye dantāgnivedaṃ bhavati suragurorbhārgave'gojinākṣī || 80 ||
varṣāha rityādi |
varṣāho'śītyuttaramekaṃ sahasram ta deva pañcaguṇyaṃ punariṣu- riti pañcabhinihataṃ guṇitamityarthaḥ |
evaṃ khaṇḍaguṇitaṃ pañcaviṃśadguṇitaṃ bhavati |
ayanāho- śītyuttaraśatam, tena hīnam, ekāyane grahāṇāṃ bhuktivaśādekāyanadinānyūnī bhavanti;
randhaimaśro bhavati, randhramiti nava, ṣatriguṇāstamiśrāṇi dinamaṇḍalāni grahāṇāṃ svasvagativaśāt pañcānāṃ bhavati , ekapiṇḍata ekonatriṃśadadhikāṣṭaśatottaraṣaṭsahasrā- yutadvayam, tato bhaume śailāhiṣaṭkamiti saptāśītyuttaraṣaṭśataṃ dinagaṇaṃ bhavati |
śailarandhrādināgaṃ saptanavatyadhikaṃ saptaśatāṣṭasahasraṃ saumye bhavati |
dantāgnivedaṃ dvātriṃśa- dadhikatriśatacatuḥsahasraṃ suragurorbhavati |
bhārgave'gojinākṣī saptacatvāriṃśadadhika- dviśatadvisahasraṃ bhavati |
ṣaṭṣaṭśailākṣaraikaṃ bhavati dinagaṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ sūryaṃ sūno śailaveṃkañca bhaume khalu bhavati dhanaṃ candraputre śataghnam |
hīnaṃ vaha nayarkaśaile daśaga ṇitadinaṃ bhārgave'bdhyaṣṭahīnaṃ śūnyākāśarta netraṃ khakaraphaṇiyutaṃ mantriputro ṛṇaṃ syāt || 81 ||
bhaume sārddhā navārddhādhikanṛpati budhe sūryasū kṣaṃ gurau syāt ṣaṭ śukre doṣamaṃ kā vṛṇamiti sakala śodhayed bhuktimadhye |
paścācchuddha śca teṣāṃ bhavati narapate janmanakṣatracārāt svāṅge nīcasya śuddhirbhavati gativaśāduccakasyārkamadhye || 82 ||
ṣaṭṣaṭśelāsva raikam, ṣaṭṣaṣṭhyadhikaṃ saptaśatāyutamekaṃ bhavati dinagaṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ sūryasūnoriti |
śailaveṃkañca bhauma ityādi pūrvoktamiti ṭīkāyāṃ vṛttadvayaṃ yāvaditi |
idānīṃ grahāṇāṃ janmaśuddhayarthaṃ nakṣatrasamuccayamucyate- ṣaṇmāsairmāsabhuktaṃ guṇitamapi bhavet kālacakrābdamāse- rbhāge labdhaṃ ṛṇaṃ syādapi raviśaśinā nānubhuktārddhanāḍī |

paṭale] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ ketorbhayo'dhikaṃ yat pratidinasamaye guṇyamindordinaiśca ṣaṣṭyā bhāgena nāḍī pratidinasamaye śīghravakrodaye ca || 83 ||
ṣaṇmāsairmāsabhuktamiti tadupari śuddhiḥ siddhānte, kintu sattvāśayavaśena karaṇā- pekṣā iti |
ketūdaye punastripakṣadinaiścārapadaṃ guṇitaṃ sārddhadvādaśādhikaśataliptāpiṇḍaṃ bhavati |
tadeva pratidine udayāt ketorbhavati iti kāryaṃ veditavyam |
yārddhanāḍī ṛkṣa- ṛṇasthāne labdheti mātṛmodakavākyaṃ sattvānāṃ pācanāyeti |
idānīṃ grahāṇāṃ cārabalamucyate- saumyo mantrī ca śīghre prabhavati balavān bhaumaketū ca vakre mandaḥ śukraśca mande prakṛtiguṇavaśāt śeṣacāre'balāśca |
śīghre pūrvā mukhāḥ syuḥ punaraparamukhā vakracāre praviṣṭā mande savyānanāḥ syuḥ svagatiguṇavaśānnirgame cottarāsyāḥ ||84 ||
sauma ityādi |
saumyo budhaḥ, mantrī bṛhaspatiḥ śīghraṃ śīghracāre uditaḥ san pūrvārdhe prabhavati balavāniti |
bhaumaśca ketuśca bhaumaketū vakre arddhamaṇḍale parityakte utkramabhogāt balavān bhavati |
mandaḥ śaniḥ śukraśca mandakrameṇāparārdhe balavāniti |
prakṛtiguṇavaśād vānta guṇa vaśāditi |
śeṣacāre cāratraye abalāśca bhaumādaya iti |
śīghre pūrvā mukhāḥ syuḥ, sarvagrahāḥ śīghracāre pūrvārdhe pūrvā mukhāḥ syuḥ ; punaste te aparamukhāḥ paścimamukhāḥ vakracāre praviṣṭāḥ santaḥ, mande krameṇāparārdhe savyānabhā- (nanā) bhavanti |
svagatiguṇavaśānnirgame cotkrameṇāparārdhe uttaramukhā bhavantoti |
etat svarodaya bhūmibalārthaṃ vākyam |
sūryacandrapadānyucyante- māsaistriṃśaddinā ye jalanidhinihatāḥ khākṣicandraṃ yadīndo rāśīnāṃ dvādaśānāṃ yugaguṇitapadaṃ cāṣṭavedaṃ veśca |
tanmadhye hānivṛddhī tvayanagativaśādutkrameṇa krameṇa cakrārddhārddha samastā vasuyugagaṇanā veditavyā narendra ||85||
mārseviśaddinā ye jalanidhinihatāḥ, triṃśaddināścaturbhiguṇitāste, khākṣi- candraṃ yadindoḥ, viṃśatyadhikaśataṃ padaṃ bhavati, candrasya saptadināvadheḥ kalāvṛddhayarthaṃ vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyam |
rāśīnāṃ dvādaśānāṃ caturbhiguṇitānāṃ cāṣṭavedaṃ raveḥ padaṃ ṣaṇmāsāvadherbhavati, tadeva vakṣyamāṇe vaktavyam |
atra saṃgrahamātreṇaiṣāṃ padānāṃ niyamaḥ |

vimalaprabhāyāṃ idānīṃ candramaṇḍale rāhupraveśalakṣaṇamucyate parvaccheda ityādi - parvacchede ca rāhoḥ praviśati śaśino maṇḍale maṇḍalaṃ ca sarvagrāso viśuddho bhavati śaśivaśānmaṇḍalaṃ ṣaṣṭināḍhyaḥ |
ardhagrāso'rddhaśuddhe bhavati śaśivaśānnāḍikā yāvadasti chedo grāso ravīndodinaniśisamaye kṛṣṇaśukle ca pūrve || 86 ||
[lokadhātu- parvacchede, pūrṇāvasāne, amāvāsyāvasāne vā, rāhoḥ praviśati maṇḍale śaśino maṇḍalam ; maṇḍalaśabdenātra ṣaṣṭighaṭikā, ekanakṣatraparibhogaḥ, sa ca dvādaśarāśyātmakaḥ, ṣoḍaśakalātmako veditavyaḥ, sarvagrāsavaśāditi, sarvagrāso viśuddho bhavati |
yadi rāhubhogena candrabhogaḥ pariśuddhastadā sarvagrāsaḥ ṣaṣṭināḍikāparyantam |
ardhagrāso'rddha bhoge śuddhe sati śaśicaraṇavaśānmaṇḍalaṃ ṣaṣṭināḍyoścandrasya ṣoḍaśakalātmakam |
evaṃ śaśi- caraṇavaśānnāḍikā yāvadasti rāhubhoge praviṣṭā tāvadekakalāyāścaturthāṃśaṃ yāvad graha- samāgamo jñeya iti' |
etat siddhānte rāho [78a] va janādikaṃ vistareṇa bāhyajñānārthaṃ veditavyam |
asmin tantre laghuhetuto mañjuśriyā na prakāśitam |
atra yadadhyātmopayogyaṃ tadevoktaṃ saṃkṣepata iti |
evaṃ grahayuddhādikaṃ samastaṃ siddhānte jñātavyam |
atra tantre siddhāntāpekṣā jyotiṣaviṣaye grahayuddhādike; adhyātmani grahayuddhavivakṣā nāstīti bhagavato niyamaḥ |
na bāhye grahaṇe jāte sati sattvānāṃ śukragrahaṇaṃ bhavatyadhyātmani; ravigrahaṇād rajograhaṇaṃ bhavatīti niyamaḥ sarvatra nāsti |
tasmād bauddharbāhyaparijñānārthaṃ brahmasūryayamanakaromaka- siddhāntaṃ (T: ) jñātavyamiti ( jñātavyā iti ) bhagavato niyamaḥ |
idānīṃ sarvagrahāṇāṃ caturyugānte śūnyacaraṇapraveśa ucyate mānuṣyāṇāmityādi- mānuṣyāṇāṃ śatābdaṃ guṇitamapi bhaved varṣabhuktyā grahāṇāṃ dvisthaṃ digbhāgalabdhaṃ bhavati ca tad ṛṇaṃ śodhayenmūni rāśau |
viśatyekaṃ hi lakṣaṃ ṣaḍayutamapi ca ṣaṭvarṣasaṃkhyā yugāddhe bhūyo rāśidvayena prabhavati niyataṃ varṣasaṃkhyā caturṇām ||87||
mānuṣyāṇāṃ śatābdaṃ yat tadguṇitaṃ varṣabhuktyā grahāṇāmiti kālacakreṇa saha grahāṇāṃ sārddhadinena varṣaśatabhuktiḥ, tridinairdviśatam, triṃśaddinairdvisahasram ṣaṣṭyuttaratriṃśaddinaiḥ caturviṃśativarṣasahasra bhuktirbhavati |
tayā varṣaśataṃ guṇitaṃ dvisthaṃ digbhāgandham, daśabhāgena labdham, mūni rāśau ṛṇaṃ śodhanīyaṃ bhavati;
avaśiṣṭaṃ viśatyekaṃ hi lakṣaṃ ṣaḍayutamiti ‍ sragdharāyāḥ 'chedo grāso' ityādikasya antimāṃśasya vyākhyānaṃ saṃskṛte bhoṭānuvāde vāpi na vartate |
ga. mudrādikaṃ |
ga. gha. pustakayoḥ 'ṣaḍyutam' iti nāsti |
paṭale ] jyotirjñānavidhimahoddeśaḥ ekaviṃśatilakṣaṃ ṣaḍayutamapi yad varṣasaṃkhyā yugā bhavati;
yugārddhāntāt pañca padāni - kṛd- yugasya catvāri padāni tretāyāḥ padamekamiti, yugārddhamākāśavāyutejaudakapṛthvīmaṇḍalā- tmakam, yathākramaṃ bharturvāmanāḍyāṃ' prāṇasañcāra iti |
bhūyo rāśidvayena punadvatīyarāśinā pañcapadātmakena |
tatra tretāyāḥ padadvayam, dvāparasya padadvayam, kalerekapadam, pṛthivyaptejo-
vāyuśūnyātmakam; yathānu[78b]krameṇa dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ bhartuḥ prāṇasañcāraḥ pañcamaṇḍalātmaka iti
evamubhayanāḍī maṇḍalavarṣa rāśidvayena varṣasaṃkhyā caturṇāṃ yugānāṃ dvayayutādhika- tricatvāriṃśallakṣāṇi manuṣyavarṣāṇāmiti;

śūnyaṃ śūnyaṃ khanāgāḥ karamuniśaśinaḥ kṛyugasya pramāṇaṃ tretāyāṃ khaṃ khaśūnyaṃ rasanavadinakṛd varṣasaṃkhyā prasiddhā
śūnyaṃ śūnyaṃ khavedaṃ rasabhujagamiti dvāpare'bdāni samyak śūnyākāśaṃ khanetraṃ guṇajalanidhayo varṣasaṃkhyā kalau syāt || 88||
śūnyaṃ śūnyaṃ khanāgāḥ karamuniśaśina iti aṣṭāviṃśatisahasrādhikasaptadaśalakṣāṇi varṣāṇāṃ kṛdayugasya pramāṇam, caturmaṇḍalapadopabhogata iti

ācārya - khes - ḍuba - je mahābhāgaiḥ svakṛtāyāṃ vidyāsāgarābhidhāyāṃ (Rig Pahi rGya mTso, pṛ vimalapra bhāṭīkāyāṃ navatisaṃkhyaka ( śloko'yaṃ mūla- kālacakratantrasyaiveti svamataṃ prakāśitam
tairidaṃ sthāpitaṃ yad yadyapi bhoṭadeśīyānu- vādakena locavā - śoṅa - ton mahāśayena mūlakālacakratantrasyānuvādapra saṅga asya ślokasyānuvādo na kṛtaḥ, svabhoṭānuvāde asyānuvādo na prastutaḥ, tathāpi tasya idaṃ kāryaṃ spaṣṭatayā skhalita rūpameva
idaṃ pratīyate yadanuvādārthaṃ tena yā saṃskṛta- pāṇḍulipiradhigatā, sā tathāvidhaiva etacchlokarahitā evaṃ truṭipūrṇā ca āsīt
vihāyaināṃ prathamapaṭalasya kārikāsaṃkhyā nyūnā bhaviṣyati

pas paṭale ] svarodaya yantra vidhiniyamamahodda eśaḥ bhadrā saumye dvitīyo nṛpati (mriyate ) * yadi maghāyoga evātra vāre aśleṣākṣī (kṣaṃ) jayāyāṃ yadi bhavati gururyāti mṛtyuṃ tṛtīyaḥ
riktātithyāṃ dhaniṣṭhā yadi bhavati bhṛguryāti mṛtyuṃ caturthaḥ pūrṇā mandena sārddhaṃ prabhavati bharaṇī pañcamo yāti mṛtyum || 96 ||
evaṃ bhadrā saumye yadi maghānakṣatraṃ bhavati, tadā dvitīya ikārasvaranāma striyati evaṃ jayāyāṃ bṛhaspatau yadi aśleṣā bhavati, tadā tṛtīya ṛkāra- svaranāmā mriyate evaṃ riktāyāṃ śukro yadi bhavati dhaniṣṭhā, tadā caturtha ukārasvara- nāmā mriyate tathā pūrṇā śanau yadi bharaṇī [pra]bhavati, tadā pañcama lṛkārasvara-

idānīṃ phaṇicakramucyate aśvinyetyādi- aśvinyādyaiḥ saptaviṃśatinakṣatraistrināḍyāmahirapi racito revatī nakṣatraṃ yāvadeva pāpenduṃ pāpagrahaśca induśca pāpendu'; rogiṇo yodhasya vā yajjanmanakṣatraṃ yadi bhavati nṛṇāmekanāḍyām, tadā vināśo matyurbhavatīti trināḍī phaṇicakraniyamaḥ
kṛttikādi- naikāntarita ' trināḍyā tadvadādrādi ' '
nāḍi nakṣatrāntarite trināḍyāṃ candre gate yaḥ prathamaṃ yuddhabhūmi viśati , sa vijayī bhavati nāḍībāhyeṣu trinakṣatre candre sthite yaḥ paścāt praviśati, sa vijayī bhavatīti tṛtīya phaṇicakraniyamaḥ

vimalaprabhāyāṃ idānīṃ pratītyasamutpādamu ( u ) cyate- puṣye* māse tvavidyā makaragataravau [ lokadhātu- kumbhasūrye ca māghe saṃskāro mīnasūrye bhavati narapate phālgune meṣasūrye
caitravaiśākhajyeṣṭhe vijñānaṃ nāmarūpaṃ vṛṣamithunagate netrādyaṃ karkaṭe'rke bhavati hariravau sparśanaṃ vedanā ca || 114 ||
āṣāḍhe śrāvaṇe yoṣidapi tulagate māgha (bhādra) māse'śvine ca tṛṣṇopādānameva prabhavati ca bhavaḥ kārtike vṛścike ca
jātiścāpastu (stha) sūrye maraṇamapi tathā mārgaśīrṣe krameṇa evaṃ sūryendubhederubhayagativaśād dvādaśāṅgāni rājan || 115 ||
puṣye māghe'bhisandhau makaragataravau tatra vāre tvavidyā tasmānmṛtyudvitīye bhavati narapate jātirevaṃ tṛtīye
evaṃ sarvaṃ bhavādyaṃ kramagatiguṇitaṃ dvādaśāṅgāni yāvat tasmādvahrayabdhibāṇaṃ maraṇa (makara) gativaśāt ṣaṣṭhamāsaṃ (ṣaḍaṅga) kadācit || 116 ||
pakṣāstithayākhyavāre ravicaraṇavaśāt ṣoḍaśāṅgaḥ kadācit saṃskāro māghasandhau kalaśagataravau tatra saṃskāra eva
vijñānaṃ tadddvitīye prabhavati divase nāmarūpaṃ tṛtīye evaṃ māsadvayāṅgaṃ bhavati ravivaśāt sṛṣṭisaṃhārayogāt || 117 ||
atrāvidyāṃśa viṣamamapi bhavecchobhanaṃ sarvakārye saṃskārādya samaṃ yat tvaśubhamapi yadā sekayātrāvivāhe
evaṃ pakṣaprabhedaiḥ śaśigamanavaśād dvādaśāṅgāni yāni śukle kṛṣṇe ca pakṣe prathamatithivaśāt sṛṣṭisaṃhārayogāt || 118 ||
svarodayayantra vidhiniyamamahoddeśaḥ [143a] ' puṣyetyādi
puṣye māse tu puṣyanirgame māghapraveśadine avidyā
atra
dvādaśāraḥ [raṃ ] rāśicakraṃ makarādikam
tatra thapari [ prathame āre] makara iti dine gataravau kumbhasūrye ca māghe, cakārādatraiva māghānte phālguṇa (na) praveśadine saṃskāraḥ, evaṃ phālguṇā (nā) nte monacaitrādidine vijñānam, tathā caitrānte vaiśākhādidine meṣe nāmarūpam, evaṃ vaiśākhānte jyeṣṭhādivṛṣabhe dine ṣaḍāyatanam, tathā jyeṣṭhānte āṣāḍhādimithunasaṃkrānti - dine sparśaḥ
evamāṣāḍhānte śrāvaṇādikarkaṭa saṃkrāntidine vedanā
evaṃ śrāvaṇānte bhādra- padādisiṃhasaṃkrāntidine tṛṣṇā, tathā bhādrapadānte'śvinyādikanyāsaṃkrāntidine upādānam, evamaśvinyante kāttikāditulāsaṃkrāntidine bhavaḥ, tathā kārtikānte mārgaśīrṣādivṛścika- saṃkrāntidine jātiḥ, tathā mārgaśīrṣānte puṣyādidhanusaṃkrāntidine jarāmara [143b]ṇamiti dvādaśāreṣu yathāsaṃkhyaṃ jñeyāni dvādaśāṅgāni
trisūryabhedaḥ ' candrabhedaḥ sarvatra prasiddhaḥ, puṣyapratipadādinā
ubhayagativaśāditi saṃkrāntivaśāt pakṣatithivaśāditi dvādaśāṅgāni
puṣye māghe'bhisandhau makaragataravau tatra vāre tvavidyā tasmād dinā [d] dvitīye jña dine jarāmaṇam tṛtīya vāre jātiḥ, caturthe bhavaḥ, pañcame upādānam, vedanā, aṣṭame sparśaḥ, navame ṣaḍāyatanam, daśame nāmarūpam, ekadaśame vijñānam, dvādaśame saṃskāra iti
yathā tṛtīye vāre tathā trayodaśame jātiḥ, caturdaśame bhavaḥ, pañcadaśame vāre upādānam
kadācit ṣoḍaśame vāre tṛṣṇeti sūryagatibhedenāṅgāni kvacit saṃkrāntipakṣe ṣoḍaśavāro bhavatīti dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ makarasamaṃ lagnavaśāditi
evaṃ māghaphālgunya- [144a ] bhisandhau kumbhagataravau tatra vāre saṃskāraḥ, dvitīye vijñānam, tṛtīye nāmarūpam, caturthe ṣaḍāyatanam, pañcame sparśa, ṣaṣṭhe vedanā, saptame tṛṣṇā, aṣṭame upā- dānam, navame bhavaḥ, daśame jātiḥ, ekādaśame jarāmaraṇam, dvādaśame'vidyā iti vāma- nāḍī kumbhaviṣayalagnavaśena sṛṣṭikrameṇa
atra yathā tṛtīye tathā trayodaśame nāmarūpam, pañcadaśame sparśaḥ, kvacit ṣoḍaśame vedaneti
evaṃ sūryacandravāraḥ sarvatrasamaviṣamabhedena jñeya ubhayapakṣe saṃkrāntau vārabhedeneti
vimalaprabhāryā [lokadhātu- atrāvidyāṃśaṃ viṣamaṃ sarvasaumyakāryeṣu śobhanaṃ avidyā, vijñānam, ṣaḍāyatanam, vedanopādānajātiriti viṣamāṃśaṃ saṃskārādyaṃ samaṃ yat saumyakārye'śubhaṃ saṃskāro nāmarūpa- sparśa - tṛṣṇā-bhava jarāmaraṇamiti samaṃ viṣamaṃ krūrakarmaṇi praśasyate
evaṃ śuklapakṣe puṣye māse pratipadi avidyā dvitīyādyāṃ (yāṃ) saṃskāraḥ ṣaṣṭi- (sṛṣṭi) 'bhedena kṛṣṇapadi [ prati ] padi avidyā, dvitīyāyāṃ jarāmaraṇamityupasaṃhārakrameṇa (dvāda) śa[ 144b]ti [ thi] ṣu dvādaśāṅgāni yathā puṣye māse śuklakṛṣṇapakṣabhedenā- vidyātya (dya ) ṅgāni tathā māghe śuklakṛṣṇapakṣabhedena saṃskārādīni sṛṣṭisaṃhārabhedenāva- gantīti (ntavyānīti), evaṃ phālgunādike śuklakṛṣṇapakṣeṣu vijñāne yāni (vijñānādīni ) pratyekaṃ dvādaśāṅgāni bhavanti
atraiva yathā tyai ( laukika vyavahāro jyotiṣajñāne'bhijñārahito jñeya iti pratītyasamutpādaniyamaḥ tena vistaro nokta iti
idānīṃ bhūmibalamucyate- sūryārau ketumandau ripunidhanakarau saṃsthitaḥ savyapṛṣṭhe vāme'gre saumyarūpaṃ grahagaṇasakalaṃ yuddhabhūmyāṃ tathaiva
yoginyo viṣṭirudrāśca pavanasahitāḥ saukhyadāḥ savyapṛṣṭhe vāme'gre mṛtyurūpā samaviṣamagatā dve bale ghātayanti || 119 ||
sūryetyādi
atra bhūmivala (ya) maṣṭadiśātmakaṃ jñātvā rāśicakre grahana (kṣa) tra (cakra) bhūmyāmaṣṭadiśāsu yatra sūryārau ketumandau ripunidhanakarau saṃsthitāḥ sarve savye dakṣiṇapṛṣṭhe vā śatrūṇāmabhimukho vāmataḥ sthitā maraṇaṃ kurvantīti
vāme'gre saumyarūpaṃ grahagaṇasakalaṃ yuddhabhūmyāṃ tathaiva ripunidhanakaraṃ (:) candro budhaḥ śukro bṛhaspati- riti saumyarūpaṃ grahagaṇaṃ śatrūṇāṃ dakṣiṇe pṛṣṭhataḥ sthitaṃ ripunidhanakara iti niyamaḥ
evaṃ vakṣa māṇe yo 'ginyo viṣṭirudrāśca pavanasahitāḥ saukhyadāḥ savyapṛṣṭhe ripūṇāṃ vāme'gre sthitā nidhanakarā bhavantīti
samaviṣamagatā arddhakūrā ripūṇāṃ savye pṛṣṭhe arddhakurā na bhavanti; arddhasaumyā vāme'gre bhavanti, arddha- saumyā na bhavanti; tadā dve bale' ghātayanti, ubhayavalānāṃ maraṇaṃ bhavatīti niyamaḥ
paṭale ] svarodayayantravidhiniyama mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ brahmāṇyādiyoginībalamucyate- 139 brāhmī raudrī kumārī khagapatigamanā śūkarī vajrahastā cāmuṇḍā caiva lakṣmīrbhavati vasutithāvāditithyāṃ navamyām
śakre yakṣe'gnikoṇe danuyamavaruṇe mārute ceśabhūmau kurvantyatrodayaṃ tāḥ punarapi tithiṣu dvayaṣṭabhedaivibhinnāḥ ||120||
brāhmītyādi
atra śuklapakṣe kṛṣṇapakṣe vā pratipadi brahmāṇī śakre, dvitīyāyāṃ pa (ya) kṣe' raudrī, tṛtīyāyāmagnikoṇe kaumārī, cartuthyāṃ naiṛtyai vaiṣṇavī, pañcamyāṃ yāmyāṃ vārāhī, ṣaṣṭhyāṃ vāruṇe (jye) aindrī, saptamyāṃ vāyavyau (vye) cāmuṇḍā, aṣṭamyām aiśānye mahālakṣmīriti vasutithau aṣṭatithī, ādi pratipadādau navamyāmiti
punarnavamyāṃ brāhmī śaka e, daśabhyāṃ raudrī pa (ya) kṣe, ekādaśyāṃ kaumāryagnau dvādaśyāṃ vaiṣṇavī naiṛtye, trayodaśyāṃ vārāhī yāmye, catuddaśyāṃ aindrī vāruṇye, pañcadaśyāṃ pūrṇimāyāmamāvasyāyāñca arddhatithau cāmuṇḍā arddhatithau mahālakṣmīriti nyāyaḥ
evamāsvaṣṭadiśāsūdayaṃ kurvanti, tāḥ yoginyaḥ
punarapi pratipadādi ' tithiṣu pūrvārddha aṣṭabhedāḥ, aparārddha aṣṭabhedāḥ, evaṃ dvayaṣṭabhedeṣūdayanti tāsu tithisu jñeyāḥ, tāsāṃ svasvatithau prathamāṣṭamāṃśo bhogaḥ ' tithyā- dhipatyāḥ, paścāt yathoktakrameṇa saptabhogāḥ saptānāmiti nyāyaḥ
evamaparāddhe'pi tithau jñeyamiti yoginīcakrodayaniyamaḥ

svarodayayantravidhiniyamamahodda eśaḥ idānīṃ duṣṭaśatrudurga vidhvaṃsanārthaṃ yantrāṇyucyante saṃgrāma ityādi:- saṃgrāme bhagnaśatruḥ praviśati sahasā koṭṭamadhye kadācit kṛtvā yantrāṇi bāhya hyanavarataśilāvahnivāṇaprapātaiḥ |
khaḍgādyucchedayantreḥ kṣititalanilaye pañjaraiḥ śṛṅgabhede- tadurgaṃ cūrṇayitvā katipayadivasaiḥ sādhanīyaḥ sa duṣṭaḥ || 128 ||
saṃgrāme bhagnaśatruḥ praviśati sahasā koṭṭamadhye kadācit, tadā kṛtvā yantrāṇi bāhye, tairyatrairmukairanavarata śilā [pātairvaṃ ]hnivāṇaprapāteḥ khaṅgādyucchedayantraiḥ svagṛhe rakṣāṃ kṛtvā, kṣititalanilaye vajrapañjaraiḥ śṛṅgabhedaiśca taddurgaṃ cūrṇayitvā katipayadivasaiḥ sādhanīyaḥ sa duṣṭa iti nyāyaḥ
atra sthalaprākārakoṭṭe pāṣāṇayantra 'lakṣaṇamucyate catvāra ityādi- catvāro dvayaṣṭahastāḥ samaviṣamapaderichadritāṣṭapradeśā- sstambhā bhinnārgaṃlābhirjaladhiyugayugābhiśca pṛṣṭhakayā ca
mūle yantrasya mānaṃ bhavati daśakaraṃ mūni bhāge tadarddha yaṣṭistadvayonā prabhavati kaṇayo mūrddhina bhāgā diśāśca || 129 ||
catvāraḥ stambhā dvayaṣṭahastā iti ṣoḍaśa hastāścaturasrāḥ vistāreṇa ṣoḍaśā- gulā uttamayantrasya, madhyamasya stambhāścaturdaśa hastā vistāreṇa caturddaśāṅgulāḥ, adhamasya stambhā dvādaśa hastā vistāreṇa dvādaśāṅgulāḥ ; te ca cchidritāḥ aṣṭapradeśaḥ samaviṣamapadaiḥ; sama: pūrvāpara koṭiḥ, viṣama (:) savyetarakoṭiḥ, tasyāmaṣṭa' sthāneṣu cchidritāḥ, cchidraṃ vistāra tribhāgikam
te ca bhinnārgalābhiḥ pūrve catasṛbhiḥ, dakṣiṇe ca catasṛbhirvāme ca catasṛbhiḥ, pṛṣṭhe'dhobhāge ekayeti pṛṣṭhastambhayoḥ pañca cchidrāṇyeva, pūrva- stambhayoraṣṭa iti ; yantrasya mānaṃ mūle daśahastam, urdhvaṃ pañcahastaṃ yaṣṭirhastadvayenona- stambhānāmiti
kaṇayamānaṃ ṣaṭhastaṃ kaṇayasya vāmadakṣiṇe gopucchākāraḥ

5 146 vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- 5 dvau dvau garbhāntarāle skhalitamapi bhujairmudritaṃ vā samantāt pṛṣṭhe ṣaṭkāṇḍadhārā ubhayabhujasamā cāgrato yantravāhāḥ
kūpastambhainibaddheścaladanilapaṭairacālitaṃ cānilena tatrārūḍhaṃ svasainyaṃ vrajati jalanidhī toyadurgakṣayārtham || 135 ||
atra bāhye caturbhujāsu pratyekagarbhe pūrvāpare bhuje pada 'dvaye kāye vāmadakṣiṇe [ ṣaṇṇāṃ madhye ] āsu catasṛsu bhujāsu stambhakāṣṭheṣu cchidrabhujācchidrebhyo nirgateṣu bhujā- vistāratribhāgikeṣu hastatrayaphalakena ubhayakoṭicchidriteṣu na sarvagarbhān anyo'nyaṃ niyantrayet garbhakoṇacatuṣṭaye
śeṣaprakāśasthāneṣu kītivālān vāhyediti, ūrdhve mudritaṃ vā pūrvāparaṃ samantāt kītivālapravāhasthānāni varjayitvā
sarvayantrasya pṛṣṭha ṣaṭkāṇḍadhārāḥ ; evaṃ dvādaśa vi śatiḥ śataparyantāḥ, ubhayabhujā samāścāgratāḥ ; teṣāṃ kāṇḍadhārāṇāṃ yantravāhāḥ sahasraparyantā bhavanti yantrānurūpata iti
atha vā tān akulaṃ jñātvā kūpastambhainibaddha ścaladanilapaṭervāta paṭaiścālayantaṃ vātena tad yantram, tatrārūḍhaṃ svasainyaṃ vrajati jalanidhau toyadurgakṣayārtham, dvīpāntaragrahaṇārthamiti nyāyo duṣṭānāṃ damanārthamiti jalayantralakṣaṇam
idānīṃ giridurgakṣayārthamagnitailamokṣaṇāya vātayantramucyate- eke pīṭhesbdhiko caladanilapaṭa : sadhvajastambhabandhaḥ pakṣe pṛṣṭhe manuṣyai skhalitamapi mahau rajjunā mūrdhni gacchat

vātenoddhūyamāno vrajati nabhasi vai śailadurgasya mūrdhni tasmāt muktāgnitela dahati ripubalaṃ sarvadurgaṃ samantāt

ādiśabdena cāmaravitānaghaṇṭādaya iti yodhabhūmicihna
ṃ bhūmyormadhye caturdikṣu prakāśo yuddhārthamiti
sārddhakadvitri (vi) bhāgāḥ śaśikaraśikhino mūlapīṭhārgalānāṃ vṛttā sārddhaṃ kabhāgaiḥ (ge) kamalarathamahī nandibhedaiḥ samā syāt
sādhāraṃ cakramānaṃ turagagajavaśāt sārddha hastaṃ dvidhā ca devānāṃ padmajātiḥ surasutavihito nandijātirnarāṇām || 144||
evaṃ dvau dvau (sārddhakadvi) vibhāgā iti mūlapīṭhasyādha eko vibhāgo yodhabhūmyām, ardhavirā (bhā)gaḥ prakāśe cihnāvetau dvau vibhāgāviti
śaśikaraśikhina iti stambhā - rgalānāṃ mūlapīṭhopari caturaṅa gulānām eko vibhāgo dhurāsthāne; tasmāt stambhānāṃ dvau vibhāgau caturaṅa gulānāṃ yodhā yuddhasthāne; tatsthānāṃ ( tataḥ stambhānāṃ' ) tribhāgā upari caturaṅgulānām, tadupari cihnabhūmirdhvajādīnāmiti
vṛttā sārddhakabhāgaiḥ (ge) pīṭhā- u ( du ) paripūrvoktāḥ sārddhabhāge bhūmivṛttāḥ kamalarathasya bhavati; nandibhedairiti nandirathabhede caturasrā mahī bhavati
evamuktakrameṇa sādhāraṃ cakramānaṃ turagarathasya sārddhahastaṃ bhavati, turagarathasya mānāt dvidhāmānaṃ gajarathasya bhavati, cakrapīṭhārgala- cihnādīnāmiti niyamaḥ
atra devānāṃ padmajātirvṛttaratho bhavati
surasutavihita iti arjunasya vitānaṃ nandijātiścaturasraḥ, narāṇāmanyathāpi caturasro ratho vihitaḥ kṣatriyāṇāmiti
aṣṭāmbhodhyakṣa cakre strividha iha mahāsyandano'rdhaḥ samaśca divyo'rkasyaikacakro viṣamahaya rathaścāsurāṇāṃ vināśaḥ
yuddha vevattikaḥ syād viṣamasamamahāsyandanaḥ kṣatriyāṇāṃ bhagno sammukho vai samaviṣamaraṇe pṛṣṭhabhaṅgī samaḥ syāt || 145 ||

idānīṃ devānāṃ vimāne vā nṛpāṇāṃ yātrācalagṛhalakṣaṇamucyate- ṣaṭpañcābdhyagnibhāgaiḥ prabhavati ca samā cchidritā stambhakoṭi- lādūrdhvāddha bhāgo bhavati puravaśāccāmarāṇāṃ vimāne
evaṃ bhūmau nṛpāṇāṃ trividhapuravaśād deśayātrotsaveṣu hastāddha sthāntareṇa prabhavati hi mahābhrāmarī sarvadikṣu || 146 ||
ataḥ tataḥ paṭale ] svarodayayantravidhiniyamamahoddeśaḥ ṣaḍityādi
atra viśaddhastāḥ catvārastambhā viṃśatyaṅgulavistārāḥ ; eṣāṃ catuḥ koṭicchidritāḥ samā bhavanti - ekā cchidrakoṭiḥ ṣaṭ hastavibhāge dvitīyā pañcahasta- vibhāge; tatra tṛtīyā caturhastavibhāge bhavati, [caturthī' trihastavibhāge ], adha ūrdhve hastaikaikaṃ varjayitvā cchidrakoṭirabhimukhā bhavati
mūla [bhāse (ge) bhūmyāṃ trayodaśa- hasto bhavati mūla bhāsā (gā ) dūdha ardhabhāgo bhūmyāṃ saptārddhahasto bhavati; pratyeka- puravaśāt purabhūmyāṃ bhavati, catuśca (tu) rā lopari bhūmirbhavati gaṇavimāne
evaṃ bhūmau nṛpāṇāṃ trividhapuravaśād deśayātroccha (tsa) veṣu prathamapure stambhabāhyacchidre bāhya- nirgatārgalopari phalakaiḥ sarvatra bhrāmarī trīṇi hastā bhrāmarī dvitīyapure ekahastād (rdha) - bhrāmarī sarvadikṣi(vi) ti devavimānaprasthāna gṛhalakṣaṇanigamaḥ
idānīṃ ca (va) santotsavāya cakradolālakṣaṇamucyate- cakro stambhamūni trividhapuravaśād dolakāḥ sarvadikṣu dvaṣṭāvaṣṭī tato'rddhādigatuyugabhujādhāra evaṃ tribhūmau
ūrdhve'dho bhrāmyamāne bhujadhṛtakaṇaye yoṣitāmāsanāni antarbāhya niviṣṭānyadha uparigatānyaṣṭadikcālitāni ||
|| cakrordhvaṃ ityādi
atra pūrvo (rva) ratha ca (va) daṣṭacakrāṇi mūlapīṭhañca tadupari hastirathastambhāyātmana ( yāmena )ścatvāraḥ stambhāḥ, vimānaca (vat) trividhapurātmakā dolā bhavanti, catuścaturgalopari dvayaṣṭāvaṣṭau tato dvāviṃśati (ardha iti) prathamapure caturdikṣu ṣoḍaśa da (do) lakā bhavanti, dvitīye aṣṭau tṛtīye catvāraḥ
dikūdaśabhujairargalo- pari sthitaiḥ savyāva ( pa ) savyaiḥ pūrvāparasthai: dolakā prathamapure; dvitīye ṛtubhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ bhavati, tṛtīye catubhiriti dolakānāṃ bhujā ārā ( ādhārā) stribhamyāmiti dvayordvayobhujayorekaikakaṇayo cchidrādhāre praviṣṭā
ekaikakaṇaye catuścaturāsanāni yoṣitāṃ bhavanti, antarbāhya niviṣṭāni adha uparigatānyaṣṭadikcālitānīti cakradolālakṣaṇa- niyamaḥ
idānīṃ vāṭikādisiñcanārthaṃ jalayantralakṣaṇamucyate ūrdhvaṃ ityādi- ūrdhvādho vakramānaṃ bhavati ca nalikākārayantraṃ samantāt nālāgre kumbhamukhaṃ śu (su) ṣiramapi yadā nāḍirandhrapramāṇam
toyākṛṣṭi karoti skhalitamapi jalaṃ mokṣitaṃ kumbharandhrā udyāne vāṭikāyāṃ vrajati samamahau nīyate yatra tatra ||

Phyed de Ses pa ni ( ardha iti ) | ya paṭale ] vimalaprabhāyāṃ [lokadhātu- ūrdhva'dho vāmadakṣiṇe yantraṃ ca (va) kranālākārau (raṃ), madhye sacchidraṃ madhyaṃ caturhastaṃ vā mūlayantram, tasya nālāgre kumbhamukhaṃ grīvā nālāgre praveśa ( śya) mānā adhaḥ kumbhe cchidraṃ nālirandhrapramāṇaṃ yantrapṛṣṭhanālāgre aparanalikā jalamadhye praviṣṭā karttavyā; tataḥ kumbhacchidraṃ jalagati (ta) nālikācchidram agrato vā nālikācchidraṃ mudrayet; tato mūla- yantracchidreṇa jalaṃ praveśayet yāvajjalapūrṇaṃ yantraṃ bhavati
tato [ jalamadhyabindu- (cchidraṃ) kumbhacchidraṃ vā nālikācchidraṃ yaugapadyena kumbhaṃ toyākṛṣṭa śarataḥ (staḥ ) karoti, tadeva jalamudyāne vāṭikāyāṃ vrajati samabhūmyāṃ noyate yatra tatra vrajatīti jalaśe (ṣe) kayantra- lakṣaṇaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ mañjuśriyaḥ sūryarathaniyamāmantrana (ṇa) mucyate duṣṭānāmiti - duṣṭānāṃ sādhanārthaṃ pravarabhuvi tale dhārmikānāṃ jayārtha pūrvoktaṃ cādibuddhe tribhuvanaguruṇā yat sucandrasya sarvam
tanmadhye kiñcidatra sphuṭamiha viṣaye deśitaṃ te mayādya svasthāne rakṣaṇārthaṃ kuru sakalamidaṃ dveṣalobhairna sūryaḥ ||
|| iha duṣṭānāṃ sādhanārthaṃ pūrvoktamādibuddhe yat tribhuvanaguruṇā śākyasiṃhena sucandrasya vajrapāṇinirmitakāyasya sarvasvarodayayantralakṣaṇam, tanmadhye, tasya svarodaya- yantrādhyāyasya madhye, kiñcidatra sphuṭamiha viṣaye sambhalākhye deśitam, te sūryarathasya mayā mañjuśriyā yaśonarendreṇa, adya svasthāne rakṣaṇārthaṃ kuru sakalamidaṃ dveṣalobhairna sūrya iti sūryarathasya niyamo yathā tathānyeṣāmapi kālacakraparijñānāni nāma yamapi niyamo bhagavataḥ
iti śrīmūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ svarodayayantravidhiniyamamahoddeśaḥ ' daśamaḥ ||||
( mlecchadharmotpāṭanabuddhadharma pratiṣṭhāpanamevādi idānīṃ mahācakravattino mlecchadharmotpāṭanabuddhadharmapratiṣṭhāpanamevādi subodhamiti tenoktaṃ ( tena noktaṃ ) ṭīkāyāṃ lokadhāturnāma prathamapaṭala : *- madyakṣīrābdhimadhye** munimahivalaye saṃsthitāṃ karmabhūmi lakṣe yojane ca bhramati narapati: sūryakhaṇḍān krameṇa
svarodayayantravidhiniyamo mahoddeśa iti
* ataḥ paraṃ ṭīkā nāsti; ka pustake mūlamātramasti; ata eva mūlamātraṃ prastūyate
** ataḥ paraṃ mūlastha bhoṭānuvādasya 'pṛṣṭhasaṃkhyāsaṅketaḥ' 'mārjina' sthāne prastutaḥ
koṣṭhake pāṇḍulipipṛṣṭhasaṃkhyāsaṃketaḥ ka pustakāt pradattaḥ
paṭale] mlecchadharmotpāṭana buddhadharmapratiṣṭhāpanamevādi khaṇḍaikaṃ yojanānāṃ vasudalasahitaṃ pañcaviśatsahasraṃ kailāśastasya madhye vara himagiriṇā'veṣṭitaḥ sarvadikṣu || 150 ||
bhūmau kailāśaṃ khaṇḍaṃ himagirisahitaṃ tattribhāgaṃ samantāt bāhya caikaikapatraṃ dinakaraviṣayairbhūṣitaṃ dvīpadeśaiḥ
savyārddha sambhalākhye munivaranilayaṃ grāmakoṭyādhivāsaṃ koṭigrāmairnirbaddho bhavati hi viṣayo maṇḍalaṃ grāmalakṣaiḥ || 151 ||
ekadvitrayabdhibhedaiḥ śararasavijaneḥ pañca lokādibheda- bhinnaścakrī narendro bhramati bhuvi tale'nveṣaṇīyaḥ sa vijñaiḥ
cakrī cārḍī ca khaṇḍī viṣayanarapati: mudrahastastato'nyaḥ bāhye kālasya bhedaḥ khaluḥ bhavati yathā lokanāthasya tadvat || 152 ||
saptābdhyadrivārā munaya iha tathā sātvikā divyayoni- matsyaḥ kūrmo varāho naraharisahitā vāmano yāmadagniḥ
rāmaḥ kṛṣṇastathāṣṭau danukulabhayadā rājasā bhūtayoni- dvātriṃśad viṣṭarānte bhavati danuripuścakrapāṇiḥ śatāyuḥ || 153 ||
ādro'nogho varāhī danubhujagakule tāmasānye'pi pañca mleccho'sau śvetavastrī madhumatimathanī yo'ṣṭamaḥ so'ndhakaḥ syāt
sambhūtiḥ saptamasya sphuṭamakhaviṣaye vāgadādau nagaya yasyāṃ loko'surāṃśo nivasati balavān nirdayo mlecchamūrtiḥ || 154 ||
uṣṭrāśvau gāśca hatvā sarudhirapiśitaṃ śuddhapakvaṃ hi kiñcit gomāṃsa sūtatoyaṃ ghṛtakaṭukasamaṃ taṇḍulaṃ śākamiśrām
ekasmin vahnipakvaṃ navaphalasahitaṃ yatra bhojyaṃ narāṇām pānañcāṇḍaṃ khagānāṃ bhavati narapate tatpadaṃ cāsurāṇāṃ || 155 ||
binduḥ śaktyāñjaneyo garuḍasurasarinnāradaḥ kāmadhenuḥ durgā vidvatsu vidyākṣaraparamakalādivyabhāṣāśarīrāḥ
ete sañcārarūpairavatarati vibhurjñānakāyo jinasya bhūtaṃ bhavyaṃ bhaviṣyata pravadati sakalaṃ vedatarkādiśāstram || 156 ||
śrīmān rājan kalāpe katipayadivasaiḥ sambhalākhye prasiddhe śailāveṣṭe caturdika śaraguṇitaśate yojanānāṃ pramāṇe
svasthāne yāsyasi tvaṃ pravaranapatiṃ sthāpayitvā sureśaṃ sapta śrīśākyavaṃśe prakaṭanṛpatayaścāṣṭamaḥ śrīyaśaśca || 157 ||
so'yaṃ śrīmañjuvajraḥ suravaranamito vajragotreṇa kalkī datvā vrajābhiṣekaṃ sakalamunikulānyekakalkaṃ kariṣyat
samyag yānādhirūḍho danukulabhayadaḥ śrīyaśa: sellapāṇiḥ sattvānāṃ mokṣahetoḥ prakaṭamapi mahau kālacakra kariṣyata || 158 ||
tanmadhye pañcaviṃśatkramaparigaṇita viṣṭarāṇāṃ yugānte kalkīgotre sureśaḥ suravaranamito raudrakalkī bhaviṣyat
sādhūnāṃ śāntarūpaḥ sukhada iti tathaivāntako mlecchajāteḥ śailāśvārūḍhacakrī hanadarisakala sellahasto'rkatejāḥ || 159 ||
kalkīgotrasya madhye karaguṇitayuge putrapautre'pyatīte tasmin kāle bhavad vai khalu makhaviṣaye mlecchadharmapravṛttiḥ
yāvanmlecchendraduṣṭaḥ suravaranamito raudrakalkī ca yāvata, tasmin kāle dvayośca kṣititalanilaye raudrayuddhaṃ bhaviṣyat || 160 ||
yuddhe mlecchān hanan yaḥ sakalabhuvi tale cāturaṅgaḥ svasainyaiḥ kailāsādrau yugānte suraracitapure cakravarttyāgamiṣyata
rudraṃ skandaṃ gaṇendraṃ harimapi ca sakhīn dāsyate kalkinā ca śailāśvān vāraṇendrān kanakarathanṛpān śastrahastān bhaṭāṃśca || 161||
śailāśvervāyuvegairguṇaguṇitaguṇaiḥ koṭivibhiśvavarṇe- rvedākhyairlakṣasaṃkhyaimaṃdamuditagajaiḥ syandanairbhūtalakṣaiḥ
ṣaḍbhiścākṣauhiṇobhī rasanavatikulaimaulibaddha narendre- retata sainyena kalkī hariharasahito mlecchanāśaṃ kariṣyata || 162 ||
mlecchadharmotpāṭanabuddhadharma pratiṣṭhāpanamevādi hantavyaṃ mlecchavṛndaṃ varakaṭukabhaṭervāraṇendrargajānāṃ śailāśvaiḥ saindhavānāṃ samaviṣamaraṇe pārthivaiḥ pārthivānām
aśvatthāmā mahācandratanayahanū māṃstīkṣṇaśastrairhaniṣyat rudro mlecchendranāthaṃ sakaladanupati kṛnmatī raudrakalkī || 163 ||
hatvā mlecchāṃśca yuddhe hariharasahitaḥ sarvasainyaikakalkī kailāśādrau vrajiyaṣyat suraracitapure saṃsthito yatra cakrī
tasmin kāle dharaṇyāṃ sakalajalakulaṃ dharmakāmārthapūrṇaṃ śasyānyāraṇyajāni sthiraphalanamitāste bhaviṣyanti vṛkṣāḥ || 164 ||
ucchinne mlecchavṛnde parijanasahite mānavābde śatārddha kalkī siddhi vajiṣyat suraracitapure tuṅgakailāśapṛṣṭhe
putro brahmā sureśastridaśana raguroryugmadharme bhavetāṃ brahmā pṛṣṭhaikakhaṇḍe bhavati narapatiḥ savyabhūmyāṃ sureśaḥ || 165 ||
kṛtsavyān mlecchadharmaṃ tribhuvanaguruka cchediyatvā siddhā cchitvā bdāṣṭau śatāni vrajati sukhapadaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ sthāpiyatvā
bhūyastasyaiva madhye bhavati narapate varṇabhedaḥ sutānāṃ teṣāṃ madhye'surendrā narapatimunayaḥ prākṛtā'nye bhavanti || 166 ||
mlecchānāṃ nāśahetorvrajati surapatirdvādaśendrābhiyuktaḥ khaṇḍe khaṇḍe ca cakrī vrajati sukhapadaṃ mlecchadharmaṃ nihatya
putrau brahmā sureśastridaśanava guroḥ pṛṣṭhataścātra tadvat pṛṣṭhe brahmādivaṃśe vividhabhuvi tale'nekabhedā bhavanti || 167 ||
brahmādau mānavābdāṣṭadaśaśatayugaṃ cāmva retannarāṇāṃ tasyāddha kāśyapasya pravarabhuvi tale nārasiṃhasya cārddham
ṣaṣṭyā hīnaṃ tathaiva pratidina samaye vāmanādau hareśca kalkyantaṃ yāvadāyuḥ prabhavati ca śataikābdasaṃkhyā janasya || 168 ||
10 156 vimalaprabhāyāṃ evaṃ sarveṣu khaṇḍeṣvapi yugasamayo mlecchadharmapravṛtti- [lokadhātu- varṣāstai (ṣṭai)kaśataṃ vai sthitirapi ca tato mlecchadharmasya nāśaḥ
tasmād viṃśatsahasraṃ karaśatarahitaṃ buddhadharmapravṛtiḥ kṛt tretā dvāparaṃ ve vahati kaliyugaṃ śaktimānena bhūmyām || 169 ||
utpatti rlokadhātorgrahacaraṇasamā sambhavaścakriṇaśca mlecchānāṃ dharmanāśaḥ paramasukhapade kalkino mārgadānam
etat sarvaṃ yathārthaṃ kathitamapi mayā te sucandra trikālād bhūyaḥ kiṃ pṛcchasi tvaṃ sakalajanahitārthaṃ ca māṃ mokṣahetoḥ || 170 ||
iti śrīmadādibuddhoddhṛte śrīmahākālacakre lokadhātuvinyāsa
prathamapaṭalaḥ || 1 ||
2.  adhyātmanāma dvitīyapaṭalaḥ (1) kāyavā cittotpatti caturāyaṃsatyanirṇaya-mahoddeśaḥ namaḥ ' śākyamunaye'
garbhādhānamidaṃ karoti mahatāṃ yaḥ pācanārthaṃ nṛṇāṃ nirmukto bhavabandhanairapi bhavaṃ (n) mārgāya ca prāṇinām
nānājātakamālikā guṇavatī sattvārthino dṛśyate
prāg buddhasya mahaddhikasya caritaṃ tasmai namastāyine
praṇamya vajrasattvaṃ śrīśākyasiṃhaṃ adhyātmapaṭale ṭīkā tathāgatam
puṇḍarīkeṇa likhyate
mañjuśrīcoditenaiva sugatavyākṛtena ca
mayā śrī lokanāthena jagataḥ karuṇātmanā
iha śrīmahākālacakramaṇḍala gṛhapūrvadvārāvasāne śrīmati mahāratnamaṇḍape ratna- siṃhāsanasthaḥ sūryarathādhyeṣitaḥ san mañjuśrībhagavān nirmitakāyo yaśo narendo'dhyātma- paṭaladeśanārthaṃ paramāvibuddhāt sucandrādhyeṣaṇaṃ śākyamunebhaṃgavataḥ prathamavṛttenāha; tadyathā- na jñātaṃ viśvamānaṃ jinajanaka mayā yat tvayā deśitaṃ ca bhūyo'haṃ śrotukāmastribhuvanasakalaṃ dehamadhye kathaṃ syāt
śrutvā saucandravākyaṃ pravadati sugataḥ sādhukāraṃ pradāya sattvānāṃ mokṣahetoḥ paramakaruṇayā viśvamānañca dehe
1
ihādhyātmapaṭale'dhyeṣakadeśakasaṃgrahavṛttam; mañjuśriyā saṅgītikāreṇa laghutantra- nirdeśitaṃ vitanomi na jñātamityādinā - iha dehamadhye na jñātaṃ viśvamānam, jinajanaka iti vajrasattvaḥ śākyamuniḥ, nirāvaraṇaskandhānāṃ janako jinajanakastasyāmantraṇaṃ he jinajanaka, tvayā deśitaṃ bāhye viśvamānaṃ taddehe na jñātam, ato bhūyo'haṃ śrotukāmastribhuvanasakalaṃ dehamadhye kathaṃ kena prakāreṇa bhavediti
ato'dhyeṣaṇaṃ saucandravākyaṃ śrutvā prakarṣeṇa vadati sugataḥ sādhukāraṃ pradāya sucandrāya sattvānāṃ mokṣahetoḥ paramakaruṇayā viśvamānaṃ ca dehe
cakārāt sarva- tantrāntaramapi dehe vadati
dvitīyapaṭaladeśanāyai adhyeṣakadeśakasaṃgrahoddeśaḥ
- 2. bho

Srid Pa (bhava )
ka. kha. iti
ka. kha. nirdeśaṃ
kha. kāraṇena
vimalaprabhāyāṃ deśanāvṛttamāha [86a] pṛthvītyādi - pṛthvītoyāgnivātāḥ kuliśasuradhanurvāyavo rāśicakraṃ candrārkau rāhumeruphaṇimanujasurā rāśivakrāḥ satārāḥ
saṃkrāntirmāsapakṣā dinaniśitithayo dehamadhye samastaṃ jñātavyaṃ svasvavarṇaistrividhavibhugatiryoginā śūnyabhedaiḥ
2
[ adhyātma- iha dehamadhye pṛthivyādikaṃ samastaṃ jñātavyaṃ svasvavarṇairiti
dantyavarṇaiḥ pṛthvī jñātavyā, auṣṭhyaiḥ stotram, mūrddhanyairagniḥ, tālavyairvāyuriti pṛthvītoyāgnivātāḥ
kuliśaṃ vidyut punarmūrddhanyaiḥ
suradhanurindradhanurdantyaiḥ
vāyavo daśa tālavyaiḥ
rāśicakram, ādikādyaiḥ samastaiśva candro bindunā'rko visargeṇa, rāhurvyañjanena asvareṇa, merurnāmākṣareṇa, phaṇināṃ vāsa oṃkāreṇa manujāvāso martyaloka ā: kāraṇa, surāvāsaḥ svargaḥ hūkāreṇa
vakrā grahā maṅgalādayo dakṣiṇavāmabījaiḥ pūrvokta: saṃkrāntirdvādaśavargabhedaiḥ; māsā apyevam; pakṣāḥ ṣaḍvargārddhabhedaiḥ dinaniśitithayaḥ kādyaiḥ sasvaraiḥ pūrvoktariti dehamadhye samastaṃ jñātavyaṃ yoginā
trividhavibhugatiryoginā śūnyabhedaiḥ
pūrvoktakrameṇa vajrasattvasya' trividhasya kāmarūpārūpasvarūpasya gatirapi trividhā, mahāsukhena sarvātmani sthitatvāditi
sā ca śūnyabhedairiti bodhicittacatubindubhedairjñātavya iti dehe viśva- saṃgrahoddeśaniyamaḥ
idānoṃ manuṣyaśarīrotpattaye kāryakāraṇasāmagrī saṃgrahavṛttaṃ tṛtīyamāha dehe ityādi- dehe'smin dhātuvṛndaṃ bhavati hi sakalaṃ ṣaḍrasāhārapānād bhūtebhyaḥ ṣaḍrasāśca prakaṭitaniyataṃ bhūtavṛndaṃ khadhātau
śūnye jñānaṃ vimiśraṃ bhavati samarasaṃ cākṣaraṃ śāśvataṃ ca evambhūtasthaśāntaṃ trividhabhavagataṃ veditavyaṃ svakāye
3
iha dehe dhātuvṛndamiti lomatvakraktamāṃsāsthimajjādikaṃ dhātuvṛ [86b]ndaṃ bhavati hi sakalaṃ ṣaḍrasāhārapānāditi lavaṇamadhurakaṭutiktakaṣāyāmlāḥ ṣaṭ rasāḥ, eṣāmāhārapānavaśād dhātuvṛndaṃ bhavati
bhūtebhyaḥ ṣaṭ rasā iti pṛthvyaptejovāyurasebhyaḥ ṣaḍ rasāśca bhavanti
bhūtavṛndaṃ khadhātāviti pṛthvyaptejovāyurasavṛndaṃ khadhātau pūrvokta- paramāṇudharme bhavanti
śūnye jñānaṃ vimiśramiti iha vyāpyavyāpakasambandhenāvasthitaṃ saṃvṛtyā yat svaśarīre sarvasattvānāṃ vijñānam, tadeva śūnye śūnyatābimbe bhāvanābalena grāhakacittaṃ grāhyacittābhāse vimiśraṃ samarasaṃ cākṣaraṃ śāśvataṃ bhavati
atra śā yogineti
ihādhyātmapaṭale asya vṛttasya saṃkṣepavivaraṇaṃ vakṣyamāṇe jñānapaṭale paramākṣara- jñānasiddhau vistareṇa vaktavyamiti śarīrotpattaye kāryakāraṇasāmagrīniyamaḥ
garbhādhānādikamāha bījamityādinā - bījaṃ dhatte dharitro kamalagatamato rohayatyambu paścāt tejo bodhaṃ karoti grasati rasarasaṃ māruto vṛddhimasya
vṛddheḥ khaṃ cāvakāśaṃ dadati narapate kālataḥ siddhirevaṃ divyāḥ kurvantyavasthāḥ prasavanasamaye bālakādau ca kāle
4
iha dvīndriyasaṃyogād yaccyutaṃ bhagaraktaṃ guhyakamale tad bījamityucyate
tad bījaṃ dhatta dharitrī, guhyakamale yaḥ pṛthvīdhātuḥ sa ityarthaḥ
kamalagatamiti guhyakamala- gatam, ato guhyakamalād rohayatyambu paścāt tejo bodhaṃ karotīti, asya bījasya ambunā prarohitasya' tejaḥ prabodhaṃ karoti
prasati rasarasamiti ṣaḍrasaṃ mātṛbhakṣitaṃ pītañca yat tad grasati rasarasaṃ tejaḥ
māruto vṛddhimasya bījasya karoti
vṛddheḥ sakāśāt khaṃ śūnyamavakāśaṃ dadāti
nara pate ityāmantraṇam
kālataḥ siddhirevam
evamanena krameṇa matsyādeḥ siddhirgarbhaniṣpattirbhavati
divyāḥ kurvantyavasthā iti divyā dhūmādayo daśāvasthāstāḥ kurvanti
garbhabālakaumārādikā daśāvasthāḥ, prasavanasamaye bālakādau ca kāla iti garbhādhānaniyamaḥ
bījasya svadhātukṛtyamāha bojasyetyādi- vṛddhimasya
bījasya damā karoti sphuṭagurughanatāṃ toyameva dravatvaṃ tejaḥ pākaṃ karoti pravaradaśavidho māruto vṛddherbhūyo'vakāśaṃ dadati hi gaganaṃ kālataḥ nnānābhāvairanekaiḥ satanuravamanaḥ śaktayaḥ sṛṣṭireva - sphārayanti
5
iha yā bījasya śukraraktasya kṣmā sā guhyakamalagatān ṣaḍ rasān bhakṣayitvā sphuṭaṃ gurughanatāṃ karoti, śarīrasyotpattaye toyamevaṃ dravatvaṃ karoti, tejaḥ pārka karoti bhakṣitānāṃ rasānām, pravaradaśavidho mārutaḥ prāṇādivṛddhi karoti, asya bījasya śarorāya, vṛddherbhayo' 'vakāśaṃ dadati hi gaganam, kālataḥ sṛṣṭirevam pūrvoktavidhānataḥ
nānābhāvairanekaiḥ satata (nu) ravamana iti kāyavākcittāni śaktayo dhūmādayaḥ kāyavāk- cittadharmiṇyaḥ sphārayantīti bījadhātukṛtyakathanam
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- adhyātmacittavajradharaviṣṇordaśāvasthā ucyante guhyetyādinā - guhyābje raktamadhye patitamapi yadā bodhibījaṃ sajīvaṃ matsyaḥ kūrmo varāho bhavati naraharirvāmano rāmarāmau
kṛṣṇo buddho narendro bhavati daśavidhā (: ) cāpyavasthā daśetā garbhe tisrastathaikā prasavanasamaye bālakāle'pi ca dve
6
kaumāre yauvane'nyā prabhavati vipulaikā ca samyak tathaikā vṛddhatve'pyekaśāntā bhavana (ti) nidhanatā cāntimā garbhajānām
matsyākārastu mātsye bhavati katidinaṃ kūrmabhāvaśca kaumyeṃ vārāhye tasya sūryāyatanamapi bhavennārasiṃhe prasūtiḥ ||7||
iha guhyābje raktamadhye patitamapi yadā bodhibījamiti śukraṃ sajīvamālaya- vijñānasahitaṃ guhyābje patitaṃ yadā tadā daśāvasthālakṣaṇaṃ bhavati |
teṣu garbhamadhye matsya kūrmava rāhāvasthāstisro bhavanti;
tathaikā prasavanakāle narasiṃhāvasthā;
bālakāle- 'pi ca dve, dantotthānād vāmanāvasthā, dantotthānād dantapātaṃ yāvat paraśurāmāvasthā |
kaumāre yauvane'nyā iti dantapātāt ṣoḍaśavarṣāvadheḥ prathamavipulā rāmāvasthā, tathā aparā vipulā ṣoḍaśavarṣāt palitotpatti yāvat kṛṣṇāvasthā, palitotpatteḥ maraṇa- divasaṃ yāvad buddhāvasthā', maraṇadine sarvabhūtasyai kalolībhūtatvāccharīre kalkyavasthā bhavati |
vṛddhatve'pyekaśāntā bhavana (ti) nidhanatā cāntimā garbhajānāmiti daśāvasthā- niyamaḥ |

paṭale ] samudayasatyādimahoddeśaḥ kāyādhārabhedena ṣaṭkulānyucyante nābhāvityādinā - iha śarīre ṣaḍdhātuvaśena ṣaṭcakreṣu ṣaṭkulāni bhavanti yathāsaṃkhyam - mātuḥ kulāni trīṇi, bhartuḥ pituḥ kulāni ca
nābhau pṛthivīcakre cakrakulaṃ bhavati, kaṇṭhe agni- padmaratnakulaṃ bhavati, guhyaṃ jñānakamale katikākulaṃ bhavati, māturyathāsaṃkhyaṃ kāyo (ya) vāścittañca

kāye bhāvapraveśa-dhātuvarṇā ucyante kāya ityādinā - kāye bhāvapraveśaḥ khamiva samaraso bhāvamadhye ca kāyo jñātavyo yogayuktaiḥ prakṛtiguṇavaśād dhātuvarṇādibhedaiḥ
pītaḥ kṛṣṇaśca varṇaṃstvaruṇa iti sito bhūmivātāgnitoye jñānākāśe ca nīlo bhavati ca haritaḥ kāyabhāvaprabhedaiḥ || 29 ||

tena grāhyagrāhakayoḥ parasparamekatvaṃ samarasatvam, ataḥ kāye bhāvapraveśaḥ

atra kāyabhedena kāyendriyam, gandhaviṣayam, pāyvindriyam, ālāpaḥ pṛthivīkāyasvabhāvataḥ pītavarṇāḥ bhāvavaśāt, vāyuprakṛtitaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇāḥ
evaṃ ghrāṇendriyam, sparśaḥ, vāgindriyam, viṭsrāvo vāyuprakṛtayaḥ kāyabhedena kṛṣṇavarṇāḥ; pṛthivī bhāvabhedena pītā: cakṣurindriyam, rasaḥ, pāṇīndriyam, gatistejaḥ prakṛtayaḥ kāyavaśād raktavarṇā bhavanti, bhāvavaśādudakaprakṛtayaḥ sitā bhavanti

vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- jñānaprakṛtibhāvabhedena nīlāḥ
tathā manendriyam (mana indriyam ), śabdaḥ, divyendriyam, mūtrasrāvaḥ, ete jñānadhātuprakṛtayaḥ kāla (ya) ' bhedena nīlavarṇāḥ ākāśaprakṛtayo bhāvabhedena haritāḥ
ato jñānākāśe ca nīlo bhavati ca haritaḥ kāyabhāvaprabhedairiti kāya bhāva- prakṛtivarṇaniyamaḥ
prajñopāyāliṅganamasthimāṃsādidhātūnāmāha prajñetyādi— prajñopāyo'sthimāṃsaṃ sasalilarudhiraṃ pāvako mūtrameva vāto viṭśūnyaśukra bhavati varatanau śrīrajaścittameva
śrotrācchandādayo'nye svajinakulavaśād vāksvarūpādayaśca evaṃ devyaḥ sabuddhā viṣayaviṣayiṇo maṇḍale veditavyāḥ ||30||
iha śarīre prajñā asthidhātuḥ, upāyo māṃsadhātuḥ, tayoḥ parasparaṃ saṃyogaḥ pṛthivī- saṃskārayoḥ, yat kaṭhinatatvaṃ tat pṛthivīdhāturiti jñāpakāt prajñopāyāsthimāṃsamiti
evaṃ prajñopāyaḥ sasalilarudhiramudakadhātunā sārddhaṃ vedanāskandhaḥ
tathā pāvaka iti uṣṇatā mūtramiti saṃjñā, anayo prajñopāyataḥ

yathā bāhye sūryasya saṃkrāntirūnādhikā dinairveditavyā, tathādhyātmani ūnādhikaśvāsairveditavyeti sūryasya daṇḍabhedena śarīre śvāsaniyamaḥ
prāṇasya ṛṇadhanaviṣuvaṃ karkaṭādiṣu ravikā [pāda]vaśena ṛṇam, makarādiṣu dhanam
jyotiṣoktavidhinā viṣuvaṃ pratisaṃkrāntidinaṃ sapādaṣaṭpañcāśaddaṇḍātmakamiti bāhya, adhyātmani śvāsātmakaṃ prati- lagnasaṃkrāntibhedeneti dvādaśasaṃkrāntibhramaṇam
idānīṃ pratyekakamaladale pañcamaṇḍalapravāha ucyate ekaika ityādinā- ekaike padmapatre pravahati dharaṇī paścime cottare'mbu vahniḥ savye ca pūrve pravahati pavanaḥ patramadhye ca śūnyam
saṃkrānti (:) [100a] pañcabhedaiḥ pravahati viṣuvaṃ honaniḥśvāsaṣaṣṭiḥ evaṃ saṃkrāntibhedāḥ pratidinasamaye lagnabhedairbhavanti || 39
iha bāhye sūryasya ekaikasaṃkrāntau pañcamaṇḍalabhogāḥ
triśaddinaiḥ pañcamaṇḍalabhogaḥ; ṣaḍdinaiḥ ṣaṣṭhyuttaratriśatadaṇḍairekamaṇḍalabhogaḥ; tathādhyātmanyaṣṭādaśaśataiḥ śvāsaiḥ pañcamaṇḍala- bhogaḥ, ṣaṣṭhyuttaratriśataśvāsairekamaṇḍalabhogo bhavati ( tīti) niyamaḥ

tatra pratyeke padma- patrake pravahati dharaṇī- maṇḍalaṃ paścime dalasyottare'mbumaṇḍalaṃ vahati, vahnimaṇḍalaṃ sanye vahati, punaḥ pūrve pravahati pavanamaṇḍalam
patramadhye ca śūnyamaṇḍalaṃ vahati
pratyekaṃ ṣaṣṭhayuttaraniḥśvāsaśatatrayaṃ prāṇaśaktiriti evaṃ saṃkrāntiḥ pañcabhedaiḥ bhavati pratyekā
eṣu maṇḍaleṣu ṣaṣṭhayuttaratriśataśvāsamadhye sapādaekādaśaikādaśaśvāsān gṛhītvā pravahati viṣuvaṃ lagnodayābhisandhī, honaniḥśvāsaṣaṣṭiriti satripādaśvāsatrayonānuvahati śvāsān sapāda- 30 30 idānīṃ pañcamaṇḍalādhidevā ucyante śūnyetyādinā - śūnādyaṃ pañca tattvaṃ pravahati niyataṃ cottare kṣmādisavye
rāhvagnī candrasūryau buddhamahītanayau daityamantrī guruśca |
keturmandaśca khādiṣviti daśapatayaḥ kṣetriṇo maṇḍaleṣu ṣaṇmāsaṃ candra īśo bhavati narapate cottare dakṣiṇe'rkaḥ || 40 || paṭale ] samudaya satyādimahoddeśaḥ
iha bāhye sūryasya meṣādiviṣamalagne śūnyamaṇḍalādibhogaḥ, vṛṣabhādisamalagne pṛthi- vīmaṇḍalādibho gaḥ;
tathādhyātmani śūnyādyaṃ pañca tatvamiti śūnyaṃ vāyuragni- stoyaṃ pṛthivīmaṇḍalaṃ pravahati niyatamuttare vāmanāsāpuṭe |
kṣmādi ( dī ) ti pṛthivyaptejovā- yuśūnyamaṇḍalaṃ savyanāsāpuṭe vahati |
eṣu vāma ( apasavya ) savyeṣu daśamaṇḍaleṣu yathāsaṃkhyaṃ vāme śūnyamaṇlaṃ rāhuradhidevatā pravāhakāle;
dakṣiṇe kālāgniḥ vāme vāyumaṇḍale candraḥ, dakṣiṇe sūrya:, vāme vahnimaṇḍale budhaḥ, dakṣiṇe maṅgalaḥ;
vāme toyamaṇḍale śukraḥ, dakṣiṇe bṛhaspatiḥ;
vāme pṛthivīmaṇḍale ketuḥ, dakṣiṇe mandaḥ ;
evaṃ khādiṣu maṇḍaleṣvadhipatayo daśa kṣetriṇo bhavantīti |
evaṃ ṣaṇmāsaṃ candra īśo bhavati, meṣamithunasihatulādhanuḥ, kumbharāśau vāmanāḍyāṃ candraḥ śukradhāturośa uttare;
dakṣiṇe vṛṣabhakarkaṭakanyāvṛścika- makaramīna rāśī;
arko rajodhāturīśo bhavati |
narapata ityāmantraṇamiti maṇḍalādhipati- niyamaḥ |
idānīṃ prāṇādayo vāyava ucyante tasyetyādinā- tasyordhvaṃ hṛtpradeśe vasudalakamale saṃsthitaṃ nāḍicakra' prāṇādyaṃ vāyuvṛndaṃ raviśaśiśikhino vāmasavye ca madhye |
vāme nāḍī śaśāṅko vahati khalu sitā dakṣiṇe raktasūryā madhye kālāgnirūpā pravahati viṣuve hīnaniḥśvāsaṣaṣṭiḥ || 41 ||
iha nābhicakre garbhe caturdalātmake, tadbāhne aṣṭadalātmake, tadbāhaye dvādaśa dalātmake, tadbāhaye catuḥṣaṣṭidalātmake |
dalaśabdena nāḍyaḥ śvāsadharmiṇyo gṛhyante |
tasya nābhicakrasyordhvaṃ sārddhadvādaśāṅgulādūrdhvaṃ hṛtpradeśe saṃsthitam, nāḍīcakramaṣṭāram aṣṭanāḍyātmakam |
teṣu prāṇādyaṃ vāyuvṛndaṃ vasudalakamale śaśi - raviśikhinaḥ |
śikhoti rāhuḥ |
vāme candranāḍī śaśāṅko vahati sitavarṇā, dakṣiṇe rakta- varṇā sūryasya nāḍī va hati, aṣṭasu daleṣu madhye kālāgnirūpā nāḍī rāhunāḍī pravahati, viṣuve lagnodayābhisandhī sapādaṣaṭpañcāśat śvāsāni ( i ) ti |

suṣumnāyāṃ prāṇaḥ, diksaṃkhyā saṃ ( śaṃ) khinīyā sravati bodhicittaṃ tasyāmapāno'dhi- devatā |
ityevaṃ nāḍi (ḍo) cakra e daśavidhapavanāḥ saṃsthitāḥ karmabhedaiḥ |
eṣāṃ prāṇādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ vā dvāsaptatināḍīsahasreṣu vyāptiḥ kintvaṣṭasthāneṣu janmāṣṭānāmiti |
saṃ (śaṃ)- khinyantaṃ tviḍādyaṃ svahṛdayakamalaṃ nābhicakraṃ samastam, janmasthānaṃ samānādīnāmiti cakraniyamaḥ |
idānīṃ prāṇādikṛtyamucyate prāṇa ityādinā - prāṇaḥ prāṇaṃ karotyarkaśaśipathagatastvannapānaṃ samastam āpāno netyadhastāt sakalasamarasaṃ neti kāye samānaḥ |
kāye spandatyudāno mukhakaracaraṇairgītanāṭayaṃ karoti vyāno vyādhiṃ karoti prakṛtiguṇavaśād gātrabhaṅgaṃ tathaiva || 43 ||

arkapathaḥ piṅgalā, rasanā vā;

annapānaṃ samastam, yat rasaṃ bhakṣitamudakādipītam, tat sakalamapānavāyuradho netītyāgamaḥ |

vyāno vyādhi karoti prakṛtiguṇavaśāditi udakaprakṛtivaśād gātrabhaṅgaṃ tathaiva karoti yathā vyādhim |
nāgo'pyudgārameva sphuṭakaracaraṇāt saṃkucan kūrmavāyuḥ krodhaṃ kṣobhaṃ samastaṃ sa kṛkarapavano jṛmbhikāṃ devadattaḥ |

idānīṃ nāḍīsaṃjñā tīrthikasaṃjñābhirucyate piṅgatyādinā - piṅgāḥ sūkṣmāstviḍādyāstrividhapathagatā rohiṇī hastijihvā pūṣā ṣaṣṭhī jayā'gāpi ca vasu navame'lambuṣā śrīkuhe ca |
diksaṃkhyā śaṅkhinī yā sravati narapate bodhicittaṃ sukhānte etat śrīnāḍicakraṃ bhavati bahuvidha sandhibhedairanekaiḥ || 45 ||
saṃkhyā śaṅkhinī yā daśamī śaṅkhinī bodhicittaṃ travati sukhānte |
etat śrīnāḍicakraṃ bhavati bahuvidhaṃ sandhibhedairanekairiti |
atra śarīre sandhibhedāḥ ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatasaṃkhyāstaiḥ sandhibhedaiḥ ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatanāḍīcakrabhedā bhavantīti |
prāṇādivāyuprakṛtyabhedā apyevaṃ jñeyā iti nāḍīcakraniyamaḥ |
indre'gnau yāmyadaitye savaruṇapavane yakṣarudre ca madhye eṣu sthāneṣu śaktibhraṃmati dinaniśaṃ tatsvarūpaṃ bhajantī |
tasyordhvaṃ kaṇṭhacakre grahagaṇasahitaṃ saṃsthitaṃ carkṣacakraṃ brahmāṇḍe śrīlalāṭe svaraparikaritaṃ śvetabinduṃ sravan vai || 46 ||
idānīṃ tasminneva nāḍīcakre indrādyadhiṣṭhānamucyate indra ityādinā - iha indradale prāṇaśaktirindrasvabhāvaṃ bhajati praharamekaṃ yāvat;
agnidale agni- svabhāvam, yāmyadale yamasvabhāvam, naiṛtyadale nairṛtyasvabhāvam, vāruṇyadale varuṇasva- bhāvam, vāyavyadale vāyusvabhāvam, dhanadadale dhanadasvabhāvam, īśadale rudrasvabhāvam |
evameṣvaṣṭadaleṣu tatsvabhāvaṃ bhaja ntī bhramati prāṇaśaktiriti indrādisvabhāva- niyamaḥ |
idānīṃ kaṇṭhacakramucyate- iha hṛtkamalasyordhvaṃ sārddhadvādaśāṅgalānte kaṇṭhacakre prahagaṇasahitaṃ saṃsthitaṃ cakṣaṃ- cakram |
catvāri daṇḍanakṣatrāṇi, aṣṭāviṃśati pradhānanakṣatrāṇi;
ebhirdvātriṃśaddalakam |
caturdaṇḍanakṣatrāṇi tyaktvā prāṇasyāṣṭāviṃśaddalakeṣvahorātreṇa saṃkramaṇaṃ ṣaṣṭidaṇḍebhyo bhāgena labdhaṃ pratyekadale sañcāra iti |
brahmāṇḍe śrīlalāṭe svaraparikaritaṃ śvetabinduṃ sravan vai |
tasyāpi kaṇṭhakamalasyordhvaṃ sārddhadvādaśāṅgulānte lalāṭakamale ṣoḍaśadale daladvayaṃ tyaktvā caturdaśadaleṣu prāṇasyāhorātreṇa sañcāraḥ;
ṣaṣṭidaṇḍebhyaścaturdaśabhāgalabdhena pratyekadalatyāgaḥ |
vimalaprabhāyāṃ
adhyātma- binduvisargau kaṇikāyāṃ veditavyo candrapadavāheti
punarnābhicakre catuḥṣaṣṭināḍyo bhīmā- disaṃjñābhiḥ hṛdaye aṣṭa iḍādisaṃjñābhiḥ;
kaṇṭhe'śvinyādisaṃjñācatuḥ sandhyāsaṃjñābhiḥ;
lalāṭa- nāḍyāṃ pratipadādisaṃjñābhiḥ;
uṣṇīṣe catuḥ sandhyābhedena catuḥ tattvasaṃjñābhiḥ;
ityeṣu kaṇṭha- lalāṭakamaladaleṣvantarbhūtamaṣṭadalaṃ cakraṃ veditavyam
evaṃ nābhau hṛdaye kaṇṭhe lalāṭe aṣṭāra- cakraṃ pṛthivyaptejovāyusvabhāvam;
uṣṇīṣe caturdalaṃ śūnyasvabhāvam;
nābhau caturdalaṃ jñāna- svabhāvam;
guhye dvātriṃśaddalakam;
bāhya ṣoḍaśadaleṣu svaranāḍyaḥ;
antardaśadaleṣu skandha- dhātunāḍyaḥ;
tato'ntardaleṣu ṣaṭ śūnyanāḍya iti
evaṃ ṣaṭcakrasaṃkhyā ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaika- śataṃ ṣaṭcakreṣu vakṣyamāṇeṣu vaktavyami ti ṣaṭcakraniyamaḥ
mūle pṛthvyambu vāme pravahati hutabhuk dakṣiṇe mūrdhni vāyu- maṃdhye vyomadvayośca pravahati viṣuvaṃ nāsarandhrakrameṇa
bhindantyetāni śaktirvrajati parapadaṃ dvādaśāntaṃ kalāntaṃ kṣetrādyā hastapāde karakamalapuṭe'ṅguṣṭhakāntāḥ samastāḥ || 47
idānīṃ nāsāpuṭadvaye pañcamaṇḍalapravāha ucyate mūla ityādinā - iha vāmanāsāpuṭe dakṣiṇe ca mūle adho bhāge pṛthivīmaṇḍalaṃ vahati, ambu vāme vahati vāme da kṣiṇe ca nāsāpuṭe;
nāsāpuṭadvayamadhyarekhābhimukhaṃ sarveṣāścottarastho meruriti jñāpakāt
pravahati hutabhu ṅmaṇḍalaṃ dakṣiṇe, dakṣiṇaṃ ca ubhayottarayorbāhayaṃ nāsārandhrabhāgamiti
mūrdhni vāyumaṇḍalaṃ vahati ubhayornāsāpuṭayoḥ madhye vyomadvayo- rnāsāpuṭayoḥ pṛthvyaptejovāyumaṇḍalamadhye vyomamaṇḍalaṃ vahati;
vāmalagnasañcāre vāme, dakṣiṇa- lagnasañcāre dakṣiṇe maṇḍala pravāhaḥ
dvayornāsāpuṭayo: punaryaugapadyena viṣuvaṃ jñānamaṇḍalaṃ vahati prāṇaśaktiriti
bhindantyetāni nābhihatkaṇṭhalalāṭoṣṇīṣanāsāpuṭamaṇḍalāni bhindantī daṇḍa rāśi praharanakṣatratithi sandhyāmaṇḍalabhedairiti
śaktiḥ prāṇaśaktirvrajati parapadaṃ nirālambaṃ dvādaśāntaṃ dvādaśāṅgulāntamiti, kalāntaṃ ṣoḍaśakalāntamiti
iha pṛthivī- maṇḍalapravāha vāyurnāsāpuṭāt dvādaśāṅgulaṃ vrajati, tato nivartate;
udakamaṇḍalavāyustrayo- daśāṅgulaṃ vrajati, tato nivartate;
tejomaṇḍalavāyuścaturdaśāṅgulaṃ vrajati, tato nivartate;
vāyumaṇḍalavāyuḥ pañcadaśāṅgulaṃ vrajati, tato nivartate;
śūnyamaṇḍalavāyuḥ ṣoḍaśāṅgulaṃ vrajati, tato nivartate;
jñānamaṇḍalavāyuḥ pṛthivīmaṇḍalānteṣu madhye praviṣṭā dvāda- śāṅgulaṃ vahati puṭadvaye, ākāśānte madhyamā praviṣṭā ṣoḍaśāṅgulaṃ vahati
iha yadā bāhye vṛddhiṃ gacchati, tadā'dhyātmani hrasvo bhavati;
yadā ṣoḍaśādī bahihrasvo bhavati vāyu- nāḍīvaśena, tadā nābhau varddhante caturaṅgulānīti prāṇaśaktipravāhaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ kṣetrādaya ucyante kṣetra ityādinā- iha śarīre kṣetradvayaṃ bāhusandhidvaye, upakṣetramurusandha dvaye, chandohadvayaṃ upabāhu- iti nāsti

cakravattimlecchayuddha kālacakrakulatantra nāḍīkulotpatti- mahoddeśaḥ sandhidvaye, upacchandohaṃ jānusandhidvaye, melāpakadvayaṃ karasandhidvaye, upamelāpakaṃ pādasandhi- dvaye, śmaśānadvayaṃ karataladvaye, upaśmaśānadvayaṃ pādataladvaye, caturmahāśmaśānāni karāṅguli- dvaye, upa śmaśānāni catvāri pādāṅgulidvaye iti kṣetrādini yamaḥ
iti śrīmūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ laghukālacakratantra rājaṭīkāyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ samudaya satyādimahoddeśo dvitīyaḥ || 2
cakravattimlecchayuddha - kālacakra kulatantra nāḍīkulotpatti-mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ cakravattimlecchayuddhaṃ svadehe ucyate cakrītyādinā - cakrī vatre svadehe suravarapatayo dvādaśāṅgā niruddhāḥ samyagjñānaṃ hi kalkī gajaturagarathāḥ kiṅkarāryyapramāṇāḥ
pratyekaṃ rudrasaṃjñā prabhavati hanūmān śrāvakaṃ prāṇināṃ ca pāpaṃ mlecchendraduṣṭastvakuśalamapi yat kṛnmatirduḥkhadātā || 48
iha svadehe manuṣyadehe yo bāhya cakrī prathamapaṭale uktaḥ, sa dehe vajrī cittavajra ityarthaḥ
ye suravarapatayo dvādaśa īśvarādayaḥ, te dvādaśāṅgā niruddhā iti
yaḥ kalkī samyagjñānaṃ svadehe hi tasmāddhetoḥ
ye bāhya gajaturagarathāḥ kiṅkarā ārya-apramāṇā- ścatvāro dehe, te yo bāhya rudraḥ sa dehe pratyekajñānasaṃjñī; yo bāhya hanūmān sa svadehe śrāvakajñānamiti svadehe mahā ' cakrī saparivāraṃ kalkī ca bhavati
tathā yo bāhya mlecchendraduṣṭo'sau pāpacittaṃ śarīre
yo bāhyaṃ kṛnmatirduḥkhadātā akuśalapatha iti dehe
aśvatthāmā tvavidyā danubalasakalaṃ mārapakṣaścaturdhā saṃhārastasya yuddhe bhavabhayanidhanaṃ śrījayo mokṣamārgaḥ
kailāśe dharmadānaṃ bhavabhayaharaṇaṃ dravyapūrṇā ca pṛthvī putro brahmāsureśastridaśanaraguroḥ pṛṣṭhataścāgrato yaḥ || 49
mañjuśrī rlokanāthastribhuvanavijayī śrīrajo bodhicittaṃ pṛṣṭhe brahmādivaṃśā vividhamahitale'nekabuddhā viśuddhāḥ
bho.
Ne bahi Dur Khrod Chen po māyārūpaṃ tu bāhye khalu makhaviṣaye mlecchayuddhaṃ na yuddham || 50
yo bāhya aśvatthāmā sa dehe avidyāpravṛttiriti yad bāhya danubalaṃ hastyaśva- pādātikaṃ sa dehe mārapakṣacaturdhā
saṃhārastasya yuddha e, yo bāhya mleccheṣu tat svadehe bhavabhayasya nidhanam
śrījayo yo bāhya mahācakriṇaḥ kalkinaśca sa svadehe mokṣamārgaṃlābhaḥ
yad bāhya kailāśe dharmadānaṃ mahācakriṇaḥ tat svadehe bhavabhaya- haraṇam
yā bāhya dravyapūrṇā dharitrī sā śarīre dhātusamūho viśuddhaḥ
bāhya yau cakriṇaḥ putrau brahmāsureśāvabhūtām, pṛṣṭhataḥ ekakhaṇḍe dharmadeśakaḥ, agrato'grakhaṇḍe mleccha- dharmakṣayārthī
samañjuśrīrlokanāthastribhuvanavijayī tat svadehe rajo bodhicittaṃ sukhadamiti praṇidhānacittaṃ prasthānacittaṃ ca
pṛṣṭhe khaṇḍe ye brahmādivividhavaṃśāste svadehe anekabuddhā viśuddhāḥ skandhadhātvāyatanasvabhāvā iti mlecchendrayuddhaṃ bhavati hi niyataṃ dehināṃ dehamadhye
māyārūpaṃ tu bāhya khalu makhaviṣaye mlecchayuddha na yuddhamiti asya vistaro vakṣyamāṇe pañcamapaṭale paramākṣarajñānasiddhau vaktavya iti
idānīṃ madhyamā tripakṣaṃ trivarṣabhedaistrikulāditantrabhedaḥ samājādika ucyate kālābda ityādinā - kālābde vahnisaṃkhye'bdadinagaṇahate ṣaṭkulaṃ cāyanāṅkāt ṣaṭtriṃśanmāsabhedād rasaguṇitarasād yoginīnāṃ kulāni
vāracchedena labdhā prakaṭitaniyatā devatāḥ kālacako pakṣacchede'bhidhānaṃ bhavati narapate devatādevatībhiḥ || 51
iha kālābde madhyamā 'nāḍīvarṣe vahnisaṃkhye trivarṣasaṃkhye bdadinagaṇahate ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatadinairhate sati aśītyuttarasahasro dinagaṇo bhavati
tadeva trivarṣaṃ trikulaṃ kāyavāka citta kulavarṣabhedena sattvarajastamaḥ svabhāvamiti
tato dinagaṇādayanabhedena aśītyuttaraśatabhāgena labdhāni ṣaṭkulāni bhavanti
punarmāsabhedena tato dinagaṇāt triṃśadbhāgena labdhāni ṣaṭtraṃśat kulāni bhavanti
rasaguṇitarasāditi ṣaḍbhirguṇitāḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadyoginīnāṃ kulāni
evaṃ yoginīnāmupāyānāmiti
tato'śītyuttarasahasra- dinagaṇāt vāracchedena labdhā prakaṭitaniyatā devatā kālacakra māṇḍaleyā iti
catuḥpañcāśadadhikaśataṃ śeṣaṃ prajñopāyayugalam; evaṃ ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaśataṃ kālacakram; sapta- bhāgāvaśeṣaṃ kulikāsthānādhidaivaṃ devatādevīcakraṃ kulikālakṣaṇamiti; punastasmāt pūrvadina- pratīyate;
bho.

tridaśa iti trayodaśa

paṭale ] cakravattimlecchayuddha-kālacakrakulatantra - nāḍīkulotpatti-mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ kālacakradevatādevīsaṃkhyā ucyate śaktītyādinā - : iha ṣaṭcakranāḍīviśvasvabhāvo devatāgaṇa:

pañcakaṃ buddhadevya iti akṣobhyādayaḥ pañca buddhāḥ, vajradhātvīśvaryādayaḥ pañca devyaḥ iti pratyekaṃ pañcakam
rūpādyakṣyādiṣaṭkaṃ punarapi nṛpate devavṛndaṃ dviṣaṭkaṃ yoginyo nāgacaṇḍāṣṭakamiti bhuvane sarvametad dviguṇyam
etat śrīkālacakraṃ caturadhikaśataṃ tasya cārddha tridhātau sūryāṃśe yā ca ṣaṣṭiḥ pratidinaghaṭikā nirgatā māsamadhye || 54 ||
rūpādyakṣyādiṣaṭkamiti
rūpavajrādayaḥ ṣaṭ kṣitigarbhādayaḥ ṣaṭ, evaṃ pratyekaṃ ṣaṭkaṃ punardevavṛndaṃ dviṣaṭkamiti indrādikaṃ dvādaśasaṃkhyam, yoginyo nāgacaṇḍāṣṭakamiti cacikādīnāmaṣṭakam, anantādīnāmaṣṭakam, śvānāsyādīnāmaṣṭakam, etat samastaṃ ( ) devatāgaṇam (:) sādhanāpaṭale pratyekaṃ nāmabhedairvaktavyamiti
bhuvana iti bāhye'dhyātmani sarvametat punaḥ prajñopāyabhedena dviguṇyam; etat śrīkālacakraṃ caturadhikaśataṃ tasya cāddhaṃ dvāpañcāśaddevatātmakam
evaṃ sūryacandrabhedena punastadevaikatvena ṣaṭpañcāśadadhika- śatadevātmakaṃ kālacakraṃ veditavyam iti kamalakaṇikāsthadevatāgaṇaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ carcikādikamalapatradevya ucyante- sūryāṃśe dvātriṃśadbhāgena śvāsacakrāt pratidine ghaṭikādvayaṃ yallabdhaṃ māsasya madhye triṃśaddinaiḥ ṣaṣṭināḍyo bhavanti kulikārūpiṇyaḥ
yoginyastāḥ samastāstribhuvananilaye lagnamiśrā dviguṇyā sūryāṃśe'haḥ prabhinnaṃ dviguṇitamapi yad yoginīvṛndamaṃtra- piṇḍībhūtāḥ samastāstriguṇaravidināssābhidhānā bhavanti candre pakṣaprabhedo bhavati dinakare vargabhedaḥ samastaḥ ||55||
tāśca yoginyo lagnamiśrā lagnacatuḥ sandhyākulikā vimiśrā catuḥṣaṣṭirbhavanti
tāśca prajñopāyabhedena dviguṇitā aṣṭāviṃśatyadhikaśatasaṃkhyā bhavantīti
punaḥ sūryāṃśe'haḥ prabhinnaṃ ṣaṣṭayuttaratriśatadinaprabhinnaṃ caitrāditithilakṣaṇaṃ devatāgaṇam
tadeva dvādaśalāsyā- dibhiḥ sahitaṃ dviguṇitamapi viṃśatyadhikasaptaśatasaṃkhyaṃ devatīvṛndaṃ bhavatīti
piṇḍībhūtāḥ samastā iti etā devyaḥ sābhidhānā dvāsaptatidevatābhiḥ sahitā mudrāṣaṭkena sārddhaṃ triguṇaravidinā trivarṣadinā aśītyuttarasahasrasaṃkhyā iti trivarṣadinagaṇasvabhāvā devatā- devyo veditavyā yoginītantre'[ itya]bhiprāyaḥ
evaṃ sūryavarṣaprabhedo bhavati śaśadhare pakṣabhedaḥ samasto māyājālasamājayornavadhā catvāriṃśadadhikapañcaśatadevatāgaṇairiti

etā vai mṛtyunāḍyo guruniyamavaśādāyurārogyadāśca ṣaṭcakra e vāyunāḍyo maraṇabhayaharā yogināṃ nātra citram || 58 ||
eṣu ṣaṭ nāḍyaḥ ṣaṭ cakrarodhā bhavanti; daśa viṣayahāriṇyo bhavanti
tāḥ sannipātasvabhāvā iti tāḥ punadvaguṇyāḥ śleṣmāṃśāḥ viṃśatiḥ bhūyo dviguṇyā pittāṃśāḥ catvāriṃśad bhavanti, punarapi anilāṃśāstā'śītirbhavanti, iti ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaśatanāḍyaḥ ṣaṭcakreṣu vyavasthitāḥ kālacakranāḍyo'vagantavyāḥ
etā vai mṛtyunāḍyo guru- niyamavaśādāyurārogyadāśca
ṣaṭcakre kālanāḍya uktā maraṇabhayaharā gurūpadeśena bhāvitāḥ satyo yogināṃ nātra citramiti
uṣṇīṣe'bdhirlalāṭe jaladhihatayugāḥ śleṣmadhātuprakopāḥ kaṇṭhe dantā hṛdabje nayanahatayugāḥ pittadhātuprakopāḥ
nābhau guhye'bdhiṣaṣṭirnṛpatirapi tathā vāyudhātuprakopā guhye'nyādiṣaṭkaṃ prakaṭitaniyatāḥ sannipātā nirodhāḥ || 59 ||
uṣṇīṣe'bdhiścatasraḥ jaladhihatayugā iti ṣoḍaśa lalāṭe, etā viṃśati nāḍyaḥ śleṣmadhātuprakopā iti
kaṇṭhe dantā iti dvātriṃśannāḍyaḥ
hṛdabje nayanahatayugā iti aṣṭa nāḍyaḥ, etāścatvāriśannāḍyaḥ pittadhātuprakopā iti nābhau ca guhyaṃ ca yathāsaṃkhyam abdhiṣaṣṭiriti catuḥṣaṣṭināḍyaḥ
nṛpatirapi guhyakamale bāhye parimaṇle nāḍyaḥ ṣoḍaśa, etā aśīti nāḍyo vāyudhātuprakoṣā iti
guhye'nyādik ceti daśa nāḍyo madhya- parimaṇḍale
anyā ṣaṭ ca garbhaparimaṇḍale yathāsaṃkhyam
sannipātāḥ sannipātaprakopā iti
nirodhā iti ṣaṭcakranirodhasvabhāvāḥ
evaṃ ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaśatakālacakranāḍyo devatādevatīsvabhāvenāvasthitā bālānāṃ mṛtyudāyikāḥ, yogināṃ sukhadāyikāḥ ṣaḍkula- nāḍībhiḥ sārddhaṃ bhāvitāḥ satyaḥ; dvāṣaṣṭyadhikaśataṃ kālacakraṃ ṣaṭcakranāḍyātmakamiti
tāsu punardvāṣaṣṭyadhikaśatanāḍīṣu pratyekanāḍī daśavāyupracāreṇa skandhadhātudaśasvabhāvena daśavidhā bhavanti
evaṃ sarvā daśaguṇitā viṃśatyadhikaṣoḍaśaśatasaṃkhyā bhavanti saptāviṃśanna- kṣatraghaṭikāvāhinyaḥ
evaṃ śrīmānnakṣatramaṇḍalaparamādibuddhe etāvatyo devatyaḥ prajñopāye- neti'
ato vistarānnakṣatramaṇḍalāllokasaṃvṛtyā śleṣmādidhātuvāhinyo devatā laukikasiddhisādhanāya ca śarīrasiddhisādhanāya vā mañjuśriyoddhṛtāḥ ṣaṭcakranāyikā iti kālacakranāḍī saṃkara niyamaḥ
ṣaṇṇāḍyaścaka rodhāstrividhapathagatāścandrasūryāgnibhedai- dehe tā rakṣaṇīyā: guruniyamavaśānmṛtyudāḥ prāṇināṃ yāḥ
ṣaṭsu prāṇapraveśo yadi bhavati nṛṇāṃ mṛtyuhānistadā vai sūkṣmāyāṃ na praviṣṭe hyamaraṇa viṣayaśchidyate yogibhiśca || 60 ||
iha śarīre yāḥ ṣaḍ nāḍyaścakrarodhā uktāstrividhapathagatāścandrasūrya rāhuvāma- dakṣiṇamadhyapathagatāḥ ūrdhvamadhoviṇmūtraśukrapathagatāścandrasūryāgnibhedeneti
dehe tā rakṣaṇīyā yoginā; kutaḥ ?
guruniyamavaśāditi
guruniyamaḥ ṣaḍaṅgayogaḥ, tena maraṇād rakṣaṇīyā mṛtyudāḥ prāṇināṃ yāḥ ṣaṇṇamāsāṃ prāṇapraveśa iti
ṣaṭsu prāṇapraveśo yadi bhavati nṛṇāṃ sarvakālaṃ mṛtyuhānistadā vai ekāntameṣu ṣaṭsu madhye sūkṣmā'vadhūtī madhyamā, tasyāṃ sūkṣmāyāṃ prāṇe na praviṣṭe sati hyamaraṇaviṣayaḥ cchidyate yogibhiśca; api tuna cchidyate maraṇaviṣaya iti
dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ athyātmani cakravattamlecchayuddha - kālacakrakulatantra nāḍīkulotpatti-mahoddeśaḥ (4) ariṣṭamaraṇalakṣa nāḍīccheda-mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ dehināmakālamaraṇalakṣaṇamucyate prāṇa ityādinā - prāṇo yadyekanāḍyāṃ vahati dinaniśaṃ jīvitaṃ kālavarṣam avyucchinnaikapakṣaṃ vahati yadi nṛṇāṃ jīvitaṃ sārddhavarṣam
tṛtīyaḥ || 3 ||
peṭale ].
ariṣṭamaraṇalakṣaṇa- nāḍīccheda-mahoddeśaḥ avyucchinnaikamāsaṃ vahati narapate jīvitaṃ ca trimāsaṃ evaṃ yogī svedehe śaśigatimaraṇaṃ jñāyate kālacakra || 61 ||
iha śarīre nābhicakraṃ dvādaśāraṃ rāśicakraṃ meṣādiviṣamalagnātmakamekāntaritam
evaṃ vṛṣabhādisamalagnātmakamekāntaritaṃ dalam

ato vaiṣamyāt prāṇasya vaiṣamyam, tena ahorātramekarātryāṃ (nāḍyāṃ) vāmāyāṃ yadi vahati, tadā jīvitaṃ kālavarṣam; madhyamā varṣaṃśatapūrṇalakṣaṇaṃ jīvitasaṃkhyaṃ narāṇāṃ bhavati
avyucchikapakṣaṃ yadi vahati, tadā sādūrdhavarṣa māyurbhavati'; tato maraṇaṃ gacchati
avyucchinnaṃ nirantaraṃ yadi māsamekaṃ vāmanāḍyāṃ vahati, tadā jīvitaṃ māsatrayaṃ bhavati
evaṃ svadehe yoginā śaśigatimaraṇaṃ jñāyate kālacakre iti

vimalaprabhāyāṃ iha śarīre nābhikamale dvādaśanāḍyātyakaṃ rāśicakram tatra janmasthānaṃ prathama rāśināḍīgarbhotpādasya bālakasya yallagnaṃ tadeva vṛṣabhādikaṃ samanāḍīsvabhāvam tasmāt svasthānād rāśicakraṃ dvādaśāraṃ nāḍīcakraṃ ṣaṣṭimaṇḍalātmakaṃ tyajati parakalā prāṇaśaktiḥ hāniśeṣaiśca tulyairiti hānirārohaṇadivasāḥ pañca daśa pañcadaśa pañcaviṃśatiḥ ṣaḍviṃśatiḥ saptaviṃśatiriti hāniḥ śeṣāstrivarṣā, dvau varṣo ekavarṣaṃ ṣaṇmāsāḥ trimāsa dvau māsau māsamekamiti hāniśeśa stairhāniśeṣaiḥ prathamaṃ patrādikaṃ yāvat saptanāḍīpatraṃ tāvat kramo varṣairmāsairiti trinayanaśaśibhivarṣairnāḍītrayaṃ tyajati, ṣaṭtriyu- gmendubhirmāsaiḥ saptanāḍīṃ tyajati dinaiśca pauṣṇāt vai hānitulyaiḥ pauṣṇakālāvadherjanma-

ekadakṣiṇanāḍīpravāheṇa dvādaśamī dinadvayena vāmanāḍyāṃ madhyamā yāmekadineneti kaṇikāyāṃ praviṣṭaḥ
evaṃ ṣoṣṇāt ṣaṭtraṃśaddinairbhavati dinakarāriṣṭayogena mṛtyurnarāṇāmiti dvitīya- pūrve vāṇāgnilokaṃ tyajati
dinagaṇaṃ sūryavarṣatrayasya tasmāt sārohaṇaṃ ve śarayugaśikhinaṃ khāgnicandraṃ tṛtīye netrāhiśelavāṇaṃ vasukaramaparaṃ saptame vasukaramaparaṃ saptame rohaṇaṃ ca tasmāt tithyādisarvaṃ dinagaṇamapi yat kaṇikā yāvadeva || 64 ||
idānī patrāt patre trivarṣād dinatyāga ucyate pūrvaṃ ityādinā - iha rāśicakre janmalagnanāḍī pūrvaṃ ityucyate tasmin janmalagnapatre aśītyuttara- sahasradinagaṇāt madhye vāṇāgnilokamiti pañcatriṃśadadhikatriṃśaddinagaṇaṃ tyajati triva- rṣebhyaḥ
tatastatpatraṃ śūnyīkṛtya dvitīyapatre avaśeṣadinagaṇaṃ gṛhītvā prāṇaśaktiḥ praviśati,
tasmād dinagaṇāt sārohaṇaṃ pūrvādaparapatrarohaṇadinairdaśabhiḥ sārddhaṃ śarayugaśikhinamiti pañcacatvāriṃśadadhikatriśatadinagaṇaṃ tyajati dvitīyapatre
tato'vaśeṣaṃ gṛhītvā tṛtīyapatre praviśati, dvitīyaṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
punastatraiva pañcadaśārohaṇādinaiḥ sārddhaṃ saptatyadhikaśatadinaṃ tṛtīyapatre tyajati
avaśeṣaṃ gṛhītvā caturthe praviśati, tṛtīyaṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
tato viṃśa- tyārohaṇādinaiḥ sārddhaṃ netrāhiriti dvayaśītidinagaṇaṃ tyajati caturthapatre
avaśeṣaṃ gṛhītvā pañcame praviśati, caturthaṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
punaḥ pañcaviṃśatyārohaṇadinaiḥ sārddhaṃ śelavāṇaṃ saptapañcāśaddinagaṇaṃ tyajati pañcame patre
avaśeṣaṃ gṛhītvā ṣaṣṭhe praviśati, pañcamaṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
punaḥ ṣaḍviṃśatyārohaṇadinaiḥ sārddhaṃ aṣṭāviṃśatidinagaṇaṃ tyajati ṣaṣṭhe patre tataḥ saptame patre praviśati, ṣaṣṭhaṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
tataḥ saptame rohaṇaṃ saptaviṃśatidina- gaṇa tyajati
avaśeṣaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśaddinagaṇaṃ gṛhītvā aṣṭame patre praviśati, saptamaṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
tato'ṣṭame pañcadaśadinaṃ tyajati
avaśeṣamekaviśaddinaṃ gṛhītatvā navame pravi- śati, aṣṭamaṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
tato navame daśadinaṃ tyajati
avaśeṣamekādaśadinaṃ gṛhītvā daśame praviśati, navamaṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
tato daśame pañcadinaṃ tyajati
avaśeṣaṃ ṣaḍdinaṃ gṛhītvā ekādaśe praviśati, daśamaṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
tatra dinatrayaṃ tyajati
avaśeṣaṃ dinatrayaṃ gṛhītvā dvādaśame praviśati, ekādaśamaṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
tatra dinadvayaṃ tyajati
avaśeṣaṃ dina- mekaṃ gṛhītvā kaṇikāyāṃ praviśati, dvādaśamaṃ nāḍīrāśipatraṃ śūnyaṃ bhavati
evamaśītyu- ttarasahasrasaṃkhyaiḥ sūryasya madhyamātrivarṣadinaiḥ prāṇaśaktirdvādaśāraṃ rāśicakraṃ tyajati
tataḥ kaṇikāyāṃ dinamekaṃ madhyamā vahati
tataḥ śvāsacakraṃ tyaktvā vijñānaṃ prāṇavāyunā sārddhamanyatra ṣaḍgatau saṃkramaṇaṃ karoti, iti sūryasyāriṣṭaniyamaḥ
atra nyāsaḥ pūrvanyāsavat dvipuṭe varṣabhedena dinatyāgabhedeneti pūrvapuṭe ariṣṭadi- nanyāso'parapuṭe'vaśeṣāyurdinanyāsaḥ dvitīyacakre dinatyāganyāsaḥ, pūrvapatreṣu krameṇa śūnya nyāsa iti sūryāriṣṭadakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ samalagne jātānāṃ viṣamalagne jātānāṃ candrāriṣṭa- malpāyūnām, iti sūryāriṣṭaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ madhyamāriṣṭaṃ kālābdakrameṇa sūryasya yad bālajanai stīrthikairuktasattvānāṃ vyāmohajanakaṃ varṣaśatāvadhestasya pratiṣedha ucyate- iha tīrthikaiḥ kilocyate dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ pañcanāḍīpravāheṇa va rṣaśatamāyuḥ, ṣaṣṭhanāḍīpravāheṇa navativarṣāṇyāyuḥ, saptanāḍīpravāheṇāśītivarṣāṇyāyuḥ, aṣṭanāḍīpravāheṇa pañcāśadvarṣāṇyāyuḥ, navanāḍīpravāheṇa trayastriṃśadvarṣāṇyāyuḥ, daśanāḍīpravāheṇa ṣaḍvi- śaḍvarṣāṇyāyuḥ, pañcadaśanāḍīpravāheṇa caturdaśavarṣāṇyāyuḥ, triśannāḍīpravāheṇa dvādaśa- varṣāṇyāyuḥ, ahorātrapravāheṇa daśavarṣāṇyāyuḥ, dvidinapravāheṇāṣṭavarṣāṇyāyuḥ, tridinapravāheṇa ṣaḍvarṣāṇyāyuḥ, caturdinapravāheṇa pañcavarṣāṇyāyuriti; tataḥ pañcadinapravāheṇa trivarṣāṇyāyu- riti tīthikānāṃ nāḍīśvāsaniyamaḥ dakṣiṇamārge |
sa eva na ghaṭate yuktyā vicāryamāṇaḥ sarvajñoktyā
iha narāṇāmariṣṭābhāve vāma- dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ pañca sameṣu viṣameṣu lagneṣu pañca daṇḍāni prāṇavāyurvahati
varṣaśatāyuṣaḥ puruṣasya prāṇasya vāme savye saptavarṣāṇi śvāsavṛddhirayanabhedena, tataḥ ṣaṣṭivarṣāṇi pāṇīpala- vṛddhirayanavṛddhibhedena, tatastriśadvarṣāṇi nāḍīvṛddhistrimāsabhedena tatastrivarṣatripakṣāṇi dinabhedena vṛddhiriti kālavṛddhinyāyaḥ
ato nyāyāt yasyākālamaraṇaṃ bhavati garbhotpādā- dvālasya trivarṣaiḥ pañcavarṣairvā dvā' daśavarṣervā viṃśatyādibhirvā maraṇam, tasya pañcanāḍyupari ṣaṭsaptādināḍīpravāhakramo nirarthakaḥ, garbhotpādatrivarṣamaraṇāvadheriti |
atra yadi māsā - dūdhvaṃ jātakasya maraṇaṃ bhavati, tadā janmadinādārabhya prāṇāvāyuḥ pauṣṇakālāt tithidigi- ṣuguṇādyamārūḍhaḥ
evaṃ dvimāsānte maraṇaṃ trimāsānte caturmāsānte ṣaḍmāsānte eka- varṣānte dvivarṣānte trivarṣānte garbhotpādāttrayastriṃśaddinārohaṇavilomena yāvat pañcadinā- rohaṇaṃ dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ śabdādipañcaguṇakṣayabhedena vāmanāḍyāṃ dinamekaṃ sattvāditriguṇakṣaya- bhedeneti
evaṃ garbhotpādāt prathame'pi maraṇaṃ jñeyaṃ kaṇikāyāṃ prāṇapraveśāditi āhorātrataḥ, evaṃ tṛtīye dine maraṇaṃ dvādaśame patre prāṇodayāt, evaṃ ṣaṣṭhe dine ekādaśe patre prāṇodayāt, evamekādaśe dine daśame rāśipatre prāṇodayāt, evamekaviṃśatime dine navame rāśipatre prāṇodayāt, evaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśatime dine aṣṭame rāśipatre krameṇa prāṇodayāt
evaṃ dvimāse saptame rāśipatre prāṇodayāt, evaṃ trimāse ṣaṣṭhame patre prāṇodayāt, evaṃ caturmāse pañcame prāṇodayāt, evaṃ ṣaṇmāse viṃśatidinaiḥ caturthapatre prāṇodayāt, evaṃ sapakṣa ' varṣaike tṛtīye prāṇodayāt, dvivarṣe daśadinaiḥ dvitīye prāṇodayāt, tṛtīye varṣe prathame prāṇo- dayāt, caturthe varṣe (na) dinanyāya: garbhotpādācchatavarṣāvadhiṃ yāvaditi sūryāriṣṭa- niyamaḥ
yathā sūryastathā sattvādiguṇatrayabhedena garbhotpādāccandrāriṣṭaniyamaḥ, tathā kālo- ttare ūrdhvaśrotre uktamīśvareṇa- yathā vāmā tathā'vāmā madhyamā ca tathaiva ca | trivarṣānte makhādā | iti parasiddhānte'pi kutracinniyamo'stīticandrasūryāriṣṭaniyamaḥ
evaṃ trivarṣāntamekadinamārabhya bālasya yanmaraṇaṃ candramārge vā sūryamārge vā tada- śītyuttarasahasrabhedabhinnaṃ pratyekadinamaraṇabhedena, nātra niyamo garbhajānāṃ karmaṇaḥ
kintu nāḍikāśvāsapravāheṇāriṣṭadinaiḥ prāṇasaṃkhyā jñāyate
ato ( s) riṣṭacakraṃ maraṇacakraṃ rāśi- cakranāḍībhyaḥ prāṇasya pañcamaṇḍalapravāhaparityāgo'nukrameṇeti
tataḥ kaṇikāyāṃ praveśa- staddine maraṇamiti nyāyaḥ
idaṃ trivarṣakālalakṣaṇaṃ śatavarṣāvadhermadhyamāpravāheṇa sūryasya parikalpitena sarvasattvānāṃ maraṇaṃ kila bhagavatoktam
tadeva candrasya tripakṣadharmeṇa candra- syāriṣṭamaraṇaṃ na syāditi
iha yena candrasya dharmeṇāriṣṭamaraṇaṃ na bhavati tena bālaira- prabuddhaḥ prakalpitam
yathā vāmanāḍyāmariṣṭamaraṇaṃ nāsti candradharmataḥ dakṣiṇāyāṃ sūrya- pravāhato'riṣṭamaraṇamuktaṃ sarvajñeneti ajñānināṃ vākyam
iha trivarṣādhidevaḥ sūryaśabdena prāṇavāyuścandraśabdenāpānavāyustripakṣādhipatiḥ
aḍāpiṅgalāsuṣumnāmadhi patiḥ prāṇaḥ sūryo nābherūrdhvaṃ pravāhataḥ, apāno viṇmūtracandranāḍīnāmadhipatiḥ nābheradhaḥ ariṣṭamaraṇalakṣaṇa- nāḍīccheda-mahoddeśaḥ pravāhataḥ
evaṃ candrasyādho guṇatrayam, sūryasyordhve pañcamaṇḍalaviṣayaguṇapañcakam
tena triguṇabhedena vāme candrāriṣṭam, dakṣiṇe pañcaguṇabhedena sūryāriṣṭaḥ adhordhvanāḍyoriti candrasūryāriṣṭaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ madhyamāparipūrṇakālamaraṇalakṣaṇamucyate kālābdamityādinā - kālābdaṃ yāvadekā divasagativaśād rohate saṃkramantī candrākhye sūryamārge viṣamasamadinerekavṛddhyā krameṇa
bhūyaḥ saṃkrāntibhedo viṣamasamadinerdvādaśāre karoti sārddhaṃ māsaṃ hi yāvat triguṇitadaśakaṃ jīvitaṃ ca trirātram ||
iha śarīre naranārīṇāṃ sattvarajastamobhedena prāṇapravāhaḥ
sārddhadaśamāsādhika- ṣaḍnavativarṣāṇi yāvad vāmadakṣiṇe pañcamaṇḍalapravāhataḥ
trivarṣatripakṣāṇi (n) yāvat madhyamakālaḥ varṣaśatāyuṣāṃ narāṇāmiti niyamaḥ
tatra vāmadakṣiṇamadhyamāstisro nāḍyaḥ sattvarajastamaḥ svabhāvinyaḥ; āsu mṛtyustamonāḍīpravāhasaṃkhyāṃ gṛhītvā prāṇāpānasvabhāvena
vāmāyāṃ dakṣiṇāyāṃ viṣamasamadine rohate kālābdaṃ trivarṣatripakṣaṃ yāvat divasagati- vaśāt divasāḥ pañcaviṃśatyadhikaikādaśaśatāḥ, teṣāṃ gativaśādyāvadāyuḥkṣayo bhavati tāvadārohate saṃkramanniti (ntī ) candrākhye vāmamārge sūryamārge dakṣiṇe viṣamasama dinai reka
vṛddhayā krameṇeti ekadinaṃ vāmanāḍyāṃ rohate yadi meṣādiviṣamalagne janmos (T) bhūt; dakṣiṇe dinadvayamārohate yadi vṛṣabhādisamalagne janmos (T) bhūt
tato bhūyaḥ saṃkrāntibhedaṃ viṣamasamadinairvāmārohaṇāvasāne dakṣiṇārohaṇāvasāne ca karoti ārohaṇadinamā- neneti
dvādaśāre rāśicakre krameṇa pañcamaṇḍalaparityāgāya vāmanāḍyāmākāśādi- maṇḍalakṣayārthaṃ saṃkrānti karoti, dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ pṛthivyādimaṇḍalakṣayārthaṃ karoti
atra vāme pañcadinārohaṇena saṃkrāntidinairmeṣādiviṣamalagneṣu ṣaṭsu śunyamaṇḍalapravāhaṃ prāṇa- vāyuḥ tyajati dakṣiṇe ṣadinārohaṇena ṣaṭsaṃkrāntidinairvṛṣabhādisamalagneṣu ṣaṭsu pṛthivīmaṇḍalapravāhaṃ tyajati prāṇaḥ
evaṃ vāmanāḍyāmekādaśadinairvāyumaṇḍalaṃ tyajati, dakṣiṇadaleṣu dvādaśadinairudakamaṇḍalaṃ tyajati
evaṃ saptadaśadinairvāmadaleṣu tejomaṇḍalaṃ tyajati; dakṣiṇadaleṣu aṣṭādaśadinaistejomaṇḍalaṃ tyajati
bhūyo vāmadaleṣu trayoviṃśati - dinairudakamaṇḍalaṃ tyajati; dakṣiṇadaleṣu caturviṃśatidinairvāyumaṇḍalaṃ tyajati
punarekona- viśaddinairvāmadaleṣu ṣaṭsu pṛthvīmaṇḍalaṃ tyajati
dakṣiṇe triṃśaddinairākāśamaṇḍalaṃ tyajati
evaṃ viṣamasamadinairvāmadakṣiṇārohaṇeneti' triṃśaddinānāṃ triṃśatsaṃkrāntidinairdvādaśalagna- nāḍīdaleṣu pañcamaṇḍalaparityāgaḥ
tataḥ ekatriṃśaddinārohaṇena vāmanāḍyāṃ viṣamadaleṣu sattvadhātukṣayaḥ
ekatriṃśatsaṃkrāntidinairapi dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ dvātriṃśaddinārohaṇena rajodhātukṣayaḥ
dvātriṃśat saṃkrāntidinairapi
tatastrayastriṃśaddinārohaṇena viṣama- daleṣu tamodhātukṣayaḥ, trayastriśatasaṃkrāntidinairiti
evaṃ śleṣmanāḍīkṣayaḥ vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- sattvadhātuḥ, pittanāḍīkṣayo rajodhātuḥ, vātanāḍīkṣayastamodhāturityucyate
trayastri- śaddinārohaṇena trayastriṃśat saṃkrāntidinairiti
ubhayā rāśisaṃkrāntisaṃkalita- dinairdvāviṃśadadhikaikādaśaśatadināni bhavanti trivarṣatripakṣadinatrayonānāmiti pañcamaṇḍalasattvarajastamonāḍīkācchedaḥ
prāṇavāyoḥ sthānaparityāga iti
evaṃ sārddhaṃ māsaṃ hi yāvat trivarṣadināni prāṇārohaṇeneti
tatastridaśakamaparaṃ garbhādhānamāsadinagaṇaṃ triṃśaddinātmakaṃ jīvitasya trirātraṃ yāvaditi dakṣiṇānāḍyāmārohaṇaṃ sarvasaṃkrāntyabhāvaḥ trayastriṃśaddināni yāvat skandhadhātūnāṃ vicchedaḥ
atra rūpādayaḥ pañca skandhāḥ pṛthivyādayaḥ pañca dhātavaḥ kāyendriyāṇi pañcendriyāṇi gandhādyāḥ pañca viṣayāḥ, gudādyāni pañca karmendriyāṇi ālāpādayaḥ pañca karmendriyaviṣayāḥ, eteṣāṃ trayastriṃśaddinaiḥ cchedo bhavati parasparasaṃyogābhāvaḥ, tataḥ ekadinaṃ madhyamāyāṃ viśati śvāsacakrakṣayārtham
ṣaṣṭhaḥ skandhaḥ, ṣaṣṭho dhātuḥ, ṣaṣṭhamindriyam, ṣaṣṭho viṣayaḥ, ṣaṣṭhaṃ karmendriyam, ṣaṣṭhakarmendriyaviṣayaḥ, śukracyutirmaraṇānte bhavatīti skandhadhātvāyatanavicchedaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ jātakānāṃ mṛtyuśvāsārohaṇamucyate śvāsāmityādinā - śvāsāṃlliptāṃśca nāḍī dinaniśisamayān rohate saṃkrameṇa saptābdaiścāyanāṅgādayanagativaśāt ṣaṣṭisaṃvatsareśca
triśadvarṣestrimāsān divasagativaśāt kālavarṣaiśca pakṣa- dvatriṃśat sārddhabhaktarbhavati dinagaṇai rohaṇaṃ kālacakrāt // 66
iha śarīre śvāsacakraṃ liptācakraṃ ghaṭikācakraṃ dinacakraṃ yathā bāhye tathā dehe'pi
tatra ṣaṭśvāsātmakaṃ śvāsacakram, ṣaṣṭipāṇīpalātmakaṃ pāṇīpalacakram, ṣaṣṭighaṭikātmakaṃ ghaṭikācakram; aśītyuttarasahasradinātmakamariṣṭacakram, ṣaṭpañcāśadadhikaikādaśaśata- dinātmakaṃ kālamṛtyudinacakramiti
eṣu cakreṣu mṛtyuḥ śvāsarūpeṇa pāṇīpalasvabhāvena ghaṭikāsvabhāvena dinasvabhāvenārohati vāmanāḍyāṃ dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ yathāsaṃkhyamekottareṇa
prathamaṃ tāvat śvāsānārohate
saptāndaiścāyanāṃkāditi
ihotpannasya yasyāriṣṭaṃ varṣaśatāvadhernāsti kālamaraṇaṃ bhaviṣyati, tasyāyanadvayaṃ vāma nāḍyāṃ dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ pañcamaṇḍalavāhaka: prāṇavāyurvahati

ekonaṣaṣṭiliptān yāvad vāmanāḍyāmākāśamaṇḍale, dakṣiṇanāḍyāṃ pṛthivī - maṇḍale dvitīyādinā samāni ṣaṣṭipāṇīpalāni yāvat ṣaṭsu ṣaṭsu daleṣu viṣamasamalagna- svabhāveṣviti

I peṭale ] ariṣṭamaraṇalakṣaṇa- nāḍīccheda-mahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ candrasūryacārakṣayeṇa kālavṛddhirucyate ṣatriṃśadbhirityādinā - ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ sahasraṃ dhruśatadinagaṇaṃ rohate kālanāḍī yāṃ yāṃ sūryenduśaktidina niśisamaye pūritaṃ svasvacāraiḥ

sūryā'haḥ sūryacāre tvṛṇamapi ca bhaveccandracāre tadarddha
nakṣatrāhaḥ prabhede raviśaśicaraṇaṃ śūnyamāse ca mṛtyoḥ || 70 ||
iha varṣaśate dinagaṇe kṛte ṣaṭtraṃśatsahastradināni bhavanti
dhruriti varṣasaṃjñā, taiḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ sahasai dhruśatadinagaṇai rohate kālanāḍī, mṛtyuḥ nāḍīṣu varddhate dvāsaptati- sahasreṣu mṛtyuvāyuḥ praviśati
pañcamaṇḍalavāyuḥ kramato'pasarati ṣaṣṭhāṃśaṃ tatra sthāpayi- tveti
kāṃ rohate kālanāḍīvāyuḥ ?
yāṃ yāṃ pūritaṃ sūryendorna śaktidina niśisamaye svasvacārairnakṣatracāreṇa sūryasyāśaktiriti, tithicaraṇaiścandrasyāśaktiriti
sūryā'ha iti dvādaśadināni sūryacāre tvaṇamapi bhaveccandracāre tadarddha ṣaḍdināni
atra pratimāse triśaddinairna rāśimekaṃ sūryaśca rati, na candraḥ ṣaṣṭidaṇḍāniti dyau carati
ato nakṣatrāhaḥ prabhedai raviśaśicaraṇaṃ śūnyamāse ca mṛtyoḥ, tayoryatrāśaktiḥ sa śūnyamāso dvātriṃśanmāsānte, sa ca catuḥṣaṣṭīnāḍyātmaka iti
tāsu catuḥṣaṣṭināḍīṣu śūnyavāyuḥ prarohate, nābhikamale dvāsaptatisahasrāṇāṃ madhye pratyahaṃ nāḍīdvayaṃ rohate iti sāmānyaśchūla- (sthūla ) niyamaḥ
idānīṃ pratyahaṃ śvāsabhedenārohaṇamucyate prāṇā ityādinā - prāṇā dehe'dhikā ye prakaṭitaviṣuve vedapādonaṣaṣṭi: sarve saṃkrāntibhedaiḥ pratidinasamaye nāḍiyugmaṃ nihanti
bhūyo bhūyo'bdamadhye raviśaśicaraṇāt śūnyanetrādrisaṃkhyā evaṃ kālaḥ śatābdaiḥ kramati suranṛṇāṃ svāyuṣaṃ svasvamānaiḥ || 71 ||
iha ṣaṭśatādhikaikaviṃśatsahasrāṇāṃ madhye ye jñānamaṇḍalavāhino bhavanti te dehe śvāsādhikāra ityucyante
prakaṭitaviṣuve ubhayalagnamadhye pāde vedonaṣaṣṭiḥ sapādaṣaṭ- pañcāśaditi; te sarve dvādaśasaṃkrāntibhedaiḥ pañcasaptatyadhikapuṭaśataṃ bhūtvā pratidinasamaye nāḍīyugmaṃ nihantītyāgamapāṭhaḥ; ghnantīti rūpam
idaṃ nāḍīyugmaṃ vāme dakṣiṇe dvāsaptati nāḍīsahasrāṇāṃ madhye nāḍīyugmaṃ ghnanti pañcamaṇḍalavāyusañcāravināśo bhavatīti

evaṃ kālaḥ śatābdaiḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadbhiḥ sahasradinaiḥ kramati
suranṛṇāṃ vā (svā) puṣa [ ] dvāsaptatināḍī sahasraṃ pañca [ ]maṇḍala- vāhakam, candrādityacaraṇamiti
svasvamānaiḥ pūrvoktaḥ sūkṣmatanu ' [ja] bhūtadevādidinairiti nāḍīcchedaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ kālanāḍīsvabhāva ucyate daṣṭamityādinā - daṣṭaṃ vyādhiḥ prahāro yadi bhavati nṛṇāṃ kālanāḍyāṃ kadācit śīghra tenāśrayeṇa praviśati sahasā mūlacakreṣu kālaḥ
ruddhvā cakreṣu nāḍī raviśaśigamanaṃ chedayitvā samastaṃ madhye sūkṣmāśritaṃ yad harati narapate jīvitaṃ prāṇināṃ ca || 72 ||
iha śarīre kālanāḍyāṃ madhyamāpravāhakāle yadi daṣṭaṃ bhavati, vyādhirbhavati, prahāro vā nṛṇāṃ kadācit; tadā śīghraṃ tenāśrayeṇa praviśati sahasā mūlacakreṣu ṣaṭsu kālo mahātamo vāyuḥ
sa ca praviṣṭaḥ san ṣaṭcakreṣu ṣaṭpañcāśadama [ dhi ] kanāḍīśatam, asau ruddhvā raviśaśigamanaṃ savyetaranāḍyāṃ chedayitvā samastaṃ madhye sūkṣmāśritam, avadhūtīnāḍyāśritaṃ yajjīvitaṃ prāṇavāyuḥ tad haratira narapate jīvitaṃ prāṇināmiti kālanāḍīniyamaḥ
idānīṃ dharmacakrādo candracaraṇānyucyante hṛtpadma ityādinā - hṛtpadme śrīlalāṭe carati śaśipadaṃ bhūtadigvāsarākhyaṃ sambhoge nābhicakre navadaśasahitaṃ rudrayugmaṃ jinākhyam
tattvākhyaṃ saptarātrāttyajati punarasāvutkraman yaḥ śaśāṅkī bindī pūrṇāṃ prakṛtya praviśati hṛdaye śuklapakṣe krameṇa || 73 ||
iha candrasya caraṇabhedena caturdaśabhāgena samaviṣamagatena tithiṣu vṛddhirhānirvā; sā saptamadine saptatithiṣu [ ] pañcaviṣayaguṇā bālakumārādibhedena saptame dine vṛddhi- rhānirvā nivartate viṃśatyadhikaśatacaraṇeṣu pūrṇeṣu
atra viṃśatyadhikaśatāṃśānyucyante- iha caturdaśabhāgāvaśeṣe ekacaraṇe dṛṣṭe taddinakalāyāḥ ṣaṣṭināḍikāṃśānāṃ madhye pañcanāḍikāṃśāḥ kalāyā dhane'dhikā bhavanti, kṛṣṇāyāḥ śuklāyā vā ṛṇasthāne hīnā bhavanti
dvitīye dine dvitīyāyāḥ pañcāṃśāḥ, prathamāyāḥ kumārabhedena dviguṇā jñātavyā ka. kha. tatra; bho. Lus sKyes ( tanuja )

ka. kha. 0 varaṇā | paṭale ] ariṣṭamaraṇalakṣaṇa- nāḍīccheda-mahoddeśaḥ iti
evaṃ tṛtīyadine tṛtīyāyāḥ pañcāṃśāḥ evaṃ dvitīyāyāḥ daśa, prathamāyā vā[yu ] bhedena pañcadaśa
evaṃ bālaprabhedena caturthyāḥ pañcāṃśāḥ, tṛtīyāyā daśa, dvitīyāyāḥ pañcadaśa, prathamāyā yuvābhedenaikonaviṃśatiḥ
evaṃ pañcame dine pañcamyāṃ (:) pañcāṃśāḥ, caturthyā daśa, tṛtīyāyāḥ pañcadaśa; dvitīyāyā ekonaviṃśatiḥ, prathamāyā vṛddhabhedena dvāviṃśatiḥ
evaṃ ṣaṣṭhe dine ṣaṣṭhyāḥ pañcāṃśāḥ, pañcamyā daśa, caturthyāḥ pañcadaśa, tṛtīyāyā ekonaviṃśatiḥ, dvitīyāyā dvāviṃśatiḥ prathamāyā ativṛddhi (ddha) bhedena caturviṃśatiḥ
evaṃ saptame dine saptamyāḥ pañcāṃśāḥ, ṣaṣṭhyā daśa, pañcamyā pañcadaśa, caturthyā ekonaviṃśatiḥ, tṛtīyāyā dvāviṃśatiḥ, dvitīyāyāḥ caturviṃśatiḥ, prathamāyāḥ pakvāvasthābhedena saptame dine pañcaviṃśa- tyaṃśāḥ dhanaṃ vā ṛṇaṃ vā caraṇavaśād bhavanti
evaṃ bālādikrameṇa sarveṣāṃ prameyo jñeyaḥ
evaṃ saptatithīnāṃ caraṇāṃśān gṛhītvā hṛtkamalādiṣu viṃśatyadhikaśatadaleṣu śaśāṅkaḥ kramati, tataḥ punarutkramati
śaśipaḍhaṃ svakīyaṃ padamityarthaḥ
hṛdaye'ṣṭadaleṣvaṣṭāṃśāḥ, ਚਲਾਣੇ ṣoḍaśadaleṣu ṣoḍaśāṃśāḥ, sambhoge dvātriṃśadaleṣu dvātriṃśadaṃśāḥ, nābhikamale catuḥṣaṣṭidaleṣu catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśāḥ
evaṃ viṃśatyadhikaśatāṃśān bhuktvā candrakalāyā hānirvā vṛddhirvā nivartate
hṛtpadme lalāṭe ca bhūtadigvāsarākhyaṃ sambhoge nābhau ca navadaśasahitaṃ rudrayugmaṃ jinākhyam
tattvākhyaṃ saptarātrāttyajati punarasau utkraman yaḥ śaśāṅkaḥ
prathamakalāyāḥ pañcaviṃśatyaṃśāḥ paripakvāyā bhavanti sā cāṣṭame dine nā[ ]bhāva [[]dhidevatā bhavati
punaḥ pūrṇamāsyāṃ nābhihṛtkaṇṭhalalāṭeṣu pūrṇaṃ karoti
bindusthāne bindoṃ pūrṇāṃ prakṛtya iti, tato nābheḥ punarutkraman catuḥṣaṣṭidaleṣu catuḥṣaṣṭyaṃśāḥ, hṛdaye'ṣṭāṃśāḥ, kaṇṭhe dvātriṃśadaṃśāḥ, lalāṭe ṣoḍaśāṃśāḥ evaṃ caturdaśadinaiḥ padānāṃ pūrṇaṃ bhavati
