ataḥ śatābdāyuḥ trailokye nāsti yogī surabhujaganṛṇāṃ pūrituṃ yaḥ samartha candrādityo svadehe carati dinagaṇaṃ naiva candrasvacāraiḥ sūryaścarkṣāṇi sarvāṇi carati bhuvane varṣe varṣe tvṛṇena grasati sabhuvanaṃ varṣamadhye krameṇa kālacakra kavīraḥ || 78 ||
iha trailokye surabhujaganṛṇāṃ madhye yogī nāsti yaḥ samarthaḥ pūritu candrādityau svadehe tat kasmāddhetoḥ yataḥ svacāraiścandraḥ ṣaṣṭyuttaratriśatadinagaṇaṃ na carati varṣā vadheḥ sūryaścarkṣāṇi sarvāṇi saptaviṃśanna carati bhuvane śarīre varṣamadhye krameṇa pūrvokta vidhinā ataḥ prativarṣāt prativarṣe ṛṝṇena grasati sabhuvanaṃ śarīram kālacakrakavīra utpādakṣayahetubhūtaḥ sāriṇāṃ kālacakrekavīraḥ ataḥ sa sādhanīyaścandrārkau paṭale ariṣṭamaraṇalakṣaṇa nāḍīccheda-mahoddeśaḥ 213 pūrayitvā bodhicittarajo dharmoṃ prāṇāpānau pūrayitvā yogineti niyamo buddhasya bhagavataḥ idānīṃ mūtrādidhātuvikāralakṣaṇamucyate ariṣṭavaśāt chinnetyādinā - chinnā yadyekanāḍī bhavati narapate mūtramambutva meti tanmātrān pañcarātrāṃstyajati parakalā piṇḍavicchedakāle śabdaṃ karṇe rasañca tyajati khalu mukhe tārakāmakṣimadhye gandhaṃ ghrāṇe narāṇāṃ karacaraṇatanau coṣṇabhāvaṃ krameṇa || 79 ||
iha śarīre chinnā yadyekanāḍī vāmā dakṣiṇā vā prāṇapravāharahitā bhavati narapate ityāmantraṇam mūtramambutva 'meti ṣaṇmāsāvadheḥ tanmātrān pañca rātrān tyajati parakalā prāṇaśaktiḥ ṣaḍ dināvadheryathākramam pratidine piṇḍavicchedakāle śabdaviṣayaṃ karṇāt tyajati karṇa ityāgamapāṭhāt pañcamyarthe saptamīti rasaṃ ca tyajati
khalu mukhe jihvāyā iti pañcadināvadheḥ tārakāmakṣimadhyāt caturdināvadheḥ gandhaṃ ghrāṇāt tridināvadheḥ narāṇāṃ karacaraṇatanāvuṣṇatvabhāvaḥ sparśabhāvaḥ dvidināvadheḥ antadine dharmadhātuṃ śukracyuti tyajati śvāsacakraṃ ceti aparaṣaḍdinābhyantare mṛtyulakṣaṇamucyate- taddine mṛtyureva || 80 ||
nāsāgraṃ lambamānaṃ śirasi dhṛtabhujo dṛśyate tatsvarūpaṃ lākṣārāgapraghṛṣṭo'pyubhayakaratale naiva rāgaṃ karoti ādityaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇaḥ pariṇataśaśabhṛd dṛśyate pītavarṇaḥ mūtraṃ dehaśca śīto bhavati narapate iha śarīre maraṇe praviṣṭe svakīyaṃ nāsāgraṃ lambamānaṃ hasti karākāraṃ dṛśyate śirasi dhṛtabhujaḥ svakīyaṃ tatsvarūpaṃ dṛ śyate na sūkṣmo dṛśyate tathā lākṣārāga praghṛṣṭo'pyubhayakaratale naiva rāgaṃ karoti karatalacarmaṇi ādityaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇo dṛśyate pariṇataśaśabhṛt pūrṇimācandro dṛśyate sampūrṇa pīta iti ṣaḍdināvadheḥ tato yasmin dine mūtraṃ dehaśca śīto bhavati tadvidane tasmin dine mṛtyureva narapate ityāmantraṇam aparamapi ṣaḍdinābhyantare mṛtyulakṣaṇamucyate - jihvādha kālasūtraṃ prabhavati nayane brahmarekhātisūkṣmā śvāsaścandrārkamārge sphurati narapate dve kapole tathaiva 214 vimalaprabhāyāṃ adhyātma- kakṣāviṣṭaṃ stanordhvaṃ grahagaṇasakalaṃ dṛśyate sasphuliṅga stabdhagrīvā sagātrā bhramaṇamapi tanau lakṣaṇaṃ cāṣṭamṛtyoḥ || 81 ||
ihāriṣṭamaraṇākrāntasya jihvādhaḥ kālasūtraṃ kṛṣṇarekhā bhavati tathā nayane brahmarekhātisa ūkṣmā keśapramāṇarekhā kṛṣṇaraktamaṇḍalabhedinī bhavati śvāsaḥ candrārkamārge sphurati dve kapole sphurataḥ tathaiva kakṣāviṣṭaṃ stanordhvam nāḍyau kakṣāviṣṭau bhavataḥ dṛśyate na grahagaṇasakalaṃ dṛśyate sasphuliṅgam stabdhagrīvā tasya sagātrā hastapādasahitā niśceṣṭā bhramaṇamapi tanau bhavati lakṣaṇaṃ cāṣṭa mṛtyoretaduktaṃ bhavati aparamapi yat kiñcit viparītaṃ dṛśyate śarīre bāhye vā chāyāpuruṣādikaṃ tat sarvaṃ maraṇalakṣaṇaṃ yoginā veditavyam tat jñātvā dānādikaṃ kāryam yadi puṇyabalena jīvati tathāpi śobhanam atha maraṇaṃ gacchati tathāpi yad dattaṃ tasya phalamanubhuṅkte ataḥ ubhayapakṣato dānaṃ deyaṃ saugataiḥ sukhārthibhiriti bhagavato niyamaḥ iti śrīmūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyām ariṣṭamaraṇalakṣaṇa - nāḍīccheda-mahoddeśaḥ kṣaṇalakṣaṇa - kālacakra niyama mahoddeśaḥ caturthaḥ || 4 ||
idānīṃ lokasaṃvṛtyāṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhāralakṣaṇa kartocyate utpattimityādinā — utpattiṃ yaḥ karoti prasavanasamayaṃ bālakaumārarūpaṃ saṃhāraḥ saiva loke punarapi kurute kālacakrī na sūryaḥ candrādityādidevān grasati sa bhagavān rātribhāge sabhūtān bhūyo muñcantyanantānapi dinasamaye sṛṣṭihetoḥ krameṇa || 82 ||
iha saṃsāre yaḥ cyutikṣaṇaḥ sattvānāmutpati karoti rajaḥ śukrasaṃyuktam ālaya vijñānadharmī garbhe tato garbhāse prasavanasamayaṃ karoti bālakaumārādi rūpaṃ karoti śarīrasya sṛṣṭisa hāra sa evya cyutikṣaṇo loke svargamartyapātāle punarapi kurute kālacakro na sūryaḥ sūryo rajaḥ candraḥ śukram ālayavijñānena vinā kālacakreṇa vinā na karotītyarthaḥ iha kasmānna karoti rajaḥ śukradharmī sūryacandra iti iha sarvatrāgnisūrya somātmakaṃ jagaditi prasiddhaṃ vākyamityucyate iha yataścandrā dityādidevān grasati sa bhagavān rātribhāge sabhūtāniti iha saṃsāre rātribhāgaścandraḥ sṛṣṭiḥ divābhāgaḥ sūryaḥ saṃhārastān atra rātribhāge utpattikāle sa ālayavijñānalakṣaṇo ' kṣaṇalakṣaṇa- kālacakraniyamamahoddeśaḥ mātṛgarbhe candraḥ śukram ādityo rajaḥ; ādiśabdena viṇmūtrasambhūtāni pṛthivyaptejovāyvā- kāśadhātusahitān grasati rajaḥ śukrādhārasthitaḥ kāyavākcittaniṣpādanārtham, candraḥ śukramīśvaraḥ, rajaḥ sūryaḥ sadāśiva:, majjā rudraḥ, mūtraṃ viṣṇuḥ, brahmā viḍiti |
candrādi- tyādidevān grasati rātribhāge utpattikāle |

sṛṣṭihetoḥ punara- parotpādahetormuñcati, krameṇa pṛthivyādidhātuparityāgena nābhihṛtkaṇṭhalalāṭoṣṇīṣādhāre krameṇeti saṃvṛtikartāniyamaḥ |

idānīṃ saṃsārabandha ucyate badhnātyātmetyādinā - [ adhyātma- badhnātyātmā vikalpaiḥ prakṛtigataguṇaiḥ kośakīṭo yathaiva ātmānaṃ cātmanā vai punarapi manasā muñcate nirvikalpāt |
tasmād rājan svakarma prakṛtiguṇagataṃ duḥkhasaukhyaṃ karoti kaḥ kartā kiṃ karoti prakṛtivirahito muhyate'jñānalokaḥ || 85 ||

idānīṃ pṛthivyādi - aṣṭavidhā prakṛtirucyate pṛthvītyādinā - pṛthvītoyāgnivātā gaganamapi mano buddhyahaṅkāracittaṃ sthūlā sūkṣmā parā ca trividhaguṇagatā varṇabindvādibhedaiḥ
sthūlāsthūlendriyeṣu prakṛtiradhigatā sūkṣmacitte ca sūkṣmā jñāne ca jñānamūttiḥ prakṛtiravikṛtiḥ jīvabhūtā na bhūtā || 86 ||
iha śarīre ādāvaṣṭavidhā prakṛtiḥ, pṛthivyaptejovāyvākāśamanobuddhyahaṅkārā- tmikāṣṭavidheti saṃsāracittasya
eṣāṃ punastridhā sthūlā sūkṣmā parā ca trividhaguṇagatā sattvarajastamoguṇeṣu gatā guṇagatā iti varṇabindrādibhedaiḥ sthūlāsthūlendriyeṣu gatā jāgrada- vasthādharmiṇī, sthūlā jāgradbhāvagrahaṇāditi sthūlacittasya jaḍavāsanāgrāhakasyeti
sūkṣmacittasya sūkṣmā svapnāvasthārdhāmaṇī, manomayendriyairmāyopamabhāvagrahaṇāditi
sūkṣmā parā ca sarvendriyanirodhalakṣaṇā suṣuptāvasthārdhāmaṇī, parā sarvabhāvaparityāgāditi, na paśyatītyāhurekībhūta ityādilakṣaṇā
evaṃ trividhā sattvarajastamobhedena jāgrat - svapna- suṣupta dharmiṇī prakṛtiḥ, sthūlādi vā lakṣaṇā, sūkṣmā sandhyālakṣaṇā, parā mahāniśā- lakṣaṇā iti
cakārādaparā caturthī prakṛtiḥ jñānamūttiḥ; sā jñānacittasya turyācittasya śukracyutikāle turyāvasthārghārmaṇī sattvānāmutpādanirodha hetubhūtā yataḥ, ato jñānamūrtiḥ; sā ca prakṛtiravikṛtirjīvabhūtā mahāprāṇabhūtā dharmadhātusvabhāveti

paṭale ] kṣaṇalakṣaṇa-kālacakraniyamamahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ duḥkhasaukhyapradātocyate ātmetyādinā- ātmā kartā na tatra prabhavati nṛpate duḥkhasaukhyapradātā bandho mokṣo na kaścidbhavati narapate cittaśakta vivajyaṃ
ākāśaṃ kumbhamadhye vrajati na ca jalaṃ nīyamāne ca yadvad vyomavyāpī khavajrī viṣayavirahito dehamadhye ca tadvat || 91 ||
saṃsāre ātmā kartā na tatra prabhavati nṛpate duḥkhasaukhyoḥ pradātā, duḥkhasaukhya- pradātā na kaścidasti tribhuvananilaye cittaśakta vivarjyaṃ
cittaśaktayo'tra jāgratsvapna - susu ptaturyā lakṣaṇā iti, tāsāṃ pradātā turyā utpādanirodhadharmiṇyavasthā, tāṃ cittaśakti vivarṇya nānyaḥ kaścidātmā kartā vā saukhyaduḥkhapradāteti
bandho mokṣo vā'paraḥ [na] ' kaścidasti śukracyutiṃ vihāya sattvānāmiti saṃsāracittaṃ vihāyetyarthaḥ
nirvāṇacittaṃ punaḥ saṃsārātītaṃ sarvadehe sthitaṃ na badhyate na mucyate kenacit

khavajrīti; viṣayavirahi to nirguṇa iti tanmātrādisattvarajastamorahitaṃ jāgrat- svapnasusu ptaturyā rahitamityarthaḥ ;

[s] kandhabhūtaiḥ ṣaḍdhātubhirindriyaistrividhabhavabhayaiḥ ṣaḍviṣayestanmātraiḥ pañcakarmendriyaiśca karmadoṣaiḥ śubhāśubhaiḥ saha guṇa manasā buddhayahaṅkārakādyairādyaśabdena prāṇaśaktimūlaprakṛtiriti, etairbaddho hi jīvo bhramati rasagatau sūkṣmabhāvena, bhūyaḥ punaracyutyānandeneti bandhaḥ sūkṣma- sthūlabhāvo jāgratsvapnasusu ( ṣu) ptaturyālakṣaṇaḥ |
etasmiśca bhāve tyakte prayātyakṣayaparamapadaṃ yatra gato na bhūyo janmī saṃsārī bhavati, iti saṃsāravāsanā - avidyāniyamaḥ |
idānīmasyā vipakṣā vidyocyate [vedaḥ] sāṅga ityādinā - vedaḥ sāṅgo na vidyā smṛtimatasahitastakaṃ siddhāntayuktaḥ śāstrañcānyaddhi loke kṛtamapi kavibhirvyāsavaiśvānarādyaiḥ vidyetyadhyātmavidyā'kṣaramapi munibhiḥ proktamevātra loke trailokyaṃ yatra kṛtsnaṃ bhavati narapate līyate yatra bhūyaḥ || 96 ||
iha saṃsāre lokavyavahāre vidyā yā paramārthatattvaviṣaye'vidyā sā |
prathamaṃ vedaḥ sāṅga ṣaḍbhiraṅgaiḥ saha, aṅgāni ca sūtraṃ geyaṃ vyākaraṇaṃ chando nirukti jyotiśceti, ebhiḥ sahetyarthaḥ |
sa ca smṛtimatasahitaḥ smṛtayo manvādayaḥ taiḥ sahitaḥ saṃyuktaḥ |
tarkoṃ laukikavastupramāṇaśāstrāṇi siddhāntā laukika siddhisādhakā maṇḍalacakrādivikalpa- bhāvanādharmāstairyukta iti viśeṣaṇam |
evaṃ śāstraṃ cānyaddhi loke kṛtamapi kavibhirvyā- savaiśvānarādyairiti vyāsakāvyaṃ bhāratam, vaiśvānarakāvyaṃ bhāvanādharmaḥ, ādiśabdena vālmīka (ki) kāvyaṃ rāmāyaṇam, mārkaṇḍeyakāvyaṃ purāṇadharmādayaḥ saṃgṛhītāḥ kṛtaṃ kavibhirebhirna vidyā |
tarhi vidyā ketyāha - vidyeti avikalpitā'dhyātmavidyā śūnyatā sarvākārairupetā, anyaccākṣaramapi vidyā'nālambinī anāśra ( sa ) vasukhātmikā iti |
vidyā hetuphalātmikā hetuphalai kalolībhūtā graḥ (agni) prabhā dahanaikalolībhūtavaditi |
munibhiratītavartamānaiḥ proktamevātra loke dharmacakrasthaiḥ |
kiṃrūpā setyāha - trailokyaṃ yatra kṛtsnamiti, yasmāt sukhakṣaṇāttrailokyaṃ sakalaṃ bhavati utpādakāle, līyate yatra bhūyaḥ, maraṇakāle yatra kṣaṇe līyate |
bhāvasamūhaḥ kṣarakṣaṇe sa eva kṣaṇo'nāśra (sra ) vasukhamakṣaramucyate |
yasmād buddhā utpadyante dharmacakrapravarttanāya, yasmin mahāparinirvāṇo (ṇaṃ) hi narapata iti vidyāniyamaḥ |
idānīṃ pūrvayogābhyāsabalamucyate yogītyādinā- yogīndro'prāptayogaḥ pracalitamanasā yāti mṛtyuṃ kadācit śrīmān mānuṣyaloke pravaramunikule jāyate yogayuktaḥ |
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [adhyātma- pūrvābhyāsena tenāharati punarapi jñānayogaṃ viśālaṃ labdhe jñāne prayātyakṣayaparamapadaṃ yatra janmī na bhūyaḥ || 97 ||
iha martye yadi yogī vidyāyogamabhyasyamāno maraṇamupaiti aprāptayogaḥ kadācit pracalitamanasā śrīmān mānuṣyaloke pravaramunikule buddhabodhisattvakule, yogaḥ śūnyatā- karuṇābhinnaṃ viśuddhatattvaṃ viśuddhacittam ;
tena yukta iti yogayuktaḥ, na yogaparipūrṇaḥ punaḥ pūrvābhyāsena tenāharati avāpto hi jñānayogaṃ viśālamiti na prādeśikaṃ sarvākāra- miti bhāvaḥ |
labdhe jñāne paripūrṇe prayāti akṣayaparamapadamiti na vidyate kṣayo yasyetyakṣayaparamapadamiti vigrahaḥ |
kimbhūtaṃ tadityāha — yatra punarjanmī utpattimānna bhavati, tat sarvaṃ buddhānāṃ padaṃ paramapadamiti |
idānoṃ bauddhānāmasurāṇāṃ mlecchānāṃ jñānotpattikāla ucyate jñāna ityādinā - jñānotpattijinānāṃ ravidinasamaye cārddharātre niśānte madhyāhne cāsurāṇāṃ śaśiniśisamaye nirgame vāsava (ra) sya |
samyagjñāne vibhaṅge prabhavati vacanaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ prākṛtaṃ ca śāntaṃ raudraṃ ca karma tribhuvananilaye pauruṣaṃ prākṛtaṃ ca || 98 ||
iha khalu trividho yogābhyāsaḥ bauddha āsuro bhotaśca
tatra bauddho yogaḥ śūnyatā karuṇātmakaḥ, āsuraḥ kalpanādharmaḥ, bhautiko dviprakāraḥ, śāśvatarūpa uccheda- rūpaśca
evaṃ trividho yogī sa eva vidyate yasya tadyogābhyāsaratatvāditi
teṣu divābhāge bauddhayogināṃ jñānotpattiḥ, rātribhāge āsurayoginām, catuḥ sandhyārahite kāle bhauta yogināṃ jñānotpattiriti
atra kālavibhāgaḥ jñānotpattijinānāṃ ravidina- samaye cārddharātre niśānte, madhyāhne cāsurāṇāṃ śaśiniśisamaye nirgame vāsava sya
bhautānāmanuktatvādapi sandhyārahitakāle
atrārddharātre pūrvasandhyāyāṃ vāgjñānādhiṣṭhānaṃ bhavati bauddhānām, asurāṇāṃ madhyāhnasandhyāyāṃ aṣṭaṅga tasandhyāyāṃ vāgjñānādhiṣṭhānaṃ bhavati
bhūtānāmapara catuḥ praharasandhyāyāṃ divābhāge śāśvatajñānādhiṣṭhānam, rātribhāge ucchedajñānādhiṣṭhānam, anayorboddhāsurayoryathāsaṃkhyam; samyagjñānaṃ bauddhānāṃ bhavati, vibhaṅgaṃ taddharmavirodhi bhavati asurāṇām
samyagjñānaṃ divālokavat sarvadarśa, vibhaṅgajñānaṃ rātryālokavat kiñcit sattvānāṃ jīvamaraṇadarśīti
kathaṃ jñāyata ityata āha - dharmadeśanayā iti, iha samyagjñāne vibhaṅga prabhavati vacanaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ prākṛtaṃ ceti
samyagjñānotpannānāṃ saṃskṛtavākyaṃ sarvarutātmakamiti, vibhaṅgajñānotpannānāṃ prākṛtaṃ vākyaṃ bhavati deśakānām eka' viṣayabhāṣāntareṇeti
śāntakarmadeśakaṃ bauddhānāṃ jñānaṃ sarvasattvakaruṇātmakam, raudrakarmadeśakaṃ asurāṇāṃ jñānaṃ tiryaksattvāpakārimāṃsabhakṣaṇāyeti
paṭale ] kṣaṇalakṣaṇa-kālacakraniyamamahoddaśaḥ tribhuvananilaye pauruṣaṃ karma bauddhānāṃ jñānaṃ deśayati, kṣitau prākṛtaṃ karma asurāṇāṃ jñānaṃ deśayati
bhūtānāṃ vimiśraṃ karma deśayati pṛthivyāmiti jñānadeśanāniyamaḥ
idānīṃ bauddhāsurayobhuṃ ktikāla ucyate madhyāhlādityādinā - madhyāhlādarddharātraṃ dinaniśisamaye bhuktikālastayośca annaṃ gomāṃsabhojyaṃ bahuvidharasadaṃ pānamaṇḍasya śukram
raktaṃ śvetaṃ ca vastraṃ raviśaśigativat svargapātālavāsaḥ dharmo'hiṃsā ca hiṃsā guruniyamavaśād vajradaityāsanaṃ ca ||99||
iha pratidine madhyāhnadārabhyāddha' rātraṃ yāvaddinasamayam (:)
arddharātrādārabhya madhyāhnaparyantaṃ niśisamayaḥ
tasmin dinaniśisamaye svasvasamayasya parārddha bhuktikāla: tayorboddhamlecchayoryathāsaṃkhyaṃ tapasvinām, na gṛhasthānāmiti niyamaḥ
bauddhāsurayoḥ punaḥ khānapānaṃ yathāsaṃkhyam
annaṃ viśiṣṭataraṃ bauddhānām, gomāṃsasahitaṃ mlecchā- nām
pānaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ bahuvidharasadaṃ miṣṭaṃ bauddhānām, kukkuṭādīnāmaṇḍasya śukrapānaṃ mlecchānāmiti
paridhānaṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ raktavastraṃ bauddhānām, śvetaṃ mlecchānāṃ tapasvinām ; gṛhasthānāṃ na niyamaḥ
tathā maraṇānte āvāso yathāsaṃkhyam, raviśaśigativaditi raverūrdhvaṃ gatiḥ, candrasyādho gatiḥ, tayorgativat svargavāso ravigatirboddhānām, pātāla- vāso'surāṇāṃ candragativaditi
yathā mūlatantre sekoddeśe' bhagavānāha - “adhaścandrāmṛtaṃ yāti maraṇe sarvadehinām
ūrdhve sūryaṃ rajo rāhu vijñānaṃ bhāvalakṣaṇe ||
tathā dharmo yathāsaṃkhyam
bauddhānāṃ dharmo'hiṃsā, mlecchānāṃ hiṃsā, cakārād bhūtānām
guruniyamavaśād bhāvanākāle iṣṭadevatāstutikāle yathāsaṃkhyaṃ bauddhānāṃ vajrāsanaṃ praśastam, mlecchānāṃ daityāsanaṃ praśastam, cakārādaparaṃ sāmānyamiti tasya na vidhirna niṣedha iti
atra daityānāṃ bhūtale vāmajānuprasārataḥ, vāmajānūrdhvaṃ dakṣiṇapādaḥ, dakṣiṇajānūdurdhvaprasāro vāmapādordhvam, dakṣiṇapādaścakārādadhaḥ dakṣiṇapāda ūrdhvam, pādatale'pi cakārāt pṛṣṭhe kaṭiniṣaṇṇa iti vajrāsanādivakṣyamāṇe vaktavyamiti bauddhāsura kriyāniyamaḥ
bhūtānāṃ miśrakarma prabhavati vacanaṃ miśrapaiśācikaṃ ca dharmo'hiṃsā ca hiṃsā khalu guruniyamājjīvaghātādidoṣāḥ
yāgādyarthaṃ pravṛttirbhavati narapate vā nivṛttiḥ kadācit khādyaṃ peyaṃ ca miśraṃ raviśaśigamanānmiśramārgaḥ paratra || 100||
paṭale ] vimalaprabhāyāṃ [adhyātma- bhūtānāṃ punaḥ pūrvoktamiśrakamaṃ
tadatra' vṛtte na vispaṣṭaṃ kathitam; tadyathā - bhūtaṃ vā ( bhūtānāṃ ) miśrakarma prabhavati vacanaṃ miśrapaiśācikaṃ ca, dharmo'hiṃsā ca hiṃsā khalu guru niyamājjīvaghātādidoṣaḥ
yogārthaṃ pravṛttiriti yo yā gādyarthamādiśabdāt saṃgrāmārthaṃ hiṃsāpravṛttiḥ, phalārthaṃ nivṛttirahiṃsāpravṛttirbhavati
narapate vā nivṛttiḥ kadāciditi
khādyaṃ peyaṃ ca miśraṃ raviśa śigamanāt miśramārgaḥ paratra iti vyāmiśramārge bhūtānāṃ sarvo veditavyaḥ, iti bhūtakriyāniyamaḥ
idānīṃ mārgaṃ vaktukāma āha jyotirityādinā - jyotiḥ sūryācarabdhau sitadinamayanaṃ ca ttaraṃ cārddhavarṣaṃ candro jyotirniśā cāsitamayanamidaṃ dakṣiṇe dhūmamārge
aciḥ svarge surāṇāmapi bhavati tanau nāgaloke'surāṇāṃ vyāmiśro martyaloke bhavati narapate pretatiryaṅnarāṇām || 101 ||
iha khalu trividho mārgaḥ – jyotirmārgo dhūmamārgo vimiśramārgaḥ
eṣu acirathe- jyotiḥ sūryaḥ
śi sitadinamayanamuttaraṃ divāvṛddhilakṣaṇam arddhavarṣa bāhye, adhyātmani makarādiṣaḍlagnātmakamiti, udayāstamanamiti saṃjñitam
dhūmamārgo jyoti- ścandro niśā cāra śi tamayanamidaṃ dakṣiṇe dhūmamārge karkaṭādikaṃ ṣaṇmāsadakṣiṇāyanam, yatra rātrervṛddhistad dakṣiṇāyanam ; astamanādudayaṃ yāvanniyama iti
atrāciḥ svarge surāṇāṃ mārgagamanāya
asitamayanaṃ dhūmamārgo nāgalokagamanāyāsurāṇāmiti
vyāmiśro divā- rātridharmā martyalokagamane pretatiryaṅganarāṇāmutpattaye prakaṭita iti mārganiyamaḥ, sattvarajastamoguṇasvabhāvena pūrvajanmavāsanājaniteneti
idānīṃ sattvānāṃ vāsanāgrahamucyate śrāvakabauddhāsurabhūtakarmaratānāṃ yo yanmadhya ityādinā - yo yanmadhye praviṣṭo vrataniyamarataḥ karmapāśairnibaddha- stanmadhye svasvabhāvād bhavati narapate tatkule tadgraheṇa
yāva ccittasya bhāvastrividhabhavavaśād vedanā saukhyaduḥkhaṃ tāvat saṃsāraghore bhramaṇamiha nṛpa svargamartye tvadhaśca || 102 ||
iha saṃsāre janma karmavāsanābalena devāsurabhūtakriyānurakto yo yanmadhye praviṣṭo bhavati vrataniyamarataḥ karmapāśanibaddhaḥ, tasya dharmasya kriyābhirbaṃddha iti; tanmadhye sva-sva- bhāvād bhavati ; narapate ityāmantraṇam
tatkule devāsurabhūtakule
tadgraheṇeti tadbhāva- graheṇa
tena devāsurabhūtakule janmī bhavati, sattvarajotamohaṅkāreṇa pūrvoktavidhineti yāva- paṭale ] kṣaṇalakṣaṇa-kālacakraniyamamahoddeśaḥ ccittasya bhāvastrividhabhavavaśāditi
iha saṃsāracittasya trividhabhavavaśāditi kāmarūpā- rūpabhavavāsanāvaśāt vedanā saukhyaduḥkhaṃ sāṃsārikam, tāvat saṃsāre duḥkhalakṣaṇaṃ bhramaṇaṃ cittasyāpi nṛpa svargamartyeṣu, adhaśca devamanuṣyanarakādiṣaḍgatiṣu bhramaṇamālayavijñānasyeti vāsanāgrahaniyamaḥ
atastridhā vāsanātītaḥ- saṃsārapārakoṭisthaḥ kṛtyakṛtyasthale sthitaḥ”
jñānakāyo bhagavān samyaksambuddho devāsuranāgādibhirnamaskṛtacaraṇāravindaḥ kālacakra iti vāsanāpagamaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ yathā garbhe vyañjanasvarotpādo'bhūt, tathā mṛtyulakṣaṇamucyate kādyā- nityādinā - kādyān vargān samātrānapi guṇaguṇitān yān tripakṣasvarāṃśca triṃśad vai vyañjanāni trividhagativaśād rohate tāni mṛtyuḥ
cārān pañcagrahāṇāṃ raviśaśicaraṇaṃ rāhuketvoḥ padaṃ ca dvātriṃśadvarṣapakṣānapi guṇaguṇitāṃścandrasūryāgnibhinnān || 103 ||
ihādhānakāle rajastriśadvyañjanātmakam, śukraṃ triṃśatsvarātmakam
anayoḥ saṃyo- gaḥ, ālayavijñānādhiṣṭhitayoḥ
atra garbhotpāde prathamapakṣe triśadvyañjanāni kakārādīni hrasvasvarādhiṣṭhitāni aparapakṣe dīrghasvarādhiṣṭhitāni iti vyañjanapakṣadvayaṃ maraṇe prakaṭitaṃ bhavati
evaṃ śukrasyākāśadhātupraveśaḥ; rajasi śuklapakṣe' kṛṣṇapakṣe pṛthvī- dhātupraveśaḥ
evaṃ rajaso'pyudbhūtānudbhūtayoḥ pṛthivyākāśadhātvoḥ śukradhātau praveśaḥ
evaṃ dvitīyamāsa prathamapakṣe ikārādhiṣṭhitāni vyañjanāni rajasi vāyudhātupraveśaḥ, aparapakṣe ūkārādhiṣṭhitāni udakadhātupraveśaḥ
tṛtīyamāsaprathamapakṣe ṛkārādhiṣṭhitāni tejodhātu-
praveśaḥ, aparapakṣe ṛkārādhiṣṭhitāni tejodhātupraveśāya
caturthamāsaprathamapakṣe ukārādhiṣṭhitāni udakadhātupraveśāya, aparapakṣe īkārādhiṣṭhitāni vāyudhātupraveśāya
pañcamamāsaprathamapakṣe lṛkārādhiṣṭhitāni pṛthivīdhātupraveśāya, aparapakṣe dīrghākārā- dhiṣṭhitāni ākāśadhātupraveśāyeti
sṛṣṭisaṃhārabhedenākāśādipṛthivyādipraveśa: śukra- rajasoḥ parasparamiti
tat indriya- karmendriyādhiṣṭhānārthaṃ guṇavṛddhihādayo hrasvadīrghā vyañjaneṣu praveśaṃ kurvanti
ṣaṣṭhamāsasya pūrvapakṣāddhe a, pūrvapakṣāparārddhe ha, aparapakṣārddhe āla, aparapakṣārddhe lā, saptamasyaivaṃ e ya o vā, aṣṭamasya ara ra ār rā, navamasya o vā ai yā, daśada (ma) sya ala ra la ā hā lā, iti triṃśadvyañjaneṣu praveśaṃ daśamāsaiḥ skandha- dhātvāyatanādīnāṃ niṣpattiriti garbhamāsādūrdhvamiti śukrarajaḥ saṃyogaḥ, skandhadhātvāyatanā- dīnāṃ spharaṇaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakrameṇa
evamekādaśe māse bindunā triṃśadadhiṣṭhitāni dvādaśe māse visargādhiṣṭhitāni bhavanti
ataścandradhātuśukradhātūnāṃ nāḍīmajjāstī (sthī) nāmutpāda- paricchedaḥ
arko rajo vṛddhi karoti dvādaśavarṣāṇi prajñāyāḥ, upāyasya ṣoḍaśavarṣāṇi, tato ala al ā
vimalaprabhāyāṃ [ adhyātma- jñānadhātupākaḥ
prajñāyā rajaḥ pākaḥ, upāyasya śukrapākaḥ, tataḥ prajñāyā'paradvādaśavarṣāṇi, upāsyāparaṣoḍaśavarṣāṇi pṛthivīdhātupākaḥ
ato'vadheḥ sattvabhāgo madhyamo bhavati
prajñāyā'parāṣṭādaśavarṣa toyadhātupākaḥ, upāyasya sārddhadvāviṃśatidinamāsaikādhikaṃ ṣoḍaśavarṣaṃ pūrvavat toyadhātupākaḥ
pūrvavat prajñāyā agnipākaḥ, upāyasyāpi pūrvavat
evaṃ vāyudhātupākaḥ, ākāśadhātupākaḥ, ṣaṇṇavativarṣe sārddhadaśamāsaiḥ sarvadhātūnāṃ pākaḥ sattvaraja- stamobhedena
tato madhyanāḍī varṣapakṣamānena dinamānena triṃśadvyañjanamānena pañcapañcāśadadhi- kaikādaśaśatadinagaṇena kālaḥ prarohati
kādyān vargān samātrān pūrvoktānapi guṇaguṇitānaśītyuttaradina sahasrabhūtān yān tripakṣasvarāṃśca hrasvadīrghaplutān pañcacatvāriṃśattraṃśadvyañjanāni
trividhagati- vaśāditi vāmadakṣiṇamadhyamāgativaśād rohate tān (ni) mṛtyuriti
evaṃ cārān pañcagna- hāṇāṃ maṅgalādīnāṃ catuḥ triṃśadadhikaikādaśaśatān pūrvoktāniti
raviśaśicaraṇaṃ rasayugaśa- śinam ekādaśapadātmakaṃ rāhuketvoḥ padaṃ sārddhasaptātmakaṃ māsaparicchinnātmakaṃ ketorudayapadaṃ pratidinaṃ dvighaṭikātmakam
ete pada ghaṭikātmakāstrivarṣastripakṣatriṃśadvyañjanadinasaṃkhyā- stānevārohate mṛtyuriti
evaṃ dvātriṃśadvarṣapakṣānapi guṇaguṇitān candrasūryāgnibhinnān sattvarajastamobhinnāniti ṣaṇṇavativarṣe'pare sārddhadaśamāsapakṣāniti
pakṣā varṣaṃtrayāṇāmapi śaśini guṇāḥ kālavahnidvipakṣāḥ ūnībhūtāḥ śatābde samaviṣamagatī śe [ṣa] pakṣā ravīndvoḥ
lokākṣyagnyakṣisaṃkhyā dinaniśisamayān bhatṛhīnān samastān vargān mātrān ravīndvogra sati gativaśād vyañjanādīni mṛtyuḥ || 104 ||
vāmadakṣiṇanāḍīpakṣā' varṣaśatapakṣāṇāṃ madhye pakṣā varṣatrayāṇāṃ dvāsaptatiḥ, śaśinaḥ api guṇāstripakṣāḥ, kālavahnidvipakṣāḥ ; evaṃ saptasaptatipakṣā ūnībhūtāḥ śatābde śatā- bdapakṣarāśau caturviṃśatiśatasaṃkhye, śeṣapakṣā ravondroḥ savyāvasavyayoriti
lokākṣyagnyakṣisaṃkhyā iti trayoviṃśatyadhika trayoviṃśatiśatāśceti
evaṃ dinaniśisamayān bhartṛhīnān samastān, varṣaśatasya ṣaṭtriṃśatsahasrāṃśca, bhartṛhīnān trivarṣatripakṣavyañjanamāsa- dinahīnān vāme dakṣiṇe bhakṣate mṛtyuḥ
vargān mātrān ravīndrorgrasati gativaśād vyañja- nādīni mṛtyuḥ, ariṣṭādārabhya madhyamākāle bhakṣate iti
sārddhā vai cakranāḍīḥ śaśipadarahitā vāranāḍīvihīnāḥ prajñopāyaprabhededinaniśisamayairbhakṣate tāṃśca mṛtyuḥ
evaṃ vargān samātrān svaragaṇasahitān vyañjanānyeva vārān hīne vā madhyameṣṭa śaśiravimaraṇe vyañjane cādhidaive || 105 ||
paṭale ] kṣaṇalakṣaṇa-kālacakraniyamamahoddeśaḥ evaṃ sārddhā vai cakranāḍīḥ sārddhacakramaṣṭādaśarāśi saṃkhyāṃ pañcaviṃśadadhikaśataguṇitā ghaṭikā triṃśadadhikacaturviṃśatiśatasaṃkhyā iti; śaśipadāni kramo'krama' bhedena bhūtābhūteṣu vedādīni śatasaṃkhyāni, tai rahitāḥ śaśipadarahitā vāranāḍī sapta, tehīṃnāḥ samastāstrayo- viṃśatyadhikatrayoviṃśatiśatasaṃkhyāḥ, prajñopāyaprabhedaiḥ saṃhārasṛṣṭibherdedana niśisamayaikṣate tā~śca mṛtyuḥ
evaṃ vargān samātrān svaragaṇasahitān vyañjanānyeva vārān, hone vā madhyameṣṭe śaśiravimaraṇe vyañjane cādhidaive iti hīne candrāriṣṭamaraṇe, madhyame sūryāriṣṭa- maraṇe, vyañjane cādhidaiva iti madhyameṣṭamaraṇe pūrvoktaniyamaḥ
dvātriṃśaccaraktaṃ saśaśiravigatau nāḍikāccheda eṣaḥ bhūyastraśattrirātraistyajati sakuliśān skandhadhātvindriyādīn
tyaktvā candrārkaṃnāḍīṃ praviśati śi (śaṃ) khinīṃ prāṇavāyurdinaikaṃ vicchedaṃ yāvadeva kṣitijalahutabhug (ṅa) mārutākāśadhātoḥ || 106 ||
tato vātriṃśaccai raktamiti trayastriśad dināni śaśiravigatau vāmagatyā sārddhaṃ dakṣiṇagatau nāḍikācchreṭa eba (:) candrasūryāriṣṭe sādhāraṇaḥ, kālamaraṇe bhūyaḥ punaḥ triśat- trirātreḥ, trayastrāddinaistyajati sakliśān kāyavākcittasahitān skandhadhātvindri- yādīna
tatastyaktvā candrārkanāḍīṃ lalanārasanāṃ praviśati śi (śaṃ) khinīmavadhūtīṃ prāṇavāryādanaikaṃ vicchedaṃ yāvadeva kṣitijalahatabhug (ṅa) mārutākāśadhātoḥ
iha madhyamāyāṃ maraṇadine prāṇapraviṣṭa: kālavātākrāntaḥ san nābhī pṛthvīdhātuṃ pañcaguṇasvabhāvaṃ tyajati, tataḥ pṛthvīdhātovicchedo bhavati; hṛdaye toyadhātuṃ caturguṇasvabhāvaṃ tyajati, toyadhātu- (to) vicchedo bhavatiḥ kaṇṭhe tejodhātuṃ triguṇasvabhāvaṃ tyajati, tatastejodhātorvicchedo bhavati; lalāṭe vāyadhātuṃ dviguṇasvabhāvaṃ tyajati, tato vāyudhātovicchedo bhavati; uṣṇīṣe ekaguṇasvabhāvamākāśadhātuṃ tyajati, tataḥ śūnyadhātoviccheda iti
prāṇasya dhātoḥ parityāga ūrdhvam
adho nābhau kāryābindudharmamapānaṃ tyajati, tato jāgradavasthākṣayo bhavati; guhye vāgbandudharmaṃ tyajati, tataḥ svapnāvasthācchedo bhavati; maṇau cittabindudharmaṃ tyajati, tataḥ susu (ṣa)ptāvasthācchedo bhavati
ūrdhve'pyevaṃ rajodhātubindūn prāṇāṃstyajati, tato hi kāyavākcittavicchedo bhavati
saṃkrāntikāle jñānadhātuṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ tyajati; sarvāṅge śukra madhastyajati, raja ūrdhvaṃ tyajati, turyāvasthāvicchedo bhavati
evaṃ garbhajānāṃ maraṇakālaniyamaḥ

idānīmakālamaraṇavañcanocyate mārttaṇḍa ityādinā- mārttaṇḍendronirodhaḥ samaviṣamagatau madhyame'gnau praveśaḥ prāṇāpānadvayośca prathamadinagate vañcanākālamṛtyoḥ
prāṇenāpūrayitvā saha karacaraṇairaṅguliparvānnakhāntān ṣaṭcakre buddhadevyo'bhayakarakamalā yoginā bhāvanīyāḥ || 109 ||
| iha yadā'kālamaraṇalakṣaṇaṃ paśyati yogī tadā mārttaṇḍendronirodhaḥ dakṣiṇavāmanā- ḍyonirodhaḥ
samaviṣamagatāviti saṃhārasṛṣṭigatau madhyame agnāviti rāhukālāgnau madhyanāḍyāṃ praveśaḥ kartavyaḥ
prāṇāpānadvayośca prathamadinagate madhyamāpravāhaikadinagate sati vañcanā sā'kālamṛtyorbhavati yogināmiti

idānīṃ vātarogādiśamanamucyate apānetyādinā - āpānākuñcaneṣṭaṃ bhavati varatanau vātaroge samaste prāṇāyāmaḥ kaphe syāt punarapi ca tayormuñcanaṃ pittadoṣe
ūrdhvādha: [137a] sannirodho bhavati sukhakaraḥ sannipāte jvare ca nābhyūrdhvaṃ prāṇavāyuḥ sakalarujaharo nābhimūle'paraśca || 110 ||
iha śarīre pūrvoktā'śīti nāḍyo vātadoṣakāriṇyaḥ, nābhī guhye ca tāsāṃ samadhātutvakaraṇāya apānasyākuñcanamiṣṭaṃ sukhakaraṃ bhavati varatanau mantriṇāṃ vātaroge śūlādike samaste iti
tathā lalāṭe uṣṇīṣe viśati nāḍyaḥ śleṣmadoṣakāriṇyaḥ
tāsāṃ samadhātukaraṇāya prāṇāyāma iṣṭo bhavati
kaphe syāditi kapharoge jāte sati prāṇanirodhaḥ kartavyo yogineti

rasāyanādibālatantramahoddeśaḥ svacchāyāmātapasthāmaparamukharaveḥ stabdhadṛṣṭyāvalokya paścād vyomābhivīkṣye t samarasapuruṣo dṛśyate dhūmravarṇaḥ
ṣaṇmāsābhyāsayogādava nigatanidhi darśaye cchidrabhūmi vṛkṣacchāyāṃ praviśyattatha gaganatale bhāvitā bindumālā
// 119 //
yā śaktirnābhimadhyād vrajati pa rapadaṃ dvādaśāntaṃ kalāntaṃ sā nābhau sanniruddhā tadidananamitā daṇḍarūpotthitā vā
cakrāccakrāntaraṃ vai mṛdulalitagatindhāritā madhyanāḍyāṃ yāvaccoṣṇīṣarandhraṃ spṛśati haṭhatayā sūcibaddhā hyaca
// 120 //
āpānaṃ tatra kāle paramahaṭhatayā prerayedūdūrdhvamārge uṣṇīṣaṃ bhedayitvā vrajati pa puraṃ vāyuyugme niruddhe
evaṃ vajraprabodhāt satanu sa viṣayā khecaratvaṃ prayāti pañcābhijñāsvabhāvā bhavati punariyaṃ yogināṃ viśvamātā
// 121 //
idānīṃ prāṇāyāmaniyama ucyate- prāṇāyāmaṃ prakuryād hṛdi śirasi tathā yāvadagnirvyathā'bhūt tasmādūrdhvaṃ hi mūrcchā vrajati sukamale'yantrito vā balena
uṣṇīṣaṃ bhedayitvā vrajati parapuraṃ skandhadhātūn vihāya mudrāsaṅgapraṇaṣṭo na hi sukhaphalado janmanīhaiva puṃsām
// 122 //
iha śarīre prāṇāyāmaṃ kumbhakaṃ kuryāt mantrī hṛdi śirasi tathā yāvadagnirvyathā- bhūt tāvad hṛdayaṃ dahyate, śirasi ca vyathā bhavati
tata ūrdhvaṃ na kuryāditi niyamaḥ, yadi karoti tasmādūrdhvaṃ hi mūccha vrajati sukamale, nābhikamale prāṇo mūrcchā vrajatīti
ayantrito vā balenoṣṇīṣaṃ bhedayitvā vrajati hi maraṇaṃ skandhadhātūn vihāya yataḥ, ato mudrāsaṅgapraṇaṣṭo na hi sukhaphaladaḥ prāṇāyāmo janmanīhaiva puṃsāmiti prāṇāyāma- niyamaḥ
mudroktā bhāvānārthaṃ dinaniśisamaye neva rāgakṣayārthaṃ vāgvajraṃ tarpaṇārthaṃ na khalu madakaraṃ mantriṇāmuktamevam
sarvāhāraḥ sukhārthaṃ pratidinasamaye'jīrṇahetorna coktaḥ śrīcaryāsiddhito maṇamapi cittau kīlanārthaṃ na rātrī
// 123 //
asya ślokasya vyākhyā saṃskṛtapratiṣu nopalabhyate, kintu bhoṭānuvāde sā labdhā, atra ko heturiti vivecayatā ācārya - khes ḍraba-je mahābhāgairuktaṃ yat vyākhyāyāḥ viśuddha- bhāratīya saṃskṛtapratiṣu 'idānīṃ yogināṃ mudrāsvādanāhāracāraniyama ucyate iti subodham' iti mātraṃ labhyate eva
so'pyaṃśo mayā saṃskṛtapratiṣu na dṛṣṭaḥ
atra khes-ḍraba-je-mahābhāgānāṃ svamatamullekhanīyamasti
tena uktaṃ yad anye'pi paṭale ] rasāyanādibālatantramahoddeśaḥ idānīmavadhūtayogibhaiṣajyamucyate- akṣobhyaṃ kiñciduṣṇaṃ mukharujaśamanaṃ dantaśūlasya caiva pratyūṣe'kṣobhyanasyaṃ śirasi rujaharaṃ toyanasyaṃ tathaiva
karṇe netraṃ praviṣṭaṃ tva bhayarujaharaṃ mūtramuṣṇaṃ ca śītaṃ bhūtārte'kṣobhyanasyaṃ trikaṭukasahitaṃ saukhyadaṃ cāpi daṣṭe
// 124 //
iha yadā yogino mukharogo bhavati, akṣobhyaṃ kiñciduṣṇaṃ kṛtvā mukhe dhṛtaṃ mukhara- ru jasa manaṃ bhavati
dantaśūlasya caiveti cakārā d dantakīṭasya ca
yadā śirasi rogo bhavati, tadā pratyūṣe akṣobhyaṃ nasyaṃ kṛtaṃ śirasi rujaharaṃ bhavati
toyanasyaṃ tathaiva yogināmiti
yadā karṇarogo bhavati, tadā mūtramuṣṇaṃ kṛtaṃ karṇe praviṣṭaṃ netraroge śītaṃ kṛtaṃ netre praviṣṭaṃ yathāsaṃkhyamubhayarujaharam, mūtramuṣṇaṃ ca śītamiti
bhūtārtta iti bhūtapretādigraste akṣobhyanasyaṃ trikaṭukena sahitaṃ saukhyadaṃ cāpi daṣṭe, sarpadaṣṭe'pi saukhyadaṃ bhavatīti mukharogādyupasa mananiyamaḥ
viṇmūtraṃ śukraraktaṃ nṛpata lasahitaṃ bhakṣitaṃ cāyudaṃ syāt sadhyānaṃ puṣpanasyaṃ harati sapalitānaṅgajātān jarāṃśca
bhuktaṃ pañcapradīpaṃ sakalarujaharaṃ makṣikācchardimiśraṃ strīpuṣpaṃ śukramiśraṃ tvapaharati rujaṃ bhakṣitaṃ varṣaṃyogāt
// 125 //
idānīṃ pañcāmṛtayoga āyuvṛddhyarthamucyate viḍityādinā - iha 'yathā bāhye tathā dehe', iti vacanāt bāhye pañcadravyāṇi, adhyātmani pañcadravyāṇyekīkṛtya pañcāmṛtayogaḥ, tataḥ pañcāmṛtaṃ bhakṣitaṃ yogināmāyudaṃ syāditi
bhoṭācārya śrīpaṅlo-prabhṛtibhirapi svīkriyate yat bhoṭānuvāde upalabdho'yaṃ vyākhyāṃśaḥ mūlasaṃskṛtasya nāsti, api tu kenacid bhoṭadeśīyena viduṣā bhoṭānuvāde ayamaṃśa āropitaḥ

ājyaṃ kṣīrāhirakteḥ kvathitamapi sadā ghrāṇarogasya nāśaḥ karkoṭī lāṅgalandrī harati sahakharāṃ gaṇḍamālāṃ pralepāt // 127
iha sarvadravyāṣṭaguṇaṃ toyaṃ jātīpatrāṣṭaguṇaṃ toyaṃ pādāvaśeṣaṃ jātīkvāthāmbutoyaṃ kiñciduṣṇaṃ mukharujasa śa manaṃ bhavati

idānīṃ vajrakaṇṭhakopasa (śa) manamucyate kuryāddhastāvityādinā - kuryāddhasto pralambī samapadakamale prāṇavāyonirodhaṃ yāvad bhūmyāṃ prapāto na hi bhavati tanormuñcanaṃ ca jvarasya
bhūyo bhūyaḥ samādhau maraṇabhayakarān nāśayet kaṇṭakān vai hṛtpadma candramūrdhni tvamalaśaśinibhā bhāvitā viśvamātā // 128
iha yadā pāparogopadravo bhavati, tadā prathamaṃ jvaro bhavati, hastapādasandhiṣu vyathā bhavati, śiraśca vyathate
idaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ jñātvā samādhimavalambayet
tatrāyaṃ vidhiḥ - ni (nī ) randhre gṛhe praveśya jvaritaḥ kuryāddhastau dvau pralambo ūruparyantaṃ samapadakamale kuryāt, prāṇavāyo- nirodhaṃ kumbhakaṃ kuryāt, yāvat prayā (pā) to bhūmyāṃ na hi bhavati tanoḥ tāvajjvara rogeṇa bhūmyāṃ prapāto bhavati; athāprapāto yāvat punaḥ punaḥ prāṇāyāmaṃ kuryāditi niyamaḥ
muñcanaṃ ca jvarasya yāvanna bhavati tāvat kāryam, jvare mukte sati na kuryāditi niyamaḥ
bhūyo bhūya. samādhau sthito maraṇabhayakarān nāśayet kaṇṭakān vai

iti pāparogopasa (śa) maḥ
vimalaprabhāyāṃ idānīṃ pāparogopasa (śa) manārthaṃ bhaiṣajyamucyate- [adhyātma- piṣṭvā śītāmbusūryo jvaravihi (ha) tanṛṇāṃ kaṇṭakān nāśayanti ghṛṣṭaṃ tvakṣobhyamiśraṃ harati bhayakarān vā kapālaṃ jvarānte
[141a] mantraścoṃ kārapūrvo jalaśikhimarutāṃ vajrapūrvaṃ ca nāma rakṣāṃ tenaiva kuryāt śirasi galahṛdornnābhiguhyādikeṣu // 129
iha yadā pāparogacihnaṃ bhavati jvaritahastapādasandhiṣu vyathā tathā ( dā) piṣṭvā śītāmbunā masūryo jvaravihi (ha) ta' nṛṇāṃ dattāḥ kaṇṭakān nāśayanti; tathā jvarānte tṛtīya dine kaṇṭakotthānakāle pāparogeṇa mṛtasya kapālam alābhe yathālabdhaṃ pṛ (ghṛ ) ṣṭam akṣobhyamiśraṃ pītaṃ puruṣakapālaṃ puruṣeṇa, strīkapālaṃ striyā
alābhe yathālabdhaṃ pītaṃ harati bhayakarān kaṇṭakān dhā (vā) iti yathālabdhaṃ kapālaṃ jvarānte iti niyamaḥ
atra masūryādīnāmabhimantraṇāya mantro bhavati
sa ca oṃkārapūrva iti mantraścoṃ kārapūrvaḥ
jala śikhimarutāṃ pūrvam oṃkāraḥ, jalamiti pavargasya dvitīyākṣaraṃ toyadhātuḥ pha iti, śikhītyadho ra iti, ūrdhve ekāraḥ, eṣāṃ jalaśikhimarutām ekatvaṃ phreṃ, anusvāram ākāśaṃ sarvavyāpitvāditi
vajrapūrvaṃ ca kaṇṭakānāṃ nāma, ri (ra) kṣāṃ tenaiva kuryāt yathā masūryādeḥ saptavārābhimantritaṃ tathaiva rakṣāṃ tenaiva śirasi gale hṛdaye nābhau guhyaṃ; ādiśabdā- duṣṇīṣe; eṣu ṣaṭsu sthāneṣu trisandhyāyāṃ rakṣāṃ kuryāditi niyamaḥ

ta [141b] kvāthaṃ khaṇḍamiśraṃ punaraparadināt pītametat trirātraṃ grī me sūryāṃśudāhaṃ harati maraṇadaṃ saptadhātau gataṃ ca // 130
iha yadā grīṣme sūryāṃśudāho bhavati adhvani, tadā tulyaṃ dhātrīti āmalakīphala - cūrṇam, dhānyamiti kustumburuḥ, tena tulyamaparamiti; tathā tintirī (ḍī) patracūrṇaṃ bandhu- paṭale ] rasāyanādibālatantra mahoddeśaḥ līpatracūrṇamiti tulyam; evaṃ sarveṣāṃ tulyabhāgaṃ kṛtvā, toye candrārkajuṣṭe iti candrārkakiraṇaiḥ spṛṣṭe khalu vigatamala iti śaivālādimalarahite, kvāthayet pādaśeṣamiti kvāthasya yāvat pādamekaṃ bhavati, tāvad dravyatrayaṃ kvāthayet, dravyāṣṭaguṇaṃ toyaṃ dattveti
atra tulāyā mānaṃ na bhavati, kvāthaviṣaye āḍhakena mānaṃ sarvatreti niyamaḥ
tat kvāthapādāt samātraṃ khaṇḍamiśraṃ punaraparadināt pītam, evamanena krameṇa trirātraṃ pratyūṣakāle grīṣme sūryāṃśudāhaṃ harati maraṇadaṃ saptadhātau gataṃ ceti lomacarmādau gatam, cakārādaparamapīti niyamaḥ
idānīmapararasāyanamucyate heme'rkamityādinā- hemes (nya ) kaṃ kāntalokaṃ (haṃ) paṭalajamayasaṃ vāyenmakṣikena bījārddhenāpi piṣṭirmalavigatarase vāhayet ( kārayet) ṣaṭpalaiśca
gotakaṃ dārayitvā khalu kharaśikhinā grāhyamevāgrama (vātra) mastu śrīpiṣṭyā kalkipātre' kvathitamapi punaryāvadarddhapramān || 131 ||
iha śarīre yaḥ kaścid bāhyarasāyanārthī siddharasābhāve madhyarasāyanamidaṃ kuryāt, asya ca vidhirucyate - hemnīti viśuddhasvarṇe, arkamiti tāmram kāntaṃ loham, paṭalaja- ma kaloham, ayasaṃ tīkṣṇam, eṣāṃ pratyekaṃ samabhāgakṛtānāṃ bhāgaṃ suvarṇasamaṃ mākṣikacūrṇaṃ prativāye (pe)na prakaṭamū[142a]ṣāyāṃ tovravātena nirvāhayet; yāvad hemaṃ tiṣṭhati, tadeva bojam, tena bojenārddhana svarṇapaladvayena rase catuḥpale malavigata iti saptapātanākṛte piṣṭaṃ ṣaṭpalaṃ kārayediti
tato gotraka (gotaka 2 ) mapagatanavanītaṃ khara śikhineti tīvrāgninā vidārayitvā tasya vidāritasya karpaṭena gālayitvā svacchaṃ mastu grāhyam, kalkaṃ tyaktvā
tadeva mastu ayaskāntapātre hemapiṣṭayā sārddhaṃ punaḥ kvāthayet yāvadaddha - pramāṇaṃ bhavati

ṣaṇmāsairdivyadeho valipalitagato yātyanenāmaratvaṃ tasmād bhojyaṃ tadeva pratidinasamaye kintu piṣṭyā vihīnam || 132 ||
tata uddhṛtyāśuddhakhaṇḍāṣṭapalamiti tam arājādikānām āhārapariṇāmavaśena navādikaṃ saptādikaṃ ca
khaṇḍasya catuḥṣaṣṭibhāgikamaricacūrṇamapi tatra kṣepanī (ṇī ) yamiti tadeva bhojyaṃ sarvakālam asa (śa) navirahitaṃ cāyanaṃ yāvadeva

vimalaprabhāyāṃ vāsaṃ kṛtvā supuṣpaiḥ katipayadivasairgandhatoyena miśraṃ aśrā (srā )ve mṛtkapāle dṛḍhapihitamukhe veṣṭite sikthavastreḥ
kṛtvā vistīrṇabhāṇḍe tvatha dharaṇitale pūrite vālukābhiḥ [adhyātma- pakvaṃ ṣaṇmāsayogādbhavati jalagato nābhibhartā sagandhaḥ || 144 ||
tato vāsaṃ kṛtvā daśadinaṃ supuṣpaiḥ yāvadbhūpadoṣopasa (śa) mo bhavati
gandhatoyena miśramiti pūrvavidhinā pratyahaṃ gandhatoyaṃ kāthayet, śītodakaṃ na dadyāt
śītodakenāmlo bhavati, tena gandhatoyena miśraṃ gandhaṃ kṛtvā aśrā (trā) ve mṛtkapāle bāhyasilka (ktha) vastreṇa veṣṭite dṛḍhapihitamukhe tadevāpare vistīrṇe bhāṇḍe jā [b]tikāyāmatha' dharaṇitale, asyābhāve tasmin bhāṇḍe prakṣipya upari vāli (lu) kāṃ dadyād yāvadbhāṇḍaṃ kaṇṭhaparyantaṃ pūritaṃ bhavati
tatra vāli (lu) kābhiḥ pūrite bhāṇḍe sāmānyamudakaṃ sūryataptaṃ deyam, tadeva bhāṇḍaṃ sūryatāpe sthāpayet ṣaṇmāsaṃ yāvat
evaṃ pakkaṃ ṣaṇmāsopayogādbhavati jalagato nābhibhartā gandha iti gandharājaniyamaḥ
idānīṃ puṣpatailārthaṃ gandhatailāya ca tilaśuddhirujya (cya) te kṛsvetyādi - kṛtvā śuddhi tilānāṃ kvathita dalajalairdhūpalepādibhiśca paścājjātyādipuṣpaiḥ katipayadivasaṃ vāsayed yāvadiṣṭam
yantre tailaṃ gṛhītvā nṛpa nipuṇatayā sthāpayet kācabhāṇḍe snāne vā'bhyaṅgane vā bhavati madakaraṃ puṣpatailaṃ hyapakvam || 145 ||
iha tilān paripakkān navān saṃgṛhya kvathitadalajalairiti dalānyāmrapatrāṇi, evaṃ jambū - kapittha- mātuluṅga - vilvānāṃ pañcavṛkṣāṇāṃ patrāṇi, taiḥ kathitaṃ jalaṃ tairdalajalairmardayitvā tilānāṃ tuṣā panayanaṃ prathamaśuddhiḥ
tato jālāyantropari vastraṃ datvā tadupari tilāna- parabhāṇḍe pihitvā dhūpayet, dvitīyā śuddhiḥ
lepādibhiriti triphalairlepo deyo gandhatoyena piṣṭaiḥ, āditaḥ sūryaraśmibhiḥ śoṣayet
evaṃ tilānāṃ śuddhi kṛtvā pañcā (ścā) jjātyā- dipuṣpaiḥ katipayadivasaṃ pakṣaṃ vā daśadinaṃ vā nirantaraṃ vāsayed yāvanmaditānāṃ vāsita- puṣpagandhamudvahati, tata iṣṭaṃ vāsanaṃ bhavati
tataḥ kolu (lhu) kayantreṇa tailaṃ gṛhītvā nṛpa ityāmantraṇam, nipuṇatayā kācabhāṇḍe sthāpayet
tat tailaṃ snāne vā'bhyaṅgane vā bhavati madakaraṃ puṣpatailaṃ hyapakvamiti
atha pakatailaṃ kartukāmaḥ, tadā tadeva tailaṃ samatoyena sugandhena kāthayed yāvat tailaṃ phenaṃ muñcati

idānīṃ gurviṇīnāṃ prasavanārthaṃ sarvataścatustriśatikaṃ yantramucyate bhūbhūdityādinā - bhūbhṛtsūryendumanvakṣi madanavasavo rudrarājāgnayaśca digbhūtā randhraṣaṭkaṃ tithijalanidhayaḥ sthāpanīyāśca koṣṭhe
saṃkhyākoṣṭhaiścaturbhirjalanidhiśikhino lekhayitvā samastaṃ śrīcakraṃ mānapṛṣṭhe prasavanasamaye darśayed gurviṇīnām || 146 ||
iha yadā gurviṇīnāṃ prasavanakāle garbhastambhanaṃ bhavati bāhyadūtīdoṣeṇa tadā idaṃ yantraṃ mānapṛṣṭhe likhet; mānamityāḍhakam
tasya pṛṣṭhe ṣoḍaśakoṣṭhakān kṛtvā prathamakoṣṭhe bhūbhṛt sapta, dvitīye sūrya dvādaśa, tṛtīye indurityekam, caturthe manuścaturdaśa, pañcame'kṣi hau, ṣaṣṭhe madaneti trayodaśa, saptame vasavo'ṣṭau, aṣṭame rudra ekādaśa, navame rājānaḥ ṣoḍaśa, daśame'gnaya iti trayaḥ, ekādaśe digiti daśa, dvādaśe bhūtā iti pañca, trayodaśe randhrā iti nava, caturdaśe ṣaṭkamiti ṣaṭ pañcadaśe tithiriti pañcadaśa, ṣoḍaśe jalanidhaya iti catvāraḥ; sthāpanīyāścara koṣṭhe ṣoḍaśe
eṣāmaṅkānāṃ catuḥ koṣṭhe sthitānāṃ saṃkhyā ekapiṇḍitaṃ jalanidhiśikhina iti catustriṃśat sarvatra
etad yantraṃ likhitvā samastaṃ śrīcakraṃ mānapṛṣṭhe prasavanasamaye darśayed gurvigonāmiti garbhamocananiyamaḥ
[b] idānīṃ garbhādibālatantramucyate yoginya ityādi- yoginyo'ṣṭāṣṭakā yāḥ prakaṭamahitale mātaro yāḥ prasiddhā garbhākhyā vāsarākhyā triguṇanavadaśaikādaśānyā stripañca
māsākhyā vatsarākhyā sakalabhuvitale tāḥ pragṛhṇanti bālaṃ garbhe śūlaṃ ca pīḍāṃ prasavanasamaye'pyeva kurvanti yonau || 147 ||
iha mahītale yāḥ prakaṭāḥ catuḥṣaṣṭiyoginyaḥ, tāstā ( a ) ṣṭāṣṭakā mātaraḥ prasiddhāḥ, tāsāṃ madhye garbhākhyāstriguṇanava iti saptaviṃśatiḥ, vāsarākhyā daśa, ekādaśa māsākhyāḥ, anyāstriyaṃ ( pa ) ñceti pañcadaśa vatsa rākhyāstāstrayaḥṣaṣṭiḥ bālaṃ gṛhṇanti sakalabhuvitale garbhe śūlaṃ ca pīḍāṃ prasavanasamaye'pyeva kurvanti yonāviti
iha garbhākhyānāṃ madhye pañcadaśādhānadinamārabhya pañcadaśadināni yāvad garbhaśūlaṃ prakurvanti, tato navamāsaṃ yāvannava, prasavanakāle ekā, stanakṣārīhāriṇyau dve iti garbhākhyānāṃ niyamaḥ
vimalaprabhāyāṃ jātānāṃ bālatantraṃ bhavati dinavaśānmāsavarṣaprabhedāt [adhyātma- pañca krūrāḥ kumārāḥ prakṛtiguṇavaśāt saṃsthitāḥ parvasandhau
bālā (bāla) gṛhṇanti te vai svatithibhayagataṃ naiva muñcanti rājan teṣāṃ śāntyarthamasmin prabhavati vividhaṃ maṇḍale homakādyam || 148 ||
jātānāṃ bālānāṃ bālatantraṃ bhavati dinavaśānmāsavarṣa prabhedāditi
iha bālatantramiti bālacikitsā mātṛpīḍitānām; tatra vāsarākhyānāṃ bali vakṣyamāṇaṃ dadyāt
jātānāṃ janmadinamārabhya daśauṃ dinaṃ yāvat svasvadine bālānāṃ pīḍāṃ kurvatīnāṃ pratyeka māsavasā (śā) dekādaśatā, ekādaśamāsān [149a] yāvat
tataḥ pañca- daśānāṃ pañcadaśavarṣān yāvat, tadupari bālakumāratvābhāvaḥ, ṣoḍaśame (śe) varṣe śukracyavanā- idānīṃ pañca krūrā ucyante krūretyādi - iha bhuktile nandāditithibhedena pañcatithīnāṃ sandhiṣu kaumārā ākāśādi- prakṛtiguṇavaśāt saṃsthitāḥ parvasandhau
te svatithau bhayagataṃ bālaṃ gṛhṇanti, sarvaṃ sāmānya- balinā naiva muñcanti bālam
rājan ityāmantraṇam
teṣāṃ śāntyarthamasmin prabhavati vividhaṃ maṇḍale homakādya vakṣyamāṇamiti krūraniyamaḥ
idānīṃ garbhākhyābhiḥ pīḍitānāṃ gurviṇonāṃ bhaiṣajyamucyate kuṣṭhetyādi- kuṣṭhośīra kaseru tagarakuvalayaṃ keśaraṃ paṅkajasya piṣṭvā śītāmbunā mantritamapi kuliśairgarbhaśleṣu deyam
garbhastambhe'ṣṭalomāni la (na) kulaśikhinaḥ pīṣayitvā pradeyaṃ dugdhājyaṃ pāyasānnaṃ dadhiguḍasahitaṃ dīyate vāsarīṇām || 149 ||
iha yadā gurviṇīnāṃ garbhaśūlaṃ bhavati, tadā bhaiṣajyam - kuṣṭham, uśīram, kaserum, tagaramūlam utpalakandam, padmakeśaram; etāni dravyāṇi śītāmbunā paryuṣitena piṣṭvā abhimantritamapi kuliśairiti 'oṃ āḥ hū~ amukāyā garbhaśūlaṃ hara hara svāhā' iti mantraḥ, anenābhimantrya garbhaśūleṣu deyamiti niyamaḥ
evaṃ garbhastambhe aṣṭaromāṇi nakulasya, śikhino mayūrasya picchaṃ gṛhītvā śītāmbunā piṣṭvā pūrvavaddeyānyabhimantrya
kṣīrāpahāriṇyāḥ kṣīravṛkṣatale snāpayet
saptamalli kairgokṣīrapūrṇaiḥ kṣīrabhaktena bali dadyā- diti garbhākhyānāṃ niyamaḥ
paṭale ] rasāyanadibālatantramahoddeśaḥ idānīṃ vāsarīṇāṃ vidhirucyate dugdhetyādinā-
iha daśadinābhyantare gṛhītasya bālakasya śāntyarthaṃ dugdham, ājyam, pāyasānnaṃ dadhiguḍasahitaṃ polikā' modakāṃśca gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ pradīpaṃ balau dīyate vāsarīṇām, snānaṃ dhūpaṃ vakṣyamāṇamiti vāsarīṇāṃ trirātrabaliniyamaḥ
[149b] idānīṃ māsākhyānāṃ vidhirucyate pakṣe ( pakve ) tyādinā - pakvānnaṃ pañcabhinnaṃ dadhiguḍasahitaṃ polikāmodakāṃśca gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ pradīpaṃ snapanamapi dalaiḥ pañcarātraṃ prakuryāt
godantaṃ meṣaśṛṅgaṃ mṛganakhacikuraṃ sarpanirmokadhūpaṃ bālānāṃ māsajānāṃ kathitamapi bali puṣṭihetoḥ samastam || 150 ||
iha daśadinādūrdhvaṃ māsaḥ, tata ekādaśamāsān yāvat māsajātakānāṃ śāntyarthaṃ bali mātṛṇāṃ dadyāt, pakvānnaṃ pañcabhitramiti ghṛtena paktraṃ pūrikā ghṛtapūram; saumālī sevālavaṭakāniti pañcabhinnam aparamodanaṃ dadhiguḍasahitaṃ polikāmodakāṃśca
gandhamiti candanam, sugandhapuṣpaṃ tilatailena pradīpaṃ ghṛtena vā
snapanamapi daleriti pañcakṣīravṛkṣāṇāmaśvatthādīnāṃ patraiḥ kiñcit kvathitodakena soṣṇena bāla snāpayet, pañcarātraṃ yāvat samastaṃ kuryāt
snānāvasāne bālasya dhūpaṃ dadyāt, godantam, meṣa- śṛṅgam, mānuṣyanakham, mṛgaromam, cikuram, sarpanirmocam (cakam )

yaḥ karoti sa kartā, yat kriyate tat karma ; tasya kṛtasya karmaṇaḥ phalabhoktā karmakartā
na ca karmaṇā vinā kartā siddhayati ; yathā kumbhaṃ karotīti kumbhakāraḥ
evaṃ yaḥ karma karoti sa karteti nyāyaḥ
āha- nāpīśaḥ karmakartā, svatantraḥ prayojaka iti
iha yadi karmakartā na bhavati, tadā sarvakartā samantāditi nirarthakam

yasya parādhīnatā tasya prayojakaḥ kathaṃ viruddhakarmaṇi kṛte sati nigrahaṃ na karoti; svatantratayā vinā, svatantratā īśvareṇa vyāptā

āha-iha kāraṇena vinā kāryaṃ na bhavati yasmāt tasmāt kāraṇamastīti, ata īśvarādikaṃ siddhamiti
āha - iha kāraṇe kāryaṃ yad bhavati, tat kiṃ satkāryam, asad vā ?
kāraṇe satkāryaṃ na bhavati, vidyamānasya ghaṭasya mṛdādayaḥ kāraṇabhūtā na bhavanti, sattvāt asatkāryaṃ na asattvāt, kūrmaromavat, tathā paṭasya [ta]ntu [tu]rīvemādayaḥ kāraṇabhūtā na bhavanti
ubhayātmakaṃ kāryaṃ na bhavati, parasya (paraspara) virodhāt
yat sat tadasanna bhavati, yadasat tat sanna bhavati, virodhāt
ato na satkāryam, nāsatkāryam, na sadasatkāryaṃ ' kāraṇe bhavatīti siddham
āha-iha kāraṇasya pratiṣedhena kāryasyāpi pratiṣedho bhavati; ubhayapratiṣedhāt sarvābhāva iti siddham
āha - iha sarvābhāvo na parāpekṣikatvāditi
iha kāraṇe yat kāraṇatvaṃ tat kāryamapekṣya parikalpyate, kāryaṃ ca kāraṇamapekṣya evaṃ parāpekṣikatvādubhayorapi kāraṇatva- prasaṅgaḥ
ubhayasya kāraṇatvāt kāryābhāvaḥ, tadabhāve kāraṇābhāvaḥ kāraṇasya kāryāpekṣikatvād aniyatatvaprasaṅgaḥ
tasmād aniyatatvād akāraṇatvaprasaṅgaḥ

cittaṃ vai bhāvarāgaiḥ sphaṭikavadupadhād rāgatāṃ yāti yasmāt tasmād dharmo na kaścit svaparakulagato yoginā dūṣaṇīyaḥ || 177||
svaśaktiriti siddham |
kālacakratantrasya taṭṭīkāyā vimalaprabhāyāḥ kimabhiprāyakamidaṃ vacanadvayam, yathā 'yaḥ saṃvṛtyā vivṛtyā vā sambuddhavacanasamaḥ, sa na dūṣaṇīya iti kālacakra ādibuddha bhagavato niyamaḥ' iti |
' tasmād dharmo na kaścit svaparakulagato yoginā dūṣaṇīyaḥ' vacanābhyāmetābhyām āpātataḥ paramataṃ naiva kālacakriṇā dūṣaṇīyamityābhāti |
evaṃ sati prarvatamāne'smin svaparavicāranyāyamahoddeśe kathamiha paramatakhaṇḍanaṃ kṛtaṃ mūle ṭīkāyāṃ ca kathaṃ coktam etanmahoddeśāvasāne ṭīkāyām — 'madhyamakena nirākaraṇīyastīthikānāṃ siddhāntaḥ' iti |
āpātataḥ pratīyamānasyaitadvirodhasya nirākaraṇaṃ bhavati khes ḍraba je mahābhāgānāṃ svābhiprāyāviṣkaraṇena |
tairuktam- svapara darśananyāyavicāramahoddeśaḥ dharmaḥ sattvopakāro viṣayavirahitaścāpakā ro'pyadharmaḥ hiṃsā vedapramāṇā na hi sukhaphaladā duḥkhadā sarvakālam |
sanmaitro mūrkhavākyāt paramasukhakarā sarvasattvānuraktā tasmāt sattvārthamekaṃ kuru nṛpa manasā bhāvanāṃ niḥsvabhāva (T) m ||178 ||
indro'haṃ svargaloke tridaśana ragururbhūtale cakravartī pā tāle nāgarājaḥ phaṇikulanamitaḥ sarvagaścottamo'ham |
jñānaṃ buddho munīndro'kṣaraparamavibhuryogināṃ vajrayogo vedo'ṅkāraḥ pavitro vraja mama śaraṇaṃ sarvabhāvena rājan || 179 ||
iti śaraṇaniyamaḥ |
- idānīṃ* sūryarathasya namaskāraḥ- tvaṃ mātā tvaṃ pitā tvaṃ jagati gururapi tvaṃ ca bandhuḥ sumitraṃ tvaṃ nāthastvaṃ vidhātā hita ( hi tva ) maghaharaṇa tvaṃ padaṃ sampadāṃ ca |
tvaṃ kaivalyaṃ padaṃ tvaṃ varaguṇanilayo dhvastadoṣastvameva tvaṃ donānātha cintāmaṇirapi śaraṇaṃ tvāṃ gato'haṃ jinendra * ||180||
iti śrīmadādibuddhoddhṛte śrīmahākālacakre' adhyātmanirṇayo dvitīyapaṭalaḥ ||2||
iti sūryaratho gurunamaskāreṇa mañjuśriyaṃ bhagavantaṃ stutvā pādadvayaṃ śirasi kṛtvā punaḥ svakīyāsane niṣannaḥ (ṇṇaḥ ) |
iti śrīmūlatantrānusāriṇyāṃ' laghukālacakratantrarājaṭīkāyāṃ dvādaśasāhasrikāyāṃ vimalaprabhāyāṃ svaparadarśana nyāyavicāramahoddeśaḥ saptamaḥ ||7||
samāpteyaṃ ṭokā adhyātmapaṭalasyeti ||2||
pramāṇaśāstreṣu mādhyamikaśāstreṣu vā nirdiṣṭaivistṛtadūṣaṇaprakāraistairthikāḥ dūṣaṇīyāḥ |
taithiketare ye saṃvṛti vā paramārthaṃ vā aṅgīkṛtya buddhavacanānusāreṇaiva svapakṣaṃ sthāpayanti, te naiva dūṣaṇīyāḥ” |
da hāda śrīmattantre prathamapaṭale bhājanībhūtalokaḥ spaṣṭoddiṣṭa [:] prabhavalayasaṃsthānamānādibhedāḥ (daḥ) |
taistaistulyā svatanuratulā spaṣṭadṛṣṭārthatattve- jñeyā kaiścid yadiha paṭale mudrayā mudriteva ||
adhyātmanirṇayakaraṃ paṭalaṃ vile (li) khya śrīāna kuśalaṃ yadihāptamuccam |
tenāstu sekasukhanirṇayakalpa rāja- bodhipratiṣṭhitamatiḥ sakalo'pi lokaḥ * ||
||
||
śubham ||
||
[adhyātma pravartamānaviṣayasandarbhād bahirgata iva pratīyate, athāpi dvitīyapaṭalasya antimaślokatvena bhoṭabhāṣayā; uktaṃ hi tai:- Pas”.
uparilikhitabhoṭāṃśasya saṃskṛtānuvāda:- kārikāḥ, adhyātmapaṭalasya antimā kārikā ca, imā ekādaśakārikā yaśasaḥ sūryarathasya vā vacanāni santi, na tu tā mūlatantrād uddhṛtāḥ |
* dvitīyapaṭalasya puṣpikānantaraṃ 'śrīmattantre prathamapaṭale' ityārabhya 'bodhipratiṣṭhitamatiḥ pustake eva upalabhyate |
manye, lipikāreṇa svaracitaṃ śraddhayā'tra niveśitamiti |
